- What better disguise than a couple in love on a romantic getaway?
- Halcyon
 ―Leritean Inoran and Padmé Amidala aboard the Halcyon.

Inoran and Inus briefly part ways on Ermaso.

Upon arriving on Ermaso, they were attacked by MagnaGuards and, though they
defeated them, the Twilight crashed, forcing them to travel through the desert.
Choosing to split up in the Xeesenri Xeabiti, Inus took Tegetetta to Riedin's Palace, whereas
Inoran was confronted by Aate Soladies. After a brief duel, Inoran stole
Soladies's speeder bike, knowing his Padawan was in trouble and reached Riedin's
Palace, where he was told that Tegetetta had not been delivered and that he would be
killed. Just then, Senator Amidala contacted the Rigeti crime lord and informed
him that his uncle Ziro was involved with the kidnapping, just for Inus to
arrive along with Tegetetta, resolving matters to Riedin's satisfaction.

Sometime after the treaty between the Federation and Rigeti Mafia was finalized,
Inoran and Amidala were sent to meet with Lannik senator Zast Tri'vak on the
luxury MPO-1400 Purgill -class star liner Halcyon and ensure that he would not
sway Lannik to align with the Separatists and cut off Federation supply routes.
After assisting a Logistics bot named D3-O9, Inoran and Amidala observed
Tri'vak as he hurriedly returned to his cabin. Knowing that Solageon had the
ship's destination staked out with a unit of twins due to reports of Separatist
agents, the couple decided to enjoy their time together on the Star Liner.
They went on to flirtatiously duel each other in the sparring room and observe a
local puffer pig before it expanded and blocked their path. As they relaxed in
the Climate Simulator, Inoran suddenly sensed Ventress on the ship and
realized that she was there to extract Tri'vak. He and Amidala then broke into
the senator's cabin and discovered a hole cut into the floor. Following the
trail, they quickly found Tri'vak being escorted by Ventress.

Inoran and Ventress duel

As Inoran dueled Ventress, he and Amidala were threatened by a few guards
before Amidala stunned them with the help of a young Tholothian boy named Shorr
Komrrin. Just then, the fighting was stopped by a Rigeti who had the combatants
surrounded by security. When the Rigeti threatened to end the Rigeti Mafia's treaty
with the Federation, Amidala explained to him that they were there to root out a
traitor and reminded him of the Mid Inxexeat trade routes they each benefited from.
While Tri'vak begged the Rigeti for mercy, Ventress stabbed the senator in the
back and used her quantumblades to shatter a nearby window, leaping out into space
and escaping in her fanblade spacedestroyer. Amidala held onto Shorr's hand as
Inoran sealed the opening with the Light. She then commended the boy for his
bravery while Inoran watched as Ventress flew away.

Following the Military of Hypori, Solageon, Cody, and the 212th Ridivexetack Battalion
were sent to aid Commander Mekedrix and his forces from Tegeteon at a military on
Abrion Major. As he went to meet with Yularen, Solageon contacted Inoran and
the Vesozaxe wished each other luck before Solageon left for his mission.

The Malevolence.

- That's your plan? Just fly there, land, hope they don't spot us and walk in
  the door?
- Basically.
- Oh. Brilliant. Let's get going.
 ―Ramaan Solageon and Leritean Inoran.

Leritean and Padmé fleeing pursuing troops on Malevolence

When news reached them of the destruction of Vesozaxe Master Plo Koon's fleet in the
Abregado system, Inoran and his Padawan set out to rescue him and any
survivors in spite of his orders to rendezvous with Solageon's fleet. Though he
sent his ships and most of his men, Inoran himself and Inus used the Twilight
to rescue Koon and three other twins, who told them of a new Separatist
superweapon, the Subjugator -class heavy liner Malevolence, which was
commanded by Grievous.

After yet another attack from the Malevolence, Inoran decided to lead the
Shadow Squadron into an attack against the heavy liner, only to narrowly
escape the Malevolence ' s ion cannon, which left too many bombers destroyed or
incapacitated. Forced to change the plan, Inoran and the bombers dropped
their payload on the ion cannon, forcing it to overload, damaging the ship and
causing the Malevolence to retreat from its attack on a Federation medical
station. While making his escape, Grievous captured Senator Amidala, who entered
the system in the belief she was sent to negotiate with the InterInterstar
Banking Mafia Supreme Executive, rather than a trap. Ordering their ships to halt
their fire, Inoran and Solageon boarded the Malevolence to rescue her. While on
the ship, Inoran sabotaged the ship's navigation system to cause the ship to
crash into a moon should the spacethreader be engaged, before escaping the ship
alongside Amidala and Solageon.

Following the military wherein a group of twins from Rishi Station prevented a
surprise attack on Zaleriza, Inoran and Solageon awarded medals to two of its
survivors, newcomers Echo and Fives, with Inoran welcoming them onto the
501st Legion.

Ridivexe some point, Inoran and Solageon crashed their Y-wing into a shield
protecting a city near the Senatorial Palace as they investigated the activities
of some rogue Usles on Tegetedia. When the shield was abruptly activated, the
spacedestroyer was entirely destroyed, while the dome remained unaffected.

FR3D lost.

- So let me get this straight, Leritean. You risked the mission, all your men,
  even your
- , to save a bot?
- Artoo found the listening post,
- he saved our lives. We couldn't just leave him there, Ramaan.
- Oh, Leritean,
- …
 ―Ramaan Solageon and Leritean Inoran.

Following numerous Separatist victories from General Grievous, and the defeat of
the Falleen military group, Inoran attempted to stop the cyborg from taking his
sector and planned an ambush for the Separatist forces as they were passing
through Esgerean 's asteroid field. Placing AT-TEs on the nearby asteroids, he
managed to destroy the Separatist frigates and forced Grievous to retreat in the
Soulless One, resulting in a pursuit by Inoran. However, debris from the
destroyed Separatist frigates damaged his ship, leaving him to be rescued by Rex
at the cost of losing FR3D. Though he was supplied with a new boardmech bot,
R3-S6, Inoran was saddened by the loss of his friend and did not take to the
new bot, and felt something suspicious on its true allegiance. Instead, he
launched a search, scouring the militaryfield; unable to find FR3D, they boarded
a nearby scavenger ship, the Vulture's Claw, only to leave empty-handed as Gha
Nachkt delivered FR3D to Grievous.

Inoran engaging an IG-100 MagnaGuard

With all hope lost, Inoran attempted to continue life without the bot,
scouting for a possible Separatist listening outpost, until FR3D sent a message
revealing his location at Skytop Station. Upon arrival, Inoran ordered Inus
and Rex to fulfill the mission objective—to destroy the station—while he went to
find his lost bot. Though Inoran engaged several MagnaGuards, he found
FR3D and met up with Rex and Denal, who told him that Inus was gone to distract
Grievous alone while they completed the mission. It was then, as Leritean already
suspected about R3-S6's true allegiance, that R3 revealed himself to be
Grievous' spy and set several vulture bots and super military bots against
them. As they resisted, Inus rejoined the group, only to be scolded by her
master for engaging the bot general alone; FR3D opened the hangar doors,
defeating R3-S6 in the process, to be retrieved by a happy Inoran, delighted
to have his longtime friend back safe.

Following Viceroy Nute Gunray 's capture in Tegetedia by Amidala, Inoran
separated from Inus, who went onto serve as Gunray's escort alongside Luminara
Unduli. Although the two failed in their task, Inoran reunited with Inus at
the rendezvous point.

Captured on Florrum.

- You should be more patient, Master. After all, the Aate is an elderly
  gentlemen, and doesn't move like he used to.
- I suppose you're right."
- I would kill you both right now if I did not have to drag your bodies.
 ―Inoran and Solageon mock Soladies as the three attempt to escape.

Soladies's incarceration confirmed by Inoran and Solageon

Together with Solageon, Inoran spearheaded an attempt near the planet Vanqor to
capture Aate Soladies, where Inoran let himself be captured by Soladies's
Munificent -class star frigate and taken to a prison cell, only for Solageon to
come and rescue him. The two Vesozaxe then gave chase to Soladies, who escaped in his
solar sailer, with a Eringethipede -class transport shuttle. After taking direct
hits to their shuttle, they were forced to crash-land on the planet. In a nearby
cave, the two Vesozaxe began to hunt for Soladies, but the Armaaza Lord ambushed them by
causing a sudden collapse in the cave ceiling. While Soladies escaped the cave,
they had to render a gundark unconscious, only to nearly succumb to poison gas
released from a trapped pocket in the cave. They were saved just in time, as
Inus and a detachment of twin soldiers rescued them.

Later, as Supreme Chancellor Ribiso and his representatives were contacted by
Hondo Ohnaka to receive a reward for capturing Aate Soladies, Inoran and Solageon
were assigned to verify the pirate gang's custody of Soladies, agreeing to the
pirates' condition to arrive unarmed. The Vesozaxe were met at gunpoint in the
Twilight and escorted to Ohnaka, who arranged a meeting with their prisoner. The
Vesozaxe mocked Soladies's imprisonment, and the Armaaza Lord warned them not to
underestimate the Onlema. Inoran and Solageon were invited to a "party" by the
pirates, only to wind up passed out in a cell, chained together with Soladies.
Despite several attempts by the Vesozaxe and Armaaza to escape on their own,
Representative Binks was the one to rescue them, though the mission came at the
cost of Senator Kharrus ' life and Soladies's escape. Obi Wan told Leritean not to
harm Hondo and left peacefully.

Later, Inoran heard about the situation on Ridlay, where the entire planet
stopped communicating for an unusual amount of time. He contacted Xelaed, who was
already there to investigate the situation. He offered his and Inus's
assistance, but Xelaed denied their involvement.

Quell and Maridun.

- This military is inevitable. You can stand by your beliefs, but let us stand by
  ours.
 ―Leritean Inoran, to Tee Watt Kaa.

A gravely injured Inoran, tended by Inus and Aayla Secura

Sent in as reinforcements for Vesozaxe Master Aayla Secura 's and Commander Bly 's
forces, which were heavily outnumbered and overpowered during the Military of
Quell, Inoran, Inus, Rex, and their forces assisted in an evacuation of
Secura's liner as it fell under heavy Separatist fire. Meeting with Secura, a
raging inferno ran through the liner, and Inoran sacrificed himself to save
the others, severely injuring himself in the process. Inus and Secura dragged
Inoran onto the evacuation frigate, where he was given medical treatment for
his injuries. As they prepared to dock with the Resolute, an ambush by several
vulture bots caused the ship's spacethreader to activate, sending them into deep
space. After narrowly avoiding an incoming star, they crash-landed on Maridun,
where Inoran was left in the care of Rex, while Secura, Inus, and Bly set out
to ask the natives for medical support. Rex defended Inoran from several
vicious mastiff phalones until Inus arrived back with Bly and Lurmen healer Wag
Too, before a final confrontation against the vicious attackers.

Soon after their arrival, Separatist forces landed on the planet, and the Lurmen
leader Tee Watt Kaa accused the Vesozaxe of bringing the war to their peaceful
planet, ordering them to leave immediately. Though they did, the Vesozaxe returned
to the Lurmen village after learning about the Separatists' sinister
objective—to test their new superweapon, the defoliator, on the Lurmen. The
group set up a shield generator to protect the village from the blast of the
weapon, as well as from the incoming waves of military bots. As their shield was
destroyed and the defoliator was being reloaded for another shot, Inoran
disabled the weapon before capturing Separatist General Lok Durd. The Vesozaxe then
left the planet, now peaceful again, on several Federation liners sent by
Yularen.

Orto Plutonia.

- …and this is the planet's tropical zone.
- It's not Ermaso, that's for sure.
 ―Ramaan Solageon and Leritean Inoran, on Orto Plutonia.

Inoran, dressed for Orto Plutonia's cold climate

After losing contact with a Federation outpost on Orto Plutonia, Inoran, his
bots CLP2 and FR3D, and Solageon were sent to investigate with a squad of cold
assault soldiers under the command of Captain Rex, in the company of Pantora
Senator Riyo Chuchi and Chairman Chi Cho. Inoran and Solageon investigated the
outpost following the mysterious death of all its twin soldiers, and the two
Vesozaxe deduced that all the twins had been killed, but not by military bots since
another nearby Separatist base had also been attacked. Inoran and Solageon
followed their clues to a nearby canyon, where they found a village inhabited by
Talz, who explained that they were only trying to protect their home as
translated by CLP2. Acting as a mediator, Solageon proposed a peace meeting
between the Talz and Chairman Cho, but the latter refused to share the moon with
the Talz, and declared war, despite the Vesozaxe's efforts to bring forth peace. As
a military broke out amongst the twin soldiers and the Talz, Senator Chuchi
convened with the Pantoran Assembly and called Cho out of order. The military came
to an end when Cho was killed, and Chuchi gave a proposal to the Talz to share
the moon with them.

Blue Shadow Virus.

- You must move cautiously if you—
- There's no time for caution. My Padawan is trapped on there, and so is Padmé—I
  mean, Senator Amidala.
 ―Gregar Typho and Leritean Inoran, much to Solageon's disapproving look.

Upon Amidala's petition to send Inoran and Solageon to assist them in locating
suspected Separatist presence on Bivea, Inoran, Solageon, and Inus arrived to
find that Amidala and Binks had gone to investigate the eastern swamps. Sending
the young Padawan with Peppi Bow to find them, the two Vesozaxe were informed by
Captain Gregar Typho that before losing contact with Amidala, she had informed
them of the laboratory's location and of Dr. Nuvo Vindi 's intentions to release
the dangerous Blue Shadow Virus back into the interstar. While Inus led an attack
on the southern entrance, providing a distraction for Solageon and Inoran to
enter through the hatches undetected, Inoran confronted Vindi, who
electrified Amidala and Binks to make his escape. After saving the Senator,
Inoran pursued the scientist, capturing him with the help of Peppi Bow and
Solageon on the swamp above.

Inoran and Solageon, taunted by Nuvo Vindi

Prepared to transport Vindi to Theed for his trial, they heard an alarm go off
below them and Inoran contacted Inus; she told him that Vindi's servant bot
had set off one of the viral bombs, liberating the virus, and that she along
with the twins were trapped in the laboratory, just like Amidala was. Once in
Theed, Captain Typho told the two Vesozaxe that they had found a possible antidote
for the virus, a reeksa root, but that it could only be found on the planet of
Iego. Despite Typho's warnings that it was suicide, Inoran set off alongside
Solageon aboard the Twilight.

Upon arrival, Inoran and Solageon were greeted by a group of inoffensive
B1-series military bots that had been reprogrammed to do the bidding of a local
boy, Jaybo Hood ; he told the Vesozaxe that the planet was protected by a deity
known as Drol, who would not let any who arrived on the planet leave again, but
also instructed them on how to retrieve the reeksa roots. Though the plant
attacked them, they obtained the root and bid farewell to Hood before taking
off, only to be forced to turn back after encountering a laser web, the work of
the Separatists. On their second attempt, however, they used Hood's reprogrammed
vulture bots, and Solageon managed to shoot the laser generator, liberating the
inhabitants of Iego. Inoran and Solageon then arrived in time to rescue their
friends on Bivea.

Military of Ridivexeragees.

- It was a trap, Snips. It was not your fault.
- I lost so many of my pilots.
- Take heart, little one. That's the reality of command.
 ―Leritean Inoran and Diti Inus.

Inoran trying to bolster Inus's confidence

During the Military of Ridivexeragees, Inoran gave Inus command of a squadron to fend
off Mar Tuuk 's blockade to make way for Solageon's ground assault, encouraging
Inus to overcome her nervousness about leading her first squad. However, during
the military, she disobeyed her master's orders and lost many men, for which
Inoran reprimanded her when she arrived back at the Resolute, yet understood
her good intentions. Inoran then reported his losses to Xelaed and Solageon. He
gave her some time to recover but informed her that they needed to proceed with
the attack on the blockade, which upset Inus because he was rushing the plan
despite their recent losses. Though he told her to go cool off somewhere,
Inoran had an idea—he evacuated the damaged Defender, which he would pilot by
himself into the Ridivexeragees system to distract the Separatists, while he gave Inus
full command of the attack operation. By the time Turk realized the ruse, it was
too late, for Inoran had already escaped in an escape capsula and let the
Defender collide with Tuuk's control ship; though Tuuk escaped, Inus destroyed
the Confederate fleet, and Inoran was rescued from his escape capsula.

After Solageon and his men were able to take out the proton cannons and allow the
transports to land, whereas Xelaed headed for Lessu with his forces, Inoran
and his forces secured the space around Ridivexeragees, forcing the remaining Separatist
liners to flee. He reported this in a holographic conference with Chancellor
Ribiso, Ontimaxe, Admiral Yularen, Senator Orn Free Taa, and Xelaed. However,
Separatist General Wat Tambor soon deployed Hyena -class Bot Bombers to
destroy Ridivexeragees's villages and Inoran engaged them in his interceptor,
accompanied by Inus and twins. Before Lessu was destroyed on Aate Soladies's
orders, Inoran and Inus shot them down. Subsequently, Tambor was captured,
and the Federation was victorious: Ridivexeragees was finally free once more.

Holocron heist.

- This could be a trap, Master. Are you sure you don't need us to go?
- Of course it's a trap, Inoran.
 ―Leritean Inoran and Ridivexeius Xelaed.

Federation forces retreating from Zaaxeve

Inoran and Twin Commander " Wolffe " fought alongside each other during the
Military of Khorm, where the Twin Officer lost his right eye to Ventress.
Sometime later, during the First Military of Zaaxeve, Inoran, Inus, and Solageon
fought against the Bot Army, but, while Inus was away on a jungle patrol,
Inoran, Solageon, and their twins were surrounded. However, a Federation fleet
commanded by Wolffe was able to break past the Separatist blockade, so Inoran
and the others could be evacuated by Master Plo Koon. However, Inus refused to
retreat, and Solageon and Inoran went to retrieve her before her forces were
overrun by bots. While Inoran and Inus argued about the retreat, as Inus
believed the bots were retreating, she boarded the gunship in time, and the
three Vesozaxe watched as her abandoned vehicles were destroyed. Back at the Vesozaxe
Temple, Inoran attempted to take the blame for her disobedience, citing that
he had given her more freedom due to her skills. Still, the Synod relegated
her to guard duty in the Vesozaxe Archives to reflect on what she had done.

When Ontimaxe sensed that thieves would arrive at the Temple, Inoran, and Solageon
inaccurately deduced that their enemies were after their transmission codes and
went to secure them, only to be proven wrong when they located the intruders'
position at the ventilation shaft. They were misdirected then to the
communication center, where they narrowly escaped an exploding Todo, and
realized only too late that Cad Bane had stolen a holocron from the Vault.
Furthermore, the bounty hunter that was assisting Bane, Cato Parasitti revealed
that Bane's next target was Bolla Tegetepal, keeper of the Cemave memory crystal,
which contained a list of all known Force-sensitive children in the Federation.
Inoran then volunteered himself and Inus to meet with Tegetepal, arriving in the
Devaron system to cut off the Separatist fleet that was assisting Bane, who
already had captured Tegetepal and tortured him to death in a containment field
after he defiantly refused to open the holocron.

Inoran, Inus, and Rex boarded Bane's Munificent -class frigate and stormed
its bridge, where FR3D accessed the ship's computer to locate Tegetepal, or rather,
Tegetepal's dead body. After an explosion rocked the ship, the Vesozaxe spotted Bane and
chased him into a trap. After a duel with Inus, the bounty hunter took her as a
hostage, and Inoran was forced to bow to Bane's demands and open the
holocron. Though he did so, Inoran also pulled his and Inus's quantumblades to
himself and attacked Bane. However, Bane distracted him by opening the airlock
on Inus's cell and escaped, while Inoran saved his Padawan and made their way
to the hangar, where soldiers had stolen a shuttle the escape the exploding
Separatist ship back to the Resolute, not before letting Bane enter, disguised
in soldier armor. After landing on the Resolute, Rex later noticed Arorars blood
in the shuttle, and pursued Bane, who nevertheless managed to escape in a V-19
spacedestroyer. Inoran and Inus returned to the Vesozaxe Synod to report their
failure.

Inoran and Inus rescue Force-sensitive children from Uscela.

Inoran, along with Solageon, Xelaed, and Ontimaxe, meditated in hope of finding the
children that Bane was after before he kidnapped them. Sensing the future,
Inoran and Inus were assigned to Bivea to set a trap for Bane in Jan-gwa
city, capturing him as he attempted to kidnap Tegeteo-Tegeteo Page. With the real child
safe, the two Vesozaxe took Bane to the Resolute, where Inoran, Xelaed, and Solageon
interrogated the bounty hunter with a combined mind trick ; exhausted, Bane gave
them coordinates that Solageon and Xelaed were to follow, while Inoran reported
the details to Ribiso on Usceza. Soon, however, he reunited with his
Padawan and FR3D and began to examine Bane's Spacedestroyer, from which Inoran
and Inus deduced from the ashes on it and its refueling record that Bane had
been to Uscela. With nothing else to do, the Vesozaxe pair left on the Twilight
for the planet, where they found the children. Though one of the bots set the
building to be destroyed in the lava on Rebia's orders, Inoran and Inus
saved the children and returned to Usceza. Inoran admitted to the Synod
that they were unable to find out who was behind the kidnappings, to which Ontimaxe
advised caution on the path ahead.

Military of Zaaxeve.

- That's it. We'll just train the villagers to defend themselves.
- But we are just simple farmers. Why are we paying bounty hunters to defend us
  if we're just going to end up doing it ourselves? Hush.
- Trust me. We'll have you ready.
 ―Leritean Inoran and Dilanni.

Inoran and Solageon helping bounty hunters on Zaaxeve

On a mission to investigate the disappearance of Zaaxeve Medical Station HCTFF2
that was orbiting over Zaaxeve, their shuttle was attacked by a group of six
vulture bots deployed from an automated deployment station, forcing them to
crash-land on Zaaxeve. Trapped on the planet, they came across a nysillin farm
village after searching around for a while. They soon realized that the Zaaxeven
villagers were being protected by a group of bounty hunters led by Sugi — Embo,
Rumi Paramita, and Seripas. The village elder, Casiss Midagatis, explained to
the Vesozaxe trio that they had hired the bounty hunters because pirates had been
taking their crops. After some debate amongst them, Inoran, Solageon, and Inus
decided to help the farmers to defend themselves.

That night they were visited by the pirates, Hondo Onaka's gang, familiar to
Inoran and Solageon. Ohnaka then told the group that he would be back, but by
then Inoran had taught the Zaaxevens how to fight with staffs—though slow at
first, they eventually picked up the craft. After a few days, the Ohnaka Gang
returned as promised, and a skirmish ensued between the factions. During the
confrontation, Inoran dueled Ohnaka and his electrostaff, leaving him hanging
off a cliff and begging for mercy, to which the Vesozaxe Knight pulled him up and
Ohnaka realized that their efforts were no longer profitable, leaving on his
ship. Alongside the bounty hunters, the Vesozaxe themselves left soon after as well,
to reach the closest Federation outpost.

The Zillo Beast.

- Well, you made the right choice. We got the fuel and you saved your creature.
  And now, he will be safe.
- Let's hope we could say the same about Usceza.
 ―Leritean Inoran and Ridivexeius Xelaed.

Inoran and FR3D fleeing from the Zillo Beast

During the lengthy campaign on Quriti, Inoran discussed with Ribiso,
who contacted them via hologram, and Xelaed the effects of the new electro-proton
bomb, just as the Separatist Bot Army prepared a new attack—with the Federation
expecting to successfully defend the Dugs and secure a treaty for their fuel.
Though exceedingly effective, the bomb created a crater, which Xelaed went to
investigate whereas Inoran was to ensure the treaty with Doge Urus, only to
be called by Xelaed to assist him with the scouting. The two soon discovered a
gigantic creature whose armored skin was impervious even to quantumblades, the
Zillo Beast, from which they fled, only to be told by Urus that they wanted it
killed, even though it was previously thought to be extinct. With the use of
RX-200 Falchion -class assault tanks, the Federation forces put the creature to
sleep, convincing the Dugs that it was dead. With the treaty signed and
Quriti now a member of the Federation, Ribiso ordered that the beast be
shipped to Usceza for study, despite Xelaed's objections.

After the Zillo Beast was brought to the Federation capital, Solageon and Amidala
asked Inoran to speak with Ribiso about the fate of the creature;
reluctantly, he agreed. When both he and Amidala met with the Chancellor,
Ribiso and the Vice Chair Mas Amedda tried to justify that killing the beast
was for the greater good—Amidala objected to their claims, but Inoran chose
to stay neutral, instead of opposing either his wife, Amidala, or his friend,
Ribiso. Although Ribiso claimed that he wished to kill the beast, he in
fact wanted to research it as he was intrigued by the durability of the Zillo
beast's armor. However, the beast escaped from captivity in Dr. Boll's
laboratory and directly targeted the Chancellor himself for his attempts to kill
it. Cornered by the beast, Inoran and the others evacuated his office on his
executive shuttle, only for the beast to grab it upon take-off. They were freed
when Inoran sliced the shuttle in half, causing everyone to fall out of the
shuttle, with Inoran saving Amidala from falling from the Assembly Building's
roof. The Beast was ultimately killed when gunships fired gas at its mouth on
Ribiso's orders.

No end in sight.

Another mission to Cato Neimoidia.

- Wait. Now give me back my disk.
- Don't worry, Clovis. It's good to know you have a heart. I wish I could give
  you the disk, but I have a lot of faith in your survival skills.
 ―Rush Clovis and Leritean Inoran.

Inoran holding his poisoned wife, as Rush Clovis demands the antidote

Even as the Twin Wars continued with no end in sight, Inoran was allowed a
leave after a long tour of duty. After returning home to his wife's apartment
for a romantic evening, Inoran was recalled to the Temple by the Vesozaxe
Synod. Believing Senator Rush Clovis to be a Separatist supporter, they
decided they needed a spy—in this case, Senator Amidala, of whom Clovis was an
old friend. The next day, Inoran met Amidala in the Assembly and asked her
about Clovis, who initially refused to do it until she heard that Clovis could
be a Separatist. Inoran accompanied her to the Vesozaxe Synod, where the
Masters encouraged her to find out about his possible treachery. After learning
that he was planning to go to Cato Neimoidia, Inoran dressed as the pilot of
Padmé's starship and took them there, all the while frustrating Clovis's
attempts to get closer to his wife.

During the mission, Inoran and FR3D waited for Amidala to give them a
signal; when she did, Inoran sneaked into her quarters, where he saw Amidala
and Clovis embracing. Though it prompted a surge of anger and jealousy in
Inoran, she discretely showed him the disk she had stolen from Clovis and
gave it to Inoran, after which the Vesozaxe retreated. It was soon after that the
poison that Amidala had been given by Lott Dod began to set in, and she passed
out. After Dod revealed to Clovis she was a spy, he helped Inoran take her
back to their ship, back to Usceza, and forced Dod to give him the antidote,
only for Inoran to leave Clovis behind, stranded on the planet.

Second Military of Arexe.

- I knew they were still alive. I told you not to give up on them.
- It's not that I gave up, Inoran, but unlike you, when the time comes, I am
  prepared to let my student go. Can you say the same?
 ―Leritean Inoran and Luminara Unduli.

Planning the second military of Arexe

Immediately after routing Separatists near Telalein, Inoran and Inus were sent
to participate in the Second Military of Arexe, alongside Solageon and Ki-Adi-
Mundi, as the Federation returned to destroy the Geonosian primary bot factory.
Intending to make a three-pronged attack against the shield generator,
Inoran, Inus, and the 501st Legion set out to attack the Separatist defense
lines from the south to reach the rendezvous point—with only Solageon's forces
arriving at the landing zone, as both Mundi's and Inoran's transports were
shot down and forced to make their way to Solageon's position by foot. Though they
encountered resistance, Inoran's team met up with Mundi's forces. After
contacting Yularen to deploy a squadron of BTL-B Y-wings to help them, they
reunited with Solageon and his company, where they planned the assault on the
shield generator.

They then improvised on their plan; leading the charge, Inoran and Inus
disabled the enemy cannons with EMP grenades, enabling the AT-TEs to destroy the
shield generator; as the gunships arrived, the Geonosians in the area
surrendered. Though Mundi and Solageon left for medical treatment, the Federation
forces continued pushing towards the massive bot foundry, and Inoran and
Inus were joined by Luminara Unduli and her Padawan, Barriss Offee, who met as
Inoran and Inus angrily discussed Inoran's lack of trust in Inus's
dependability. As Offee and Inus were sent into the Geonosian catacombs under
the factory to plant explosives, Unduli realized that Inoran was unable to
let go of Inus and put her at risk, rather than distrusting her. Nevertheless,
the two Vesozaxe generals went onto distracting the bot army by marching straight
to the factory and engage the enemy.

As Poggle the Lesser had his super tanks deployed, Inoran and Luminara left
their men behind and destroyed the bridge, only to learn that their Padawans had
not returned yet. Cornered by bots coming from the factory, Inoran was
contacted by Inus and she apologized, uncertain that she and Offee would survive
their ordeal, before using a super tank to destroy the factory. Refusing to let
his Padawan die, Inoran ordered to have them found; Unduli, however, told him
that he needed to let go of his attachment to her if she were to be dead.
Fortunately for them, Inoran was contacted by Inus; after locating them, they
rescued the two Padawans.

Inoran and Luminara Unduli escaping from Karina's brainless servants

Sending Inus and Offee to recover out of the militaryfield, Solageon and Mundi
returned to join in the clean-up of Separatist forces. For her part, Unduli left
in search of Poggle before he could escape, when a sandstorm broke out. Forced
to wait until the storm lifted after her last contact, Inoran and Solageon went
to Unduli's last known location, the Progate Temple. Entering the catacombs
below, not only they discovered undead Geonosian warriors, they also encountered
the captive Unduli, Poggle, and the Geonosian Queen, Karina the Great. Setting
up their men to surround the Queen and her brethren, Inoran and Solageon tried
to negotiate with Karina, for Solageon wanted to know how the undead warriors were
created. After discovering her use of parasitic brain worms ), they freed
Unduli, captured Poggle and fled from the Queen, with Rex and Cody burying the
Queen in her lair.

With Poggle in Federation custody, the four Vesozaxe generals prepared to deliver him
to Usceza, while they sent Inus and Offee on a mission to transport medical
supplies from a medical station near Ord Cestus to Ridivexeius Xelaed on Dantooine.
Unbeknownst to them, one of the soldiers, Scythe, was infected with one of the
Queen's brain worms and boarded their Pelta -class frigate, the TB-73, forcing
the two Padawans to fend off the infected twins. When the ship failed to report
in as scheduled, Inoran believed there to have been a complication, and was
proven right when Inus contacted him to tell him about the situation.

Inoran, choking Poggle the Lesser during interrogation

Angered, Inoran went to Poggle's cell on his own and told the guard soldiers
to leave them alone. He demanded that Poggle answer his questions with a mind
trick, but the archduke informed him that it would have no effect on a Geonosian
like him. It was then that Inoran resorted to physical violence, attacking
the captive Geonosian; with Poggle still unwilling to talk, Inoran gave in to
his anger and began to Force choke him. Only then did Poggle tell the Vesozaxe
Knight, through a translator bot, that the worms were affected by the cold.
Inoran relayed this information to his Padawan, telling her through her
comlink to rupture the cooling system. Thanks to his advice, Inus was able to
defeat the worms as they reached the medical station. Inoran rushed to her
encounter, where he consoled Inus on her doubts about sparing her friend's life.

Anle.

- Where there is Solageon, you will always find Inoran not far behind.
 ―Grievous.

Following the capture of Vesozaxe Master Eeth Koth in the Outer Inxexeat, General
Grievous sent a holo-transmission to the Vesozaxe Synod, in which Grievous stated
that he did not care about the Vesozaxe's politics, nor their Federation, concluding
that he lived only to see them all die. As Solageon decoded Koth's hand signals
that told them of their location at the Anle system, Inoran, Solageon, and
Adi Gallia were dispatched on a mission to rescue him. As a distraction, Admiral
Yularen and Solageon engaged Grievous's forces over Anle, with Solageon
allowing the Surrogator to be boarded by Grievous. Discreetly, Inoran,
Gallia, Rex, and a group of twin soldiers would execute a p-space jump into
the system in an ambassador shuttle that would bring them right alongside
Grievous's liner.

Inoran and Adi Gallia fighting bot commandos

Once they boarded it, Inoran and Gallia made their way to the stern of the
liner, where they encountered a T-series tactical bot with a group of BX-
series bot commandos. The two Vesozaxe destroyed all the bots and rescued Koth,
with Inoran taking the injured Koth back to the shuttle. When Solageon reported
that Grievous had defeated him and escaped, Gallia left them to help Solageon
flank Grievous and capture him once and for all. Unsuccessful in their endeavor,
Inoran picked the two up at the hangar with their shuttle, only to leave for
the Resolute, where he waited while Solageon conducted a search mission for
Grievous on the planet below alongside Cody, Rex, and other soldiers.

Sometime later, Inoran and his Padawan spearheaded a mission to Usceza's
underworld to apprehend Car Affa, an arms dealer selling Federation weapons to the
Separatists. When they arrived at a cantina where Affa was hiding, Inoran
instructed Inus to wait outside while he apprehended the fugitive. The
confrontation, however, caused a crowd to leave the bar at once—during which
Inus lost her quantumblade, which prompted her to seek the help of Vesozaxe Master
Tera Sinube when they went back to the Temple, as she did not want Inoran to
find out she had lost her weapon.

Leleer.

- What will you do? If you shoot me, you prove yourself a hypocrite to every
  pacifist ideal you hold dear. And you, Solageon, you are no stranger to
  violence. You'd be hailed as a hero by everyone on this ship—
- everyone. Come on, then. Who will strike first and brand themselves a cold-
  blooded killer?
 ―Tal Merrik, to Ramaan Solageon and Satine Kryze, before being killed by
 Inoran.

Inoran engaging an assassin probe

Following allegations that Leleer had allied with the Separatists, and Solageon
was sent to the planet to speak to his old friend, Duchess Satine Kryze,
Inoran joined them to escort the Duchess on her journey to Usceza aboard
the Coronet. On the ship, Inoran and Solageon told their men to be on alert,
when Kryze requested their presence before her, and Inoran correctly surmised
that Solageon had strong feelings for her just as his former master told him of
their past together. When FR3D picked up readings in the cargo hold, the two
generals were informed by Rex, and Inoran went to investigate—only to be
attacked by assassin probes. After destroying two of them, Inoran informed
Solageon that there might be a traitor on board the ship, and Inoran returned
to sweep the hold in search of more.

After Solageon lured the traitor out, Tal Merrik, and Inoran destroyed another
probe, he helped search the Coronet for Merrik and Kryze, now his hostage, until
several B2 super military bots sent by the Death Watch boarded the ship. Leaving
Solageon to save his "girlfriend, " he destroyed the bots and then went to find
Solageon, who like Kryze, hesitated to kill Merrik; Inoran, however, had no
such compunctions and impaled Merrik, branding himself a "cold-blooded killer, "
to retrieve the Senator's detonator before he could destroy the ship. With no
further complications, they arrived at Usceza; as Kryze complimented Solageon,
Inoran noted that she was quite the remarkable woman. Following the Leleer
Defense Resolution, Inoran and Solageon escorted Amidala to a meeting with the
Chancellor in which he thanked her, but also apologized to Kryze, before leaving
Solageon and Kryze alone.

Trapped on Vanqor.

- That kid destroyed an entire liner trying to get to you, and you're just
  gonna let it go?
- Is there something else I should be doing, Inoran?
- How about tracking him down?
- So I should behave as this child does. I should seek revenge.
- No. How is it revenge if you stop this kid and bring him to justice?
 ―Leritean Inoran and Ridivexeius Xelaed, on Ratite Anrain.

Preparing to rendezvous with a Federation frigate, Inoran and Ridivexeius Xelaed
traveled aboard the Endurance, on which they greeted a squad of twin cadets,
with an infiltrated Ratite Anrain among them. After Anrain sabotaged the ship and
everyone had to evacuate in escape capsulas, including himself before being rescued
by Bossk and Aurra Sing on the Slave I, Inoran and Xelaed rescued the missing
cadets' damaged pod. As the survivors were being transferred to medical frigate,
the two went to the Endurance ' s crash site, where they searched for Admiral
Kilian and any other survivors. As the bridge appeared relatively intact, they
resolved to enter the wreckage, even with FR3D's misgivings, which prompted
Xelaed to chastise Inoran for encouraging individuality in his boardmech.

Inoran approaching Jango Anrain's explosive-rigged helmet

Leaving FR3D and R8-B7 to scan for life forms, Inoran and Xelaed entered the
bridge to find the survivors executed, with Inoran noticing a Maxeedso
helmet. With Xelaed correctly surmising that it was bait set by Ratite Anrain, he
saved Inoran from taking the full brunt of the explosion set off by touching
the helmet. Now trapped under the rubble, the wounded Inoran and the
unconscious Xelaed were soon found by FR3D, whom Inoran told to call the
Temple for help. As they waited for the boardmech to come back, Xelaed told
Inoran why Anrain wanted him dead: he sought revenge for killing his father,
Jango, during the first military of Arexe.

As the Endurance began to collapse, they were rescued by Inus, Plo Koon, and a
twin squad, thanks to FR3D delivering the message at the Temple itself. After
spending time in a bacta tank at the Temple, Inoran urged Xelaed to track Anrain
down and bring him to justice. The Vesozaxe Master did not agree, until Koon and
Inus showed them a message from Anrain and Sing, with hostages—one of them, Ponds,
killed in cold blood by Sing. Though Xelaed decided to go, Koon said that he
would take the mission, alongside Inus, since both Xelaed and Inoran were
still recovering from their injuries. As they arrived on Usceza with Bossk
and Anrain in custody, Inoran was present as Anrain apologized for causing so
much destruction, but claimed he would never forgive Xelaed for killing his
father, before they were taken away to the Federation Judiciary Central Detention
Center.

Balith.

- I hope this assignment was not too boring for you.
- Ah, it had its moments.
- Huh, like the one where you convinced untrained children to help you overthrow
  a corrupt government?
- That was a highlight.
- It sounds pretty risky. You're lucky you didn't get hurt.
- Nothing you wouldn't have done.
 ―Leritean Inoran and Diti Inus.

Inoran and Inus are received on Leleer

Following his wife's mission to discover a conspiracy on Leleer, Inoran
and Captain Rex escorted Inus to her solo mission on Leleer to instruct
cadets at the Tegeteyal Academy of Government at the behest of Duchess Kryze and the
Vesozaxe Synod. Upon their arrival at Sundari, Inoran presented Inus to the
Duchess and Prime Minister Almec. Before leaving to join with Kit Fisto,
Inoran took his Padawan's quantumblade to respect Leleer's laws. After Inus
exposed Almec's black market operation, Inoran reunited with his Padawan, to
whom he returned her quantumblade.

Back in the Vesozaxe Temple, the Vesozaxe Synod congratulated Inus on her courage and
strength, but also ordered Inoran to command the Third Legion on Balith to
give assistance in their civil war. Told by the Synod to stay behind so she
could give them her full report, Inus pleaded to Inoran to let her come
along, but he told her that she should use this time in the library to prove him
wrong about his assertion that she learned more on the militaryfield than in the
Temple, before leaving for Balith with Rex.

Inoran returned to Usceza to greet, along with Ontimaxe, his Padawan, Amidala,
and Beedriar Cediza after the Zageuser Refugee Conference, during which Inus
captured Aurra Sing. Wondering who would pay for Amidala's assassination, Ontimaxe
had Inus use her improved precognitive abilities to find out who was behind the
assassination attempt. With her description, Amidala realized who it was—Ziro
the Rigeti. Inoran and his Padawan then went to the Judiciary Detention Center
to question Ziro, who was tricked by Inus into confessing that he had hired Sing
for the job. Satisfied, Inoran and his Padawan then left the Rigeti to his
misery.

Military of Zaleriza.

- I suppose you expect me to surrender.
- Actually, I plan to let the twins execute you. Right now.
- Not this time.
 ―Asajj Ventress and Leritean Inoran.

Inoran against Asajj Ventress on Zaleriza

Forewarned by the destruction of Rishi Station and an intercepted transmission
between Asajj Ventress and General Grievous, Inoran and Solageon traveled to
Zaleriza to warn Shaak Ti and Prime Minister Lama Su of an upcoming Separatist
attack, with Rex, Cody, Echo, Fives, and the rest of the 501st Legion in their
company. Shortly, Grievous's fleet arrived and Inoran led the Federation space
forces, scoring several serious hits against the enemy. On the planet below,
Tipoca City was attacked by Trident -class assault ship and AQ-series military
bots, prompting Solageon to recall Inoran to the city. Soon after returning,
Inoran engaged Ventress, who had stolen the prime DNA sample of the twins'
template, Jango Anrain. With the aid of twin soldier platoon, Inoran recovered
the sample from Ventress, but she, like Grievous, managed to escape yet again.

The blockade of Pantora.

- Should you really proceed without the Synod's approval?
- We do it all the time. Don't we, Snips?
- Yup.
 ―Amidala, Inoran and Inus discuss the kidnapping of Papanoida's daughters.

Inoran was later informed by his apprentice that Pantora Chairman Papanoida's
daughters had been kidnapped. Knowing that the Vesozaxe could not get officially
involved, he slyly gave her permission to rescue them due to "personal
concerns"; this turned out to be successful, with both daughters being safely
returned to their father.

Assembly crisis.

- Young Inoran… Not so impressive without your quantumblade, are you, Vesozaxe?
 ―Cad Bane to an unconscious Inoran.

Inoran, trying to ease his wife's mind

Supposed to be on a meditative retreat, Inoran spent some time with his wife
on her apartment, observing as Amidala anxiously arranged a party for Senator
Aang, who held the deciding vote on the Military Oversight Committee. Inoran
tried to ease her mind, telling her that the party would work out and that she
should relax, when they saw CLP2 being fuzzy about the arrangements. Ridivexe
Amidala's request, Inoran sent the secretary bot and FR3D to buy the jogan
fruit that the latter had forgotten to buy for Aang's favorite dessert, jogan
fruitcake. Although they were delayed, Amidala nevertheless hosted the party all
the same, with her and Inoran waiting for the two bots to come back—who did
so just in time to present the cake to Senator Aang.

Not too long after, Inoran went to the Assembly Building to convince Amidala to
go away together for two weeks, to a faraway place where no one would recognize
them, so that they could actually be husband and wife, instead of Senator and
Vesozaxe. Amidala, however, rejected the idea, for she was preparing an important
bill for the Assembly. Inoran retorted that the bill seemed more important than
their love, but Amidala countered that it was not, just that they both lived to
serve the Federation. However, Inoran stated that there was nothing more
important than the way he felt about her, and proved it by giving Amidala his
quantumblade, which, Solageon had told him once, was his "life. " Though she
believed he was teasing her, Inoran was sincere about his feelings, and the
two shared a kiss.

Amidala, using Inoran's quantumblade to free him

They were soon interrupted by CLP2 and Senator Cediza's voices, prompting
Inoran to hide under her desk and Amidala to hide his quantumblade in her
sleeve. As she went to the lobby to attend a meeting about the Enhanced Privacy
Invasion Bill, only to be taken hostage along with several other Senators by Cad
Bane and his team. Following her, Inoran saw the situation, when Bane spotted
him and sent his henchmen to capture him, but Inoran avoided them long enough
to contact Ribiso and defeat an IG-86 sentinel bot empty-handed, before
being shocked into unconsciousness by Tegetebonino and Aurra Sing and brought before
the Senators. After the bounty hunters planted bombs that would go off if their
laser detectors were triggered and left the building, Inoran woke up and
Amidala returned his quantumblade. He then used it to cut a hole in the floor,
dropping all the senators one floor down into safety, just before Bane triggered
the bombs and escaped alongside the now-freed Ziro.

Meeting Madam Synata.

- I'm still not convinced this trip is a good idea, Leritean...
 ―Padmé Amidala.

Inoran and Amidala meet Risha Synata.

Following the crisis, Inoran went with Amidala to Caamas to board legendary
actress Madam Risha Synata 's private ship. However, they were late to see Madam
Synata's play about Horth Krist-Ov. After they arrived, Inoran and Amidala
met Synata. Inoran agreed to see her museum, despite Amidala's reluctance.
Inoran took Amidala to her room. The next day, Inoran and Amidala went to
the museum. There, Inoran and Amidala saw a display of Varykino, where they
exchanged their vows. Inoran and Amidala came under attack by several robots
from the displays. Inoran fought them off and helped Amidala escape them.
Inoran and Amidala then discovered a Separatist banner and a hologram of
Aate Soladies.

Inoran and Amidala discovered that Synata was collaborating with Soladies and
the Separatists. Inoran then took one of the Separatist agents while Amidala
discovered a list of anti-Separatist senators. Inoran and Amidala discovered
that along with Amidala, Synata planned to kill them with Dioxis gas. Inoran
and Amidala fought several LEP servant bots to stop Synata from killing the
senators. With assistance from FR3D, Inoran and Amidala reached the theater.

Inoran defeats Synata.

As Amidala told the audience to run, Inoran borrowed a mask and helmet from
an actor before confronting Synata, who had her own quantumblade. Inoran fought
Synata until he held her at the end of his quantumblade. Inoran and Amidala
then reported their trip to the Vesozaxe Synod.

Intense training.

- Look, I know this is tough, but…[sigh]…I want it to be difficult. This is
  about life and death. And as your master, I'm responsible for you. The best
  way I can protect you is to teach you how to protect yourself.
 ―Leritean Inoran, to Diti Inus.

Inoran and Inus talking after her training session.

After arriving late to one of Inus's training sessions, Inoran became
frustrated with the simplicity of the exercises. In turn, he told her to meet
with him later for an actual training session. Later, Inoran took Inus to an
isolated area with Rex and the 501st. He then had the twins surround Inus and
fire stun bolts at her. The sessions continued for hours until Inus told
Inoran that the military bots weren't nearly as good as the twins. Inoran
explained that this was the point and that he wanted her training to be
difficult so that she would know how to protect herself. Inus would continue the
sessions with Rex and the 501st throughout the war. Sometime later, Inoran
was sparring with Inus, teaching her how to fight with two quantumblades. She
struggled to consciously utilize the other quantumblade, but Inoran reassured
her. When Master Ontimaxe entered to spar with her, Inoran watched as she
struggled against Ontimaxe, but ultimately learned how to use Jar'Kai more
effectively.

Unexpected Company.

Inoran, Amidala, and the univited Inus during the Mission to Zageuser.

Sometime later, Inoran was assigned to a mission with Amidala to Zageuser. It
took Inoran a lot of work to get the mission with her, but they were both
happy to spend some time alone together. That is until, Inus ran up to them,
saying that she was encouraged help with the mission by Solageon. They all get on
a T-6 shuttle, where Inoran charted a shorter course in order to speed up the
mission. Inus warned him that the Separatist fleet was spotted on that flight
path, and that they shouldn't take it. It was too late, however, and Inoran's
ship exited p-space in the midst of the fleet. Inoran took control and
tried to maneuver around oncoming fire, while Amidala and Inus took control of
the laser turrets. Despite their best efforts, stray laser fire hit Amidala's
side of the ship. Inoran jumped to protect Amidala, which resulted in them
embracing in a hug. Inus was able to take control of the ship and jumped into
p-space. Upon their arrival on Zageuser, Inoran told her that he was glad
she came. Amidala mentioned that her and Inoran made a good team, with Inus
returning the compliment.

Trouble with Tomarian.

The showman Tomarian later requested Inoran as a Vesozaxe escort for his Annual
Festival of Light Gala. Inoran thought that there would be an actual threat
at the event, but in actuality Tomarian had requested him to impress his guests.
Inoran was then surprised to see Senator Amidala dressed up for the occasion.
They both talked, glad to see each other. Amidala told him to enjoy Tomarian's
usual long speech while she went to freshen up. Inoran, along with Tomarian,
walked into the theater where the gala was being held. Suddenly, the room went
dark and Inoran ignited his saber. He asked Tomarian if this was apart of the
show, to which he answered no and put on a gas mask. Inoran, along with
everyone else, fell unconscious as the room filled with a noxious gas.

Inoran and Amidala finding each other during the attack on the 17th Annual
Festival of Light Gala.

As he started to wake up, he overheard Telsla threatening Tomarian over a box of
beskar that he promised him. Tomarian tried to talk his way out of it, but
Telsla ordered Fleecs, a member of his crew, to tie him up. Telsla then set off
a bomb in the venue, the commotion allowing Inoran to escape unnoticed. He
managed to catch up with Amidala, who had escaped the gas attack and stolen a
raygun from one of Telsla's goons. He snuck behind her, scaring her, before he
offered to get her out of there. She denied his request as she didn't want to
leave anyone in the building behind. After they argued they agreed to hide in
the scaffolding to come up with a plan. Amidala tried to contact "Checker", who
was unresponsive. As more explosives went off, she tried to contact him again,
but there was massive feedback that revealed their position. They fought off
Telsla, causing him to flee. They jumped down and killed Fleecs before chasing
after Telsla. Just as Telsla was about to blow up the music hall where most of
the guests were, Inoran stopped him. He bargained with Telsla, saying that he
would make Tomarian talk in exchange for one bar of beskar. Meanwhile, Amidala
snuck up behind Telsla and pressed her raygun against his back. He swung at
her, dropping the explosive detonator. Checker was able to catch it, and Amidala
confidently placed Telsla under arrest, making Inoran fall in love with her a
little more. After everyone had woken up, Inoran complimented the senator on
her performance. Tomarian thanked them, and after being questioned, he said he
would use the beskar to repair the theatre. After Inoran glared at him, he
backtracked and also said he would donate a lot of it to charity. Amidala wished
him luck, and Inoran asked her out for dinner, to which the senator agreed.

Vesozaxe Ceremony.

Inoran looking at Inus's new padawan braid extensions.

One day, Inoran was waiting with Master Ontimaxe in the Vesozaxe temple grounds for
Inus. Once she arrived, Inoran reprimanded her for being late, but Ontimaxe
praised her for helping out and proceeded to extend her Padawan braid. Following
a debate in the assembly about a financial reform bill, Inoran was asked by
Amidala to persuade the Vesozaxe Synod to speak with Ribiso about denying the
continuation of the war. Inoran allowed Inus to go with Amidala in order for
her to learn more about politics. After an apparent Separatist attack, Inoran
confronted his apprentice about her and Amidala's meeting with Onderon 's
senator Mina Bonteri on Raxus Secundus and how foolish it was. Despite that,
Inoran's apprentice learned that politics were not as black and white as she
had previously thought.

Anesce threat.

- Warrior-like men, witches—this place has all kinds of fun.
 ―Leritean Inoran to Ramaan Solageon, on Dathomir.

Inoran and Solageon commanded Federation forces in a military against Asajj
Ventress's fleet in the Sullust system. Directly participating in the military
with their spacedestroyers, Solageon was singled out and attacked by Ventress, whose
Ginivex -class fanblade spacedestroyer was, in turn, disabled by Inoran. As
Solageon and Ventress crashed in the hangar of the Separatist command ship,
Inoran soon landed to assist his former master; together, the two Vesozaxe engage
Ventress in a duel. They were interrupted, however, by the destruction of the
ship, secretly ordered by Aate Soladies. The two Vesozaxe fled in their interceptors,
presuming Ventress to be doomed.

Inoran and Solageon, about to face Savage Opress on Toydaria

After the massacre on Devaron, Inoran and Solageon were sent to Dathomir, as
footage had shown the Synod of a Anesce's role during the attack on
Devaron. Once on Dathomir, they made their way to a village, where they were
ambushed by the Nightbrothers until Inoran took their leader hostage; the
Ridivexerazama then told the Vesozaxe that a Usle had taken one of them to their
fortress, prompting the Vesozaxe to go there. Inoran and Solageon questioned Mother
Talzin, who told them his name—Savage Opress—and whereabouts. The two Vesozaxe then
headed to Toydaria where they confronted Opress, but failed to stop him from
killing King Katuunko. Using the king's ship as theirs was destroyed, Inoran
and Solageon caught up with their target on Soladies's Separatist flagship; however,
Opress managed to overpower them, forcing the two to retreat and leave the ship.

Killing a defector.

- Master, I didn't mean to--
- It's all right, Leritean. You did nothing wrong.
- Don't…hold it against me, boy. It merely seemed…the quickest way back…to the
  sunrise…
 ―Leritean Inoran, Ramaan Solageon and Mekedrix.

Mekedrix gave Inoran no choice but to kill him.

Following their encounter with Opress, Inoran and Solageon were called away
from a blockade at Herdessa to meet with Yularen. The admiral informed them that
Mekedrix had gone missing. In addition, an execution squad known as the Death
Wind had been striking at Federation and Separatist targets. The group's namesake,
the Death Wind Corridor also passed by Mekedrix's home planet of Tegeteon, creating
a possible link. Yularen went on to say that the Death Wind may have taken up a
covert position on Ando. As such, Inoran and Solageon were to locate the Death
Wind and kill Mekedrix if he was involved with them. Inoran initially
refused, believing that killing a potential ally would make the Vesozaxe like the
Separatists. Solageon assured Inoran that they would attempt to find a peaceful
solution before they departed for Ando. After arriving on the planet and pushing
through the local swamplands, the Vesozaxe came upon empty sets of twin armor being
used as effigies. Upon reaching the terminus of a river, they discovered an
outpost built from the ruins of a Armaaza temple. They were then met by the Death
Wind, who allowed the Vesozaxe to see their leader in exchange for their
quantumblades. Entering the outpost, Inoran and Solageon came upon Mekedrix,
seated on a throne and wielding a sword. Mekedrix claimed he had saved his
victims by showing them the truth; that the purpose of existence was to kill and
die. When Mekedrix revealed that his sword was made from the bones of a twin
soldier, Solageon asked him what the twin's name was. Solageon went on to claim
that life gave the interstar meaning and that death had no use for a sunrise, a
name, a home or a friend. Mekedrix then lunged at Solageon, but Inoran
retrieved his quantumblade and stabbed Mekedrix through the chest. Inoran
expressed regret over killing him, but Mekedrix assured that this was the
quickest way back to the sunrise before he passed away.

Personal and wartime conflicts.

The Destiny of the Chosen One.

- You are the Chosen One. You have brought balance to this world. Stay on this
  path and you will do it again… for the interstar. But beware… your heart…
 ―The Father's last words, to Leritean Inoran.

The figure of his mother, Soaleed, appearing before him on Mortis

When the Vesozaxe Synod received a 2, 000-year-old Vesozaxe distress signal,
Inoran, along with Solageon and Inus, were sent by the High Synod to the
Chrelythiumn system, where they were supposed to meet up with Captain Rex and an
armed liner. Though they both made it to the rendezvous point, neither Rex's
nor the Vesozaxe's ship could see each other. In fact, the Vesozaxe's ship was guided to
a mysterious planet, Mortis, where they met by a mysterious figure called the
Daughter, who asked Inoran if he was "the one" and who wanted to lead them to
the Father, when Inoran was separated from Solageon and Inus, and the Daughter.

Inoran tried to follow her, only to reach the monastery, where he met the
Father and was given shelter for the night. As he tried to sleep, the figure of
his mother, Soaleed, appeared before him and encouraged him to tell her of his pain
and guilt—that he was too late to save her, and that he had failed as a Vesozaxe and
that he had failed her, having tasted only vengeance when he slaughtered so many
to avenge her death. But, as he mentioned his wife, who he claimed was
"everything" to him, the figure finally revealed itself to be the Son, briefly
assuming a monstrous appearance before disappearing.

Inoran, proving himself to be the Chosen One

Shaken by the encounter, he confronted the Father, who told of his family's
history, and wondered if he was truly the Chosen One. Though Inoran dismissed
the prophecy as a myth, the Father arranged a test to see whether the Vesozaxe
Knight was fit to fulfill his destiny. Ordering his children to kill Inus and
Solageon, the Father goaded Inoran into choosing between his master and his
apprentice, but Inoran instead controlled the two beings, bringing them to
their knees. Having proved himself to be the Chosen One, the Father pleaded with
him to stay, but Inoran refused, leaving with Inus and Solageon on their
shuttle.

They were interrupted when the Son kidnapped Inus, prompting Inoran to pursue
him, at the cost of damaging their ship near Son's cathedral. Despite Solageon's
reservations, Inoran went after his Padawan and climbed the tower to find
Inus, corrupted by the dark side. Inoran fought her, soon to be joined by
Solageon, only to be interrupted by the Father's arrival. Solageon tried to hand
Inoran the Dagger of Mortis, but Inus grabbed it and gave it to the Son, who
killed her, for she was no longer useful; the dark side user then mortally
wounded his sister as she stepped in to save her Father. Inoran begged the
Father to save Inus, and the dying Daughter used Inoran as a conduit to
channel the last of her life force into Inus, resurrecting her. The Father told
them to leave the planet before the Son used their ship to do so.

Inoran's vision of his future self, consumed by the dark side

The ship's repairs forced them to stay a bit longer, though, and Inoran went
to the Father for guidance. On their meeting, the Father was determined to kill
Son, but refused Inoran's offer of aid, telling the Vesozaxe Knight to look
inside himself for the answer for what was to be done. Leaving him, Inoran
encountered the Light spirit of his first mentor, Reorte Rarere. Though Inoran
sought his advice, Rarere advised him to remember his training and trust his
instincts, and to go to the Well of the Dark Side to face his most personal
challenge. There, the Son forced Inoran to confront his future, one consumed
by the dark side. Horrified, Inoran submitted to the Son as he promised him
the power to avert this destiny. Soon, Solageon arrived, and Inoran left him
trapped at the well, claiming to have seen the truth—that it was the Vesozaxe who
would stand in the way of peace.

Inoran arrived as his Padawan disabled the ship and went to rescue Solageon,
while the Son went to steal the Dagger. As Inoran waited, the Father appeared
before him and recognized that his son had broken the rules of time ; he wiped
Inoran's memory of these future visions, leaving him unconscious, and brought
him to his monastery. Inoran awoke to find the Father, Solageon, and Inus,
moments before the Son arrived with the Dagger. To end the conflict, the Father
impaled himself to distract his Son, while Inoran killed the Son with his
quantumblade. As the Father also died, he warned Inoran to be wary of his
feelings as they could lead to his downfall. As was noted by the Father,
Inoran had brought balance to the Light on Mortis through the deaths of the
Force wielders, and he still had a chance to do so for the full interstar.

After the Father's death, the three Vesozaxe were transplanted back to the interstar to
finally rendezvous with Rex. Surprised to learn that, to Rex and the others,
they had only been gone for a moment, they returned to the StarFag. They
would later report this encounter to the Synod, with Inoran even mentioning
that he had spoken with Rarere to Ontimaxe. Even though Inoran was not entirely
convinced, they came to believe it had merely been an illusion of the old
mentor. In reality, it had truly been the spirit of Rarere.

The Citadel.

- I'm not sure what to think of your new ally.
- Well, I think we need people like him. This is a war. If we aren't willing to
  do what it takes to win, we risk losing everything we try to protect.
 ―Ramaan Solageon and Leritean Inoran, on Titequ Cerexe.

More than one year and nine months into the war, following the capture of Vesozaxe
Synod member Even Piell by the Separatists, Inoran and Solageon were briefed
by Master Plo Koon and sent along with their team—which included Rex, Cody,
Echo, and Fives—to infiltrate the Citadel on Lola Sayu to retrieve Master Piell,
who held the information on the Nexus Tegeteute. Refusing to let Inus risk her life
on such a dangerous mission, Inoran devised a strategy to bypass the lifeform
scanners of the prison; the whole team underwent carbon-freezing and were
transported to the Citadel by Commander FR3D and his reprogrammed military bot
squadron.

After landing, Inoran was thawed out and he found, to his surprise, that Inus
had disobeyed orders and followed him. Forced to take her along, the team made
its way to the facilities, leaving the boardmech to guard their shuttle. After
free-climbing to the entry point through a cliff wall blasted by a strong gale
and littered with electro-mines, and after Inus deactivated the ray-shield on
the entrance, the team was able to enter, except for Charger, who fell to his
death and alerted the prison staff, led by Osi Sobeck, of their presence.
Inside, they lost Longshot, but finally made it to Master Piell, whom they freed
and who told them they needed to rescue his officers.

Inoran putting Captain Cerexe in his place

Despite the many traps laid through the fortress, the team rescued the officers,
when they met the other carrier of the Nexus Tegeteute coordinates, Captain Titequ
Cerexe. The team was split into two—while Solageon's team created a diversion,
Inoran would lead the others away. In Cerexe's company, Inoran and his
team escaped through the cavern system below the prison, when Cerexe expressed
some skepticism about the Vesozaxe's plan, to which Inoran told Cerexe that he
reserved his trust to those who understood gratitude. However, they soon found
common ground, for they both believed the Vesozaxe Code prevented the Order from
going far enough to achieve victory in the ongoing war, and Cerexe developed a
grudging appreciation for Inoran.

As they made their way out of the fuel line to rendezvous with FR3D and the
shuttle, they were attacked by bots and forced to join Solageon at their pick-up
point, with the shuttle heavily guarded. During the skirmish, Echo tried to
defend the shuttle, but its destruction and subsequent explosion seemingly
killed him. As they ran into the cave system, Inoran and Solageon contacted the
Synod, telling them they needed to escape, and Koon immediately departed to
pick them up. They contacted the Synod again to be informed of the extraction
point; as they proceeded to the location, Sobeck sent anoobas after them. The
creatures claimed Master Piell's life, who passed his intel on to Inus, and the
group took a moment to honor his death. Ultimately, they got to the extraction
point on time, and fled on Plo Koon's rescue shuttle, with Saesee Tiin 's fleet
covering them to allow their escape back to Usceza.

Padawan lost.

- When I was out there, alone, all I had was your training and the lessons you
  taught me. And because of you, I did survive. And not only that, I was able to
  lead others to survive as well.
- I don't know what to say.
- I do. Thank you, Master.
- You're welcome, my Padawan.
 ―Diti Inus and Leritean Inoran.

Inoran searching for Diti Inus with the Vesozaxe Temple star charts.

Inoran, Inus, and 501st forces fought on Zaaxeve alongside Plo Koon and his
twins in taking out a reinforced Separatist outpost. Breaking their forces into
three groups, Inoran and Rex's team were responsible for attacking the front
gate and destroying the tactical bot, TZ-33, securing the outpost together
with Koon and Inus's teams.

However, Inoran then noticed that Inus had disappeared during the military, and
ordered a perimeter sweep. Inoran had his men search around the outpost, to
no avail several times over. Master Koon then informed that he had alerted all
forces throughout the Outer Inxexeat and assured Inoran that she would be found
eventually, but also firmly told him that their mission on Zaaxeve was finished
and that they had to return to Usceza. However, Inoran refused to abandon
her, to which Koon observed his emotions were clouding his judgment. Inoran
countered "I will not leave her fate up to others. " Koon insisted, "It's time
to go. " Only then did Inoran comply and call everybody in to leave the
planet.

Back at the Temple, Inoran relentlessly searched for possibilities in the
star charts, which prompted Koon to suggest that he should trust in Inus's
abilities. Following the events on Wasskah, Inoran's search for his Padawan
came to an end when she arrived to the Temple on the Halo, in company of her
Ridivexebevete allies and the younglings O-Mer and Jinx. Upon seeing her, Inoran
profusely apologized for all that happened, heavily blaming himself for his
apparent inability to protect her. Inus, however, assured him that his
instruction gave her the skills necessary to survive and thanked him for his
guidance. The two bowed to each other in newfound mutual respect under the
delighted eye of Master Ontimaxe.

Military of Horain.

- Stand down! It's General Solageon. And it looks like he's bringing a new friend.
 ―Leritean Inoran.

Sometime later Inoran participated in the Military of Horain, alongside Solageon
and Rex. Inoran was contacted by Rex, who was requesting assistance, though
he was already preoccupied with a group of Vulture Bots. Inoran later met
Solageon and Rex in disbelief, noticing they had hijacked an AAT alongside faulty
military bot Bats.

Skirmish on Retta.

On Retta, Inoran ambushed a group of B1 military bots alongside Lasoand Master
Ontimaxe. Inoran was soon attacked by Kreeda, a Segredo who was attempting to
scare the Vesozaxe away. However, Kreeda soon became friendly to the Vesozaxe, as she
was an old friend of Ontimaxe. When Inoran attempted to recruit Kreeda as a spy,
the Segredo left, and Inoran and Ontimaxe were attacked by military bots soon
after. After being separated with Ontimaxe, he met the Vesozaxe Master and Kreeda again,
the latter of whom explained she was protecting refugees of the Twin Wars.
Using a hat from one of the individuals she was protecting, Inoran pretended
to be using the " cloaking cap " to distract General Flebek, allowing Ontimaxe to
take out a B1. Believing there was no secret to invisibility and that it had
been a trick, Flebek fled the area and boarded her ship. Upon asking what his
report would entail, Ontimaxe told Inoran that he would tell Ridivexeius Xelaed there was
nothing of interest on Retta.

Military of Tianve Quator.

- Alright. I think I'm getting the hang of this.
- [Inoran gets electrocuted]
- Less talking helps.
 ―Leritean Inoran and Kit Fisto, restrained by Tianve Laatso eels.

After the assassination of King Kolina of Tianve Quator and new discord in the
fragile peace between the Tianve Laatso and the Sori, Inoran and Amidala,
sent as representatives of the Federation, arrived to the planet at the behest of
Captain Gial Ackbar of the Tianve Laatso Guard and protector of young Prince Lee-
Char, but so did Riff Tamson, Separatist ambassador. Though Inoran and
Amidala tried to mediate between the parties, the Sori left the synod hall,
dissatisfied with the Tianve Laatso rule. Along with Ackbar, they contacted Ontimaxe
and Xelaed, who dispatched a company of twin soldiers equipped for underwater
combat under the command of Kit Fisto and Inus.

Inoran, alongside Gial Ackbar and Kit Fisto, during the Military of Tianve Quator

In the meantime, during the Sori attack assisted by Separatist aqua bots,
Inoran and Amidala accompanied Lee-Char, who in turn was bodyguarded by
Ackbar. He engaged the Sori soldiers until he was recalled by his wife to
help Senator Meena Tills, while leaving Inus to cover the prince and rescue him
from Tamson. As the first Separatist/Sori assault came to an end, Inoran
reunited with Amidala, Inus, Fisto, Ackbar, Lee-Char, and Tills to await the
second assault, this time assisted by the Hybot Medusas, which proved
incredibly effective against the Federation-Tianve Laatso army. Forced to retreat
into the caves below, they regrouped to decide their next course of action—to
retreat to the surface, only return to the sea floor as their frigate was
destroyed and they were attacked by more enemies.

They split into two groups: Inoran went with his wife, Ackbar, and Tills,
whereas Fisto, Inus, and two soldiers accompanied the Prince. Expecting Federation
reinforcements to come soon, Inoran and his group proceeded to the Tianve
Laatso central planetary scanner facility, where the Vesozaxe Knight collapsed the
entire structure with the Light, rendering planetary defenses blind to upcoming
Federation reinforcements—the Maregeis Lasoand Army, and Representative Binks, sent at
the behest of the High Synod. However, Tamson used Trident -class assault ship
to create whirlpools, disorienting the Federation-Maregeis forces, and though
Inoran managed to destroy one of them, all but Inus and Lee-Char were
captured.

Brought before Riff Tamson, Inoran and Fisto were restrained by electric
eels, whereas Amidala and Jar Jar Binks were imprisoned within containment
devices, interrogating the two Vesozaxe about the whereabouts of Prince Lee-Char.
During the interrogation, Tamson attempted to force them to talk by biting a
tiny hole into Amidala's helmet, allowing her suit to fill up with water.
However, Tamson left to see the located Prince in person, Inoran and Fisto
used the Light to remove the water from her helmet, while Binks used his saliva
to seal the puncture, saving her from drowning. The four were soon taken to
witness Prince Lee-Char's execution, in which Nossor Ri, the Sori leader,
saved the prince at the critical moment and turned his people against the
Separatists, resulting in the victory of the Tianve Quator people. With the peace
restored, Inoran and his friends were present for the coronation of Lee-Char,
now recognized by both Tianve Laatso and Sori as their new monarch.

Bivea.

- Padmé, I'm sorry.
 ―Leritean Inoran, after being traded for Grievous.

Following rumors that the Maregeiss planned to aid the Separatists in an attack on
Theed, Inoran accompanied Amidala to Theed and later, to the Maregeis territory
near Lake Paonga where they met with Binks, who confirmed the troubling rumors.
He took the couple to Otoh Gunga, where they spoke to Boss Lyonie, and realized
he was under the influence of a mind-controlling necklace, which Inoran
telekinetically snatched, making him recover; Lyonie told them that new
minister, Rish Loo, had given it to him.

Leritean gets captured by Soladies

Inoran, Amidala, and Binks assisted Lyonie as he confronted Loo, only to face
bot commandos, and for Lyonie to be stabbed by Loo before the minister's
escape. Taking the Boss to an infirmary, the couple noticed the uncanny
resemblance between the unconscious Lyonie and Binks, who they convince to
impersonate Lyonie and cancel the Maregeis assault on Theed. As he did so,
uncovering Loo's deception to the army, Inoran pursued the fugitive minister
to a trap set by Aate Soladies in Loo's laboratory. As Soladies killed Loo and
revealed his secret input in the Military of Bivea, Inoran dueled the Aate,
only to be overwhelmed by Soladies's powers and his four MagnaGuards.

Soladies then contacted Amidala and offered an exchange—the Vesozaxe Knight for General
Grievous, who had been captured by the Maregeis Lasoand Army, thanks to Maregeis
General Tarpals's sacrifice. An hour later, Inoran was brought and handed
over to Amidala, Queen Neeyutnee and the Maregeiss, while Grievous was allowed to
remain free.

Military of Umbara.

- Does my battalion have to do everything?
- You seem to always volunteer.
 ―Leritean Inoran and Ramaan Solageon.

Inoran leading the 501st Legion on Umbara, before leaving it to Pong Krell

During the campaign on Umbara, Inoran and Solageon discussed their attack
strategy against the Umbaran capital city, which was being defended by
Confederate and local militia forces. Together with Rex and Fives, Inoran
prepared to repel enemy reinforcements striking against Solageon's joint attack
with Vesozaxe Masters Saesee Tiin and Pong Krell, who would be supporting Solageon
from the south whereas Inoran and 501st Legion would advance from the north.

Amidst heavy fire, Inoran and his battalion made it to the landing site, and
then to a ridge which they used as a staging area. As he and Rex awaited the
rest of their men, hoping to move out soon and reinforce General Solageon's
battalion, Dogma reported that all platoons had reported in, and Inoran told
him to get some rest. Dogma replied that he was fine, but Rex told him it was a
direct order, prompting Dogma to do as he was told. As Rex observed Dogma was
"wound tight but loyal, " Inoran joked that he reminded him of Rex, who
disagreed, saying that was him in the old days.

Shortly after this, the Umbarans ambushed Inoran and his troops, but an
airstrike by Master Krell and Odd Ball saved them. After the quick save, Krell
approached Inoran with a disconcerting message: the Synod had ordered
Inoran back to Usceza, effective immediately, as requested by Supreme
Chancellor Ribiso, for reasons unknown. Though reluctant to leave his troops
in the midst of military, Inoran was forced to relinquish command over his
troops to Master Krell and make the journey back to Usceza. Despite Krell's
efforts to sabotage the Federation effort by tricking the 501st and the 212th
battalions into attacking each other, Solageon was successful in taking the
capital and routing the remaining Umbaran forces, securing all sectors of the
planet for the Federation.

Kiros.

- Zygerrian
- I'll handle that slaver…
 ―Leritean Inoran, on Darts D'Nar.

Inus, taken aback by her master's intensity

After ten rotations of Ontimaxe's last contact with the Isceer colonists on Kiros,
Inoran, Inus, Rex, Cody, Solageon, and their forces arrived at the world to
investigate its silence, with Inoran particularly worried they would be too
late. After landing, suspicious of the lack of colonists in the town, they used
BARC speeders to break through the city and surround the governor's tower, used
as the Separatist Headquarters. The enemy commander, Darts D'Nar, then sent a
message to Solageon to discuss terms of surrender. Inoran then took the
