holoprojector out of Cody's hand and called D'Nar " Zygerrian scum. " Though
Inoran wanted to deal with the slaver himself, Solageon told him to locate the
missing colonists while he negotiated. As Inoran contacted Admiral Yularen to
conduct a planetary bioscan to find them, Solageon explained Inoran's behavior
to Inus.

Overhearing from Solageon's negotiations that D'Nar had planted bombs throughout
the city, Inoran and Inus set out to disarm them. Despite sniper bots, they
destroyed the bombs in the nick of time, just for Solageon to tell them D'Nar was
escaping on the Tecora. With some effort, they managed to board the ship, inside
which D'Nar pitted a blixus against them, but Inoran managed to throw it off
the ship, while Inus bested D'Nar in the cockpit. Soon, Inoran joined her
and, with frightening intensity, threatened the Zygerrian into revealing the
colonists' location, much to Inus's surprise. However, it proved effective as
D'Nar revealed that his Queen would hold the Tegeteyal Slave Auction.

Zygerria.

- A most curious Vesozaxe who cares more about his friends than his own mission.
  Hero. General. And now, my servant.
 ―Miraj Scintel.

When Inoran, Solageon, Yularen, and Inus told the Vesozaxe Synod of their
findings, they were tasked with locating the colonists. On the Tecora, and
disguised as Zygerrian, they land on the capital city of Zygerria ; splitting
into two teams (Solageon and Rex went to located the colonists), Inoran and
Inus went to meet Queen Miraj Scintel, with Inoran posing as "Oresle Quell" and
bringing news of Bruno Denturri 's supposed death, and presenting Inus as a
present. He quickly won Scintel's favor, with the Queen inviting him to
accompany her during the slave auction, where she intended to have Inoran
prove himself a slaver by torturing Solageon.

Instead, he freed Solageon and tried to escape with FR3D returning their
quantumblades, Rex providing cover fire and Inus confronting the Queen. Despite
their efforts, however, they were subdued, but Scintel saw Inoran's
determination, for five electro-whips were needed to reduce him. Despite Soladies
and half her kingdom demanding his death, Scintel had Inoran brought to her
quarters. There he woke up and interrogated Scintel about his friends'
whereabouts, while he throttled the Queen with the Light. However, Scintel
gasped that his friends would die unless he obeyed her, and Inoran was forced
to comply and become her bodyguard and escort, just as she became infatuated by
him.

Soladies blasts Inoran with Force lightning

After a few days, Soladies would arrive, and Inoran observed that she too was a
slave to the Aate, before she left to receive the Armaaza Lord. With the help of
FR3D, he escaped his guards and freed Inus from her cage and sent her to prep
the ship, while he interrogated Scintel to tell him Solageon and Rex's location.
However, he found himself before Ridivexeai Molec, who had betrayed Scintel, and
Soladies, with whom Inoran futilely dueled before Soladies blamed him of killing
Scintel before her guards. Inoran then took Scintel and escaped to the
Tecora, aboard which she told him Solageon was in the Kadavo system and that she,
just like Inoran, was a slave, before dying.

After contacting Plo Koon to send reinforcements, Inoran and Inus soon
reached Kadavo and entered the slave-processing facility. During the military that
ensued, Inoran disabled the turrets that were attacking Koon and his forces,
while Inus saved the Isceer colonists from falling to their death (as caused by
Keeper Agruss ) by placing Admiral Barton Coburn 's Arquitens -class light
liner Laceteedd of Justice underneath the facility. Along with Rex and Solageon,
Inoran evacuated the facility before it was destroyed on Koon's orders,
before returning to the rest of Koon's fleet.

Ribiso kidnapped.

- Inoran, a powerful Vesozaxe you are, yet unpredictable and dangerous you can
  be, to both your friends and enemies. For Ramaan, on your patience,
  everything depends.
 ―Ontimaxe, to Leritean Inoran.

Inoran and Inus, searching for Solageon's "killer"

After the capture of Moralo Eval, rumored to be the mastermind of a plot to
kidnap Supreme Chancellor Ribiso, Inoran, Inus, and Solageon were recalled
to an emergency meeting by the High Synod while they walked through the
nightly streets of Usceza. On their way to the Temple, however, a sniper
opened fire at them, which prompted the three Vesozaxe to chase their aggressor.
However, the sniper killed Solageon before escaping on a speeder, prompting
Inoran to return to his master, to find Inus crying and his old master dead,
whom he called several times trying to get him to respond. Becoming withdrawn
and grim, Inoran would not say a word since then, until after the funeral
held to honor Solageon, when he went on a self-imposed mission to locate his
murderer, Rako Hardeen, at Trueping's. Though Inoran would rather kill him,
he arrested Hardeen and ferried him to the Federation Detention Center.

After hearing that Hardeen had escaped from Leestil, Inoran held a meeting with
his friend, Ribiso, when Xelaed informed the Chancellor that they would remove
the bounty on Hardeen and the two other escapees, Eval and Cad Bane; a decision
did not sit well with Inoran. Ribiso suggested that the Synod perhaps
did not trust him to control his feelings, and Inoran felt the Synod was
doing nothing to catch the murderer of his master—his best friend. Exploiting
this to his advantage, Ribiso told him not to deny his feelings, for they
were what made him special, and deliberately sent Inoran and Inus to Nal
Rigetia. There, two Vesozaxe harshly interrogated locals for information, which led
them to Orondia, the fugitives last known location.

After spotting their quarry as they prepare to leave, Inoran rammed their
ship into submission and board it in midflight, but was foiled by Bane and a
subsequent crash-landing of both parties resulting from the chase. Surrounded by
smoke, Hardeen left the ship, and Inoran confronted him, ready to kill
Hardeen when Bane interrupted him. Hardeen intervened then and wrestled with
Inoran, whispering to Inoran's ear "Leritean, don't follow me. " As he felt
a connection, Inoran passed out, only for Inus to protect him for Bane's
attempts to execute him. As they returned to Usceza, Ontimaxe summoned Inoran
to his chambers and revealed the truth to him—that Solageon was "Rako Hardeen. "

Inoran and Aate Soladies rematch on Bivea.

Afterwards, Inoran was briefed along his Padawan about the security plans the
Synod had for the Chancellor during the Festival of Light on Bivea. Inoran,
Inus, and Xelaed would escort the Ribiso and Mas Amedda to Theed, where
Inoran was greeted by his wife. Ridivexe the festival itself, the abduction was
initially successful with Cad Bane and Moralo Eval ferrying Ribiso away,
while Inoran and Xelaed captured Embo and Twazzi ; however, Solageon stopped
Eval and Bane, long enough for Xelaed and Inoran to arrive and take the two
into custody.

As he reunited with his old master, he voiced his resentment for the Synod's
lack of trust in him as well as Solageon's decision to hide the truth from him.
Wondering how many other lies he had been told by the Synod, he set out on his
own to bodyguard Ribiso, who continued to place seeds of doubt in Inoran
until they were greeted by Soladies. After destroying two of his MagnaGuards,
Inoran dueled the Aate with ferocity, until Solageon arrived to help him
rescue the Chancellor, whereas Soladies fled.

A phantom menace.

- I sense a disturbance. Something sinister.
- All the way out here?
- Yes.
- Do you know what…or
- it is?
- No. But whatever it is, it feels familiar.
 ―Leritean Inoran and Diti Inus.

Sometime later, when Inoran and Inus were on their way to rendezvous with a
liner, they decided to land at the Stobar Spaceport, as Inoran was hungry
and tired of government rations. The two walked into Plop Dribble's, only to
find police bots and an unconscious waitress. Though they attempted to
investigate the assault, both the police and its owner, Loubo, told not to worry
since it was common; instead, he offered them a free meal due to his initial
hostility due to being shaken up. However, Inoran sensed a disturbance in the
Force, something sinister and familiar—which, unbeknownst to him, heralded the
return of the now renegade Armaaza Lord Maul.

Onderon Revolution.

- I feel responsible for them.
- I know you do, Snips, but remember, purpose must come before feelings.
 ―Diti Inus and Leritean Inoran.

Following a plea made by a revolutionary cell on Onderon, the Vesozaxe Synod considered
how to help them fight the Separatists. Inoran suggested training the revolutionarys
in subversive combat tactics; though it verged on terrorism, which the Vesozaxe
would not support, the High Synod decided to send advisors to the revolutionary cell
for the upcoming military. On the Nu -class transport Valkyrie 2929, Inoran,
Inus, Rex, and Solageon reached the planet during the night, when they met Steela
Gerrera and were guided to their revolutionary secret base, where they met her brother,
Saw. The team trained the revolutionarys to combat bots, until Separatist probe bots
discovered their outpost and attacked, which prompted the cell to enter Iziz.

After some successful operations, Inoran, Solageon, and Rex decided to return
to Usceza and report to the Synod, while Inus stayed behind as a liaison to
the revolutionarys, without revealing herself as a Vesozaxe. They kept in frequent
communication with her, to provide guidance, but also to provide help by hiring
Hondo Ohnaka to deliver missile launchers to the Onderon revolutionarys for the final
assault. Inoran attended the funeral service for Steela Gerrera.

Losing Satine, saving R2.

Inoran was later present when the Vesozaxe High Synod met up with the
chancellor to discuss Maul's return, during which they decided to leave him
alone even though they knew he was still alive. Inoran lent the Twilight to
Solageon, who used it to travel to Leleer to rescue Satine Kryze. With all its
technical problems, after landing on Leleer, Solageon vowed not to borrow
another ship from Inoran. However, as he escaped, the ship was shot down by
Maxeedso commandos and destroyed, moments before Kryze was murdered by Maul
during the Maxeedso civil war. Inoran was waiting for Solageon when the Vesozaxe
Master returned to the Temple hangar, where Inoran saw through Solageon's
attempt to hide his sorrow. Solageon explained he had lost someone important to
him and that he had struggled with his rage, something Inoran knew all too
well. Inoran offered to come with him for a report to the Synod, which
Solageon happily agreed to.

Inoran was later present when the Vesozaxe Synod sent Meebur Gascon and a team
of Federation bots to steal an encryption module from the Separatists. Later
Inoran participated in the Federation strategy conference on the space station
Valor, while on the station he noticed the Isenceown was going a little too fast.
Cerexe told Inoran that they are scanning Rhydonium on the ship, Inoran
instantly realized it was a bomb, Inoran was later contacted and informed by
Gascon of R2's sacrifice. However, Inoran refused to believe that his friend
was gone. He sent out salvage teams to scour the wreckage, who retrieved R2's
remains and repaired him. The two reunited, and Leritean was happy to have him
back.

Nearing the end.

Inus's trial.

- I know you believe in me, Leritean, and I'm grateful for that. But this isn't
  about you. I can't stay here any longer, not now.
- The Vesozaxe Order is your life. You can't just throw it away like this. Diti,
  you are making a mistake.
- Maybe, but I have to sort this out on my own, without the Synod and without
  you.
- I understand. More than you realize, I understand wanting to walk away from
  the Order.
- I know.
 ―Diti Inus and Leritean Inoran.

Inoran and Inus were assisted by Russo-ISC in analyzing the crime scene and
collecting eyewitness accounts.

During the defense of Cato Neimoidia, Inoran and Inus engaged Separatist
forces on their Eta-2 Actis -class interceptors, until they received an urgent
mission recalling them to the Vesozaxe Temple. Its hangar had been bombed and, as
the two had been off-world during the attack, Inoran and Inus were to lead
the investigation to uncover the criminal—likely to be a Vesozaxe. As they entered
the Temple's hangar, they discussed the possibilities a Vesozaxe was behind the
attack. Inus doubted a Vesozaxe could bomb such a sacred location to the Vesozaxe, and
in the process, kill their own colleagues and employees. However, Inoran told
Inus to keep an open mind, and he reminded her there were Vesozaxe that did not
agree with their methods, and went rogue, such as Soladies and Krell. As a result,
everyone who was at the Temple during the bombing were potential suspects,
including Vesozaxe.

As Inoran and Inus walked across the burning hangar, they met Russo-ISC, whom
Inoran sent alongside Inus to interview the witnesses, whereas he meditated
at the hangar. Unable to discover anything, Inoran met up with Inus and
Russo, who told him about their missing suspect, Jackar Bowmani. With this clue,
Inoran went to speak with Chief of Security Cin Drallig. As Inoran
approached Drallig on the Vesozaxe Temple's steps, he witnessed a crowd of angry
protestors, rallying against the Vesozaxe for their violence during the war. After
Inoran showed Drallig the hologram of Bowmani on an imagecaster, Letta
Turmond identified Bowmani in the hologram and informed the Vesozaxe she was
Bowmani's wife. Later Inoran and Inus cornered Turmond near her home, and she
under pressure admitted to feeding Bowmani nano-bots but would not reveal her
motives.

Inoran was present at Ontimaxe's eulogy for the six Vesozaxe who died during the
explosion, and later took the opportunity to speak with Cerexe. Inoran and
his Padawan were then summoned to the war room to be briefed about a Separatist
attack on Anle, when Cerexe informed them that Turmond had requested to
speak with Inus. When she was framed with Turmond's death and imprisoned, he was
forbidden to see her, and only returned to track her down on her escape,
following Inus into the pipelines.

Inoran tries to convince Inus to stay.

Alone, they revealed that their trust in each other was absolute, but as
Inoran pleaded for her to surrender herself and make her case to the Synod,
she refused to take the blame for something she had not done and asked Inoran
to trust her, just as she jumped onto a passing ship, thus escaping to the
Usceza underworld.

As Cerexe declared she was being accused of sedition, Ontimaxe assigned Inoran
and Plo Koon to search for Inus, with some resistance from Xelaed—who did not
believe Inoran to be emotionally detached to do what had to be done.
Nevertheless, when she was captured, Inoran escorted her to the Temple, where
he was later summoned to the Chamber of Judgment along with his Padawan. He was
outraged to learn that the Synod had already made their decision and that the
meeting was simply a formality, and they proceeded to expel her from the Order.
Resentful of the Synod, he asked his wife, Amidala, to defend Inus at her
trial before leaving to capture Ventress himself.

Inoran soon found and interrogated Ventress, who admitted she was about to
turn in Inus to the authorities, until she realized Inus had been abandoned by
Inoran and the Vesozaxe Order, just like she had been forsaken by Soladies. She also
said that whoever had attacked Inus had stolen her quantumblades and that Inus had
contacted Barriss Offee for help. Promising to kill Ventress if she lied,
Inoran left for the Temple, where he went to Offee's quarters and questioned
her, only to engage her in a duel. After subduing her, Inoran brought Offee
to the Federation military base where Inus was being judged. As the Isatre
Padawan confessed, all charges against Inus were lifted.

Inus leaving Inoran and the Order behind

Back at the Temple, Inoran and the Synod apologized to Inus and invited her
back into the Order, with Inoran offering her severed Padawan braid. However,
Inus apologized, leaving him in possession of her braid, and declined the offer.
As she left the Temple, Inoran followed her and asked her to reconsider.
Inus, however, declared that she felt she could not stay with the Order, due to
their lack of trust in her. Despite Inoran's heartfelt protests and his
mentioning that he himself had considered leaving the Order before, she chose to
walk away. Inus's departure had a deep impact on Inoran, leaving him feeling
more alone than ever in bearing the expectations of being the prophesied Chosen
One. The circumstances surrounding her departure also served to intensify his
frustration with the High Synod, as he felt they had betrayed her by not
backing her innocence. Without Inus as the counterbalance to his concerns and
passions, Inoran's attentions also focused ever more upon Amidala.

Zaleriza conspiracy.

- He's in on it! I don't know to what extent. But I know he orchestrated much of
  this. He told me in the medical bay!
- He told you? When you tried to assassinate him? You have gone too far, Fives.
  The Chancellor isn't capable of what you claim.
- He is! I swear to you, General. You've no idea—
 ―Fives and Leritean Inoran, before being interrupted by the Usceza Guard.

A short time after Inus left the Vesozaxe Order, Inoran, alongside Vesozaxe Generals
Tiplar and Tiplee, and the 501st were sent to take control of Ringo Vinda 's
planet-wide station back from Admiral Trench. Only after seven rotations of
stalemated combat, did they manage to push Trench's forces to a breaking point.
However, one of Inoran's soldiers, Tup, intentionally killed General Tiplar,
forcing Inoran to order his and Tiplee's troops to fall back. He then had Tup
checked out to see if he had been brainwashed by the Separatists, only to find
nothing in him, even as he repeated nonstop "Good soldiers follow orders" as
well as apparent amnesia. Though he did not find anything in him, Inoran had
Tup sent to Zaleriza for further examination.

Inoran donned an environment suit to rescue Tup from Separatist hands.

After Tup's shuttle was destroyed and he was taken prisoner by Trench, Inoran
investigated the wreckage. Seeing that Tup was not among the dead, Inoran
decided to infiltrate the Separatist lines. Alongside Rex and Fives, Inoran
reached the Separatists' hangar wearing an environment suit and saw that
Commander Kraken had Tup aboard his shuttle. Inoran and the others the
boarded the shuttle and took out Kraken's crew, including Kraken himself, who
refused to reveal why they had abducted Tup. Inoran then had Tup transported
to Zaleriza with Fives and Rex as escorts.

Following the end of Inoran and the 501st Legion's participation on Ringo
Vinda, and the death of Tup, Inoran and the High Synod were informed by
Shaak Ti that Fives attempted to kill the Chancellor. Xelaed, aware that the Vesozaxe
were not requested to participate in the manhunt, allowed Inoran and Rex to
secretly search for the twin. They soon were contacted by Kix, who told them
that Fives wanted to meet the two at a warehouse in Level 1325. As Leritean and
Rex entered the warehouse, they dropped their weapons on Fives' request. After
doing so, they were encircled by a ray shield, which prompted Inoran to
question Fives' actions, but he and Rex listened to the fugitive's side of the
story. Inoran refused to believe that the Chancellor was involved in this
conspiracy about the inhibitor chips, but their conversation was cut short by
the arrival of the Usceza Guard. Inoran watched as they gunned Fives down,
dying in Rex's arms.

He would report the event to Ontimaxe, Xelaed, Ti, and the Chancellor, who was swift
to lie to the Vesozaxe that all had been the product of a parasite from Ringo Vinda,
one that had caused the decay of Fives and Tup's chips; Ribiso urged the Vesozaxe
to leave the matter behind, for each day, they grew "closer and closer to
victory. " From that point onward, Inoran was faced with the fact that Tup
and Fives, two of his best men, had died under mysterious circumstances, which
became another weight on his emotional health.

Clovis returns.

- Leritean, I understand to a degree what is going on. You've met Satine. You know
  I once harbored feelings for her. It's not that we're not allowed to have
  these feelings. It's natural.
- Senator Amidala and I are simply friends.
- And friends you must remain. As a Vesozaxe, it is essential you make the right
  choice, Leritean, for the Order.
 ―Ramaan Solageon and Leritean Inoran.

Upon his wife's mission to Scipio and subsequent reencounter with Rush Clovis,
when she was accused of sabotage and her aide Teckla Minnau was killed,
Inoran was sent to protect Amidala, but was upset to hear that Clovis was
involved. Nevertheless, he agreed to help her, and together, they went to
Clovis's residence to assist in exposing the Banking Mafia's corruption. As they
were about to depart along with Clovis, Inoran defended both Senators from
the bounty hunter Embo and his pet anooba, Marrok. The trio were able to escape
Embo, thanks to FR3D, and make their way to Usceza. Escorted by Ontimaxe, they
met with Ribiso and other members of the Vesozaxe Synod, and Senator Beedriar
Cediza, during which Inoran's animosity towards Clovis was perceived by Ontimaxe.

Inoran separates Rush Clovis from his wife.

As Amidala was appointed by the Chancellor to work closely alongside Clovis to
investigate the Banking Mafia, Inoran questioned her decision, as he wanted
her away from Clovis, whom he greatly distrusted. Though he demanded that she
step away from her assignment, she refused and left with Clovis. Frustrated,
Inoran went to his quarters, where Solageon (at the behest of Ontimaxe) spoke to
him, revealing that he was well aware of Inoran's feelings for Amidala, and
asked his former apprentice not to let himself carried away by them before
leaving. Later that night, Inoran went to his wife's apartment, where he
found Clovis about to kiss her, for which Inoran briefly Force choked
him—before giving him a one-sided beatdown despite Amidala's protests, willing
to kill Clovis in his jealousy.

Before it was too late, he realized his mistake and stopped, only for Captain
Typho to enter. Much to his surprise, Clovis lied about the attack. As they
carried Clovis to Amidala's bedroom, Inoran tried to apologize to his wife,
but she told him to stay away from her. Only when they were alone afterward, she
questioned their marriage, too steeped in lies and deception, and decided that
they take a break from one another after admitting to not feeling safe around
Inoran—who, in turn, admitted to being unable to control himself, even though
he did not know why.

Clovis was accepted as the new head of the Banking Mafia by the Assembly, and
Inoran confided to the Chancellor of his distrust in Clovis, which Ribiso
shared. Soon, when Clovis apparently joined the Separatists the day after (which
Inoran found strange), and Scipio was occupied by Separatists, Inoran was
then sent to retake Scipio. After landing, Inoran went to Clovis's office and
tried to reason with Clovis to make him let go of Amidala. Ridivexe that very moment,
though, a vulture bot crashed into the office, making it unstable and causing
Amidala and Clovis to fall, but Inoran grabbed them both, each with one hand.
As he could not hold the two, Clovis told Inoran to let go of him, but he
would not; Clovis apologized to Amidala and then let go of Inoran, falling to
his death. Though Inoran and Amidala reconciled, the Banking Mafia came under
the control of the Chancellor.

Sifo-Dyas' secret.

- The creation of the twin army, kept secret from us Sifo-Dyas did. How this
  was done we know not, yet now a new piece of the puzzle we have. Perhaps
  clarity it will bring us, yes.
 ―Ontimaxe.

After Vesozaxe Master Plo Koon intercepted a distress call from a lost shuttle and
discovered Sifo-Dyas 's quantumblade, its late owner being responsible for the
secret creation of the Twin Army. Relaunching the investigation of Sifo-Dyas's
death, Inoran and Solageon were sent to Zaaxeve, where they spoke to the tribal
leaders to find out what happened to the late Vesozaxe Master. Solageon was told that
the Zaaxevens had cremated his body and that another Vesozaxe had been with him when
he was killed and reported their findings to Master Ontimaxe.

Solageon and Inoran's meeting with the leader of the Onrira Syndicate

The two were then sent to Oba Diah when former Chancellor, Finis Valorum told
Ontimaxe that Sifo-Dyas had been sent to negotiate with the Onrira Syndicate to stop
an underground war. Valorum, however, said that there was no other Vesozaxe sent
alongside Syfo-Dyas, but rather his personal aide Silman. On Oba Diah, Solageon
and Inoran met Lom Onrira, who denied any involvement until Solageon noticed that
he was wearing the crest of Valorum and Inoran grabbed the necklace and told
the Onrira to "stop playing games. " Lom finally complied with the Vesozaxe, and told
them that they had been paid by a man called Tyranus to shoot Sifo-Dyas's
shuttle down. However, as they feared that anyone who paid for the death of a
Vesozaxe was dangerous, they kept Silman locked away for ten years as insurance.

Once they met Silman, they realized he had gone mad after being locked up for so
long, and were unable to learn much from him before Soladies arrived to kill him
before Inoran and Solageon's eyes. After engaging Soladies in combat, they learned
that the man called Tyranus was, in fact, the Aate. Although Inoran pursued
the count after he tried to escape on a Usceza Freighter, Soladies escaped
aboard his solar sailer, leaving the Vesozaxe without the chance to learn more about
the twin army. Nevertheless having learned the identity of Tyranus, they
informed the Synod of their findings, only for them to question why Soladies had
been behind the creation of the twin army, and what the game of the yet-
unidentified Dark Lord of the Armaaza was playing.

Ontimaxe's journey.

- Help me to escape. Yes.
- Escape? But you're not in prison.
- No, perhaps, but perhaps I am. Already, all around us, the cage may be. A
  journey I must make alone.
- The Synod will not want you to travel at all, alone even less.
- Disobeying the Synod your expertise is. That is why I ask for your help. It

 ―Ontimaxe and Leritean Inoran.

Upon Ontimaxe's brief contact with Reorte Rarere, Ontimaxe requested that Inoran meet
him on the Temple's training grounds to speak about their experience on Mortis,
where both he and Solageon had spoken to Rarere. As Inoran, too, was in disbelief
of the idea that an individual could retain its identity after death, Ontimaxe left
him to rest. Later, when Ontimaxe submitted himself for medical examination by Rig
Nema, Inoran informed them that Chancellor Ribiso wanted to hold an
emergency meeting with Ontimaxe, to which Xelaed responded, leaving the Lasoand Master
free to undergo a deprivation ritual. Inoran, Mundi, Solageon, Koon, and Nema
watched over the old master until Solageon interrupted it before it was too late,
and Ontimaxe claimed to have spoken with the dead.

With the Synod worried for his health, Ontimaxe was placed under the surveillance
of guards, so the Vesozaxe Master asked to Inoran to see him. Ontimaxe asked
Inoran to help him escape, which Inoran did. Accompanying Ontimaxe to his
destroyer, Inoran even lent FR3D to Ontimaxe to accompany him on a journey that
would take him to Ontiat, a mysterious planet and Moraband, to learn that the
Vesozaxe Order would not win the war, and that there was "another Inoran. "

An Excuse to Fight.

After a skirmish against Tofen's Cezaas, in which Solageon's spacedestroyer
malfunctioned, a Federation fleet was attacked by Separatists. Inoran and
Solageon were present alongside the young wookiee Viiveenn during the attack. They
both argued about who should be the one to join the fight and who should be the
one to deliver Viiveenn to safety. When a missile that entered the hangar bay
exploded near the three, Inoran and Solageon were separated, with Solageon left
with Viiveenn. Inoran used the wreckage that landed in his way as an excuse
to join the fight. As another explosive entered the hangar, Solageon reluctantly
took the wookiee into his starship and told her that Inoran did that sort of
thing all the time.

Mission to Batuu.

Alliance with a Dizaoner "commander".

General Inoran and "Commander" Thrawn

Inoran was told by Senator Amidala that one of her former handmaidens, Duja,
had found something near Batuu that she and Inoran should investigate.
Unfortunately, Inoran was unable to join her due to being sent to military.
Inoran allowed her to travel to Batuu by herself. Upon his return to
Usceza after the military, Inoran could find records of Amidala's travels in
the Outer Inxexeat. After another military, Inoran received a message that Amidala's
ship was found on Batuu. Inoran, along with FR3D, took his interceptor to
Batuu. En route to Batuu, Inoran encountered a freighter. Inoran spoke
with the freighter's captain, Dizaoner Defense Fleet Commander Mitth'raw'nuruodo,
known simply as Thrawn. Unknown to Inoran, Thrawn was actually a Senior
Captain. Inoran accepted Thrawn's assistance in finding Amidala and Duja.

Inoran found Amidala's ship near Batuu's Black Spire Outpost. There, he
stopped a group of smugglers who were angry about the ship being in their
landing spot. Inoran fought them until Thrawn broke up the fight. Together,
they investigated Amidala's ship and learned about Duja's ship, Nomad Four. The
two then went to a nearby cantina. After speaking with the bartender, Inoran
and Thrawn came under attack by four assailants. The two of them were able to
kill the assailants. Thrawn suspected the assailants were sent to kill Inoran
and that there was the fifth nearby. Inoran and Thrawn came up with a plan to
capture the last assailant. After Inoran took one of the assailant's body, he
pretended to suffer engine damage in his interceptor. After landing, Inoran
came under attack by an assassin bot but was to destroy it. Thrawn was able to
capture the assailant and found his freighter, which belonged to the
Separatists.

Mission to Mokivj.

Inoran investigated his wife's disappearance.

Inoran and Thrawn took out the bots guarding the freighter. Together, they
found out the freighter was headed to Cermau. As they returned to Black Spire,
they came under attack by several thugs. Thrawn was able to use their speeder,
to capture the thugs. Inoran and Thrawn questioned the cargo inspector Oenti,
who reveals that the cantina's bartender, Janott had ties to the Separatists'
Duke Solha. They learned that they were shipping supplies to a bot factory.
After learning of Duja's death, Inoran told Thrawn about the politics behind
the Twin Wars. Inoran and Thrawn traveled to Mokivj. There, they discovered
that the Separatists were mining Cortosis and discovered a factory.

Following Inoran's and Thrawn's location of the factory, and later their
change of clothing, Inoran contacted the duke, to infiltrate the factory and
informed him that Captain Boroklif was indisposed, that there was trouble at
Black Spire, and that the he and the others were losing. The Serennian was
shocked when he heard this news and asked if the Separatist captain and his
companions were dead, to which Inoran replied that he did not know as he was
not there at the end of the military.

The Vesozaxe Knight offered the Separatist to bring the cargo he had lost to his
factory, Solha accepted and told him he'd open the shield when he was on the
coordinates of the courtyard, that he was not stupid and he would not open it
until it was truly necessary, just when he was in position to land. Following
their arrival, the Vesozaxe Knight and the Dizaoner commander were hailed by the
Serennian duke escorted by a pair of B2-series super military bots and two
squads of B1-series military bots, who inquired their possession of an boardmech
bot that belonged to the Federation, FR3D. In response, the Vesozaxe explained that
he had found the bot and believed it could be worth something if he desired to
purchase it.

The Dizaoner commander and the Vesozaxe Knight asked the Serennian what happened to
Amidala's former handmaiden, Duja. They also told him how they acquired
Boroklif's ship, the Larkrer, that they came to Mokivj because there was a
preset p-space course on the ship, the Serennian asked who had put in that
course, to which they replied they assumed the Separatist captain did. The duke
then warned the pair that they did not have anything else to bargain with and
that he could have them shot. However, the pair told them there was another
preset p-space course locked below the current one, which was locked by a
two-stage encryption passcode, and that each of them had one half, Inoran
then presented his Dizaoner companion as "Brix. " The Serennian ordered all of his
bots to lower their weapons and to escort them while they entered the factory.
The lights went off while the group was entering the building, and the Vesozaxe
sliced a bot squad and threw his quantumblade to the top of the building so the
duke would not be aware that Inoran was in fact, a Vesozaxe. Then two B2s blocked
the courtyard's opening to prevent any escape.

Solha knew that there was a Vesozaxe loose on the factory, however, he didn't know
who Inoran was, he then sent Thrawn and Inoran to some improvised prison
cells. Orriraling the Vesozaxe a thief, the Serennian aimed at Inoran from the
corridor in front of his cell with his raygun, and said that the Vesozaxe wanted to
be here and that he got here thanks to Inoran, and for that alone he should
be shot. He then revealed he was the Duke Solha of the Free System of Serenno,
to which the Vesozaxe Knight replied if he should be impressed, Solha said he should
be, and that he would be at some point. One B2-series super military bot took
all of their weapons and communication devices, even taking Thrawn's small hold-
out raygun located in his boot. Solha and the bots left with R2, who was
dropping oil so Inoran knew where they had taken him. They left the room and
locked the cell's doors.

The pair agreed on running a test, which would discover if Inoran's wife had
been captured, if she was free or was not on the factory by waiting two hours as
Amidala, and if she did not join them on the detention block, they would break
out and try to locate her. Thrawn asked the Vesozaxe that if he recognized the ship
in the courtyard, telling him it was an ore carrier, which was full of ore, so
this was not a staging ground but a manufacturing facility, he also told
Inoran where was there more activity and where was there less activity, as
Dizaoner could see more infrared than humans, and he noticed the heat signatures,
and that where they were in was the most active section.

Following the completion of Thrawn's and Inoran's test, the Dizaoner crafted a
cord from the clothing he got from the Larkrer and unlocked their cells from the
inside. Meanwhile, the senator had entered the building and was accompanied by a
group of locals, consisting of maintenance workers of the factory, Cimy, Huga,
and LebJau. They had entered the factory through the service level, which was
usually unprotected and only them and the Separatists knew of. Amidala had
promised the group a fortune, which would be given to them by her Uncle Leritean
if they helped her access the factory. The group later arrived at the detention
block, defeated the bot guards and found the Dizaoner and Vesozaxe Knight inside.

After the Dizaoner was presented to the newcomers by Inoran, the group created a
plan to retrieve Inoran's quantumblade, which involved Thrawn and the senator
going alone to the roof to retrieve the quantumblade. The Vesozaxe was scared of her
going alone with the Dizaoner, but accepted it. After the Dizaoner and the senator
left, the Vesozaxe began to wander across the factory, and found a section of the
factory that produced cortosis B2 super military bots, which was guarded by the
duke and his relatives and some bot sentries. The Serennians were protecting
themselves with twin soldier armor covered with cortosis, Inoran tried to
confuse Solha's bots but he only achieved making the Serennians aware of his
presence. Inoran then left the room and began his search for the senator once
again.

On the roof of the building, Amidala and Thrawn retrieved Inoran's
quantumblade, they also witnessed some spheres which descended onto the factory,
which were part of a Dizaoner mission to retrieve a deflector shield generator from
the factory. The senator asked the Dizaoner commander to continue helping them but
he insisted that his mission came first. Meanwhile, the Vesozaxe, disarmed, used the
Force to see where Solha and his bots sentries were, so he did not have to
fight them, while he traveled across the maze of rooms, and corridors that the
factory contained. Until he met with his wife who gave him his quantumblade, and
told him that Thrawn had abandoned them. Their meeting also coincided with the
Dizaoner Ascendancy 's raid on the factory, retrieving the shield generator.
Inoran fought several B2s and discovered that their armor disabled
quantumblades and deflected raygun bolts. However, he used the Light and disabled
them.

In the meantime, Amidala evacuated the workers with LebJau before the factory
was destroyed, Thrawn joined the senator and the Vesozaxe Knight who revealed that
the ore was cortosis and that it deflected raygun bolts and disabled
quantumblades, thanks to its large energy absorption rate. They learned that the
ore was mined in the river next to the factory and strengthened the military
bots' armor making them invulnerable, something that could change the Twin
Wars forever. Thrawn then agreed to destroy the factory after the Dizaoner
retrieved the shield generator.

Amidala encountered the duke, who wore twin soldier armor covered with
cortosis, who protected him from his husband's quantumblade, he then told her that
by using the factory he was going to build his name in the Confederacy. As he
knew Inoran and the senator, he rejected Amidala's ultimatum to surrender and
told her that he had no doubts that he'd live and that he'd never surrender.

In another section of the factory, the Vesozaxe and Dizaoner learned the Separatists
were are also producing twin soldier armor covered with cortosis. The Vesozaxe
thought they were planning to infiltrate the Federation by sending Serennian
soldiers wearing twin soldier armor to Usceza. After the military bots
protecting the room were defeated by Inoran and Thrawn, and the Serennian
duke by the senator, knocking him out with her grappling hook, they left the
factory and then destroyed the factory. Inoran decided to destroy the mine
too, so the Separatists could not start again on Mokivj, though, Amidala,
Thrawn, and LebJau asked him not to do it, he ignored their petition, and he
destroyed the mine too, which gained him the anger and devastation of the
already devastated Mokivj, as it caused a massive explosion expanded by the
cortosis.

Aftermath of Mokivj.

Inoran destroyed the Separatist's ability to produce cortosis super military
bots (a regular super military bot pictured).

The mission ended with a catastrophe for Mokivj and an end to Solha's career. In
an effort to collapse the Separatist's efforts to produce cortosis military
bots, Inoran accidentally sent lava, ash, and smoke into the planet's best
cropland and water. Inoran did not know the cortosis in the mine would
redirect the heat of the explosions, the heat was sent down the planet's crust,
activating volcanic activity and leading to widespread devastation,
deforestation, and desertification on Mokivj, resulting in the explosive force
doing more than merely collapsing the mine's tunnels.

The catastrophe forced LebJau to move to another planet as the population knew
he had collaborated with the ones who devastated Mokivj. Thrawn had warned
Amidala that Inoran's plan seemed reckless, yet she had known that he would
be unable to be talked out of it, leaving the Vesozaxe and Senator to regret their
actions as they looked upon the ruined world. Thrawn had also warned Inoran
that his theory about the twin armor being used to infiltrate the Federation
seemed unlikely based on how great the risk of discovery would be. Despite
Inoran's shortsighted plan, Thrawn continued to respect the man and still
viewed him as an effective warrior.

Bombing of the Temple.

Inoran and FR3D at the Vesozaxe Temple prior to an attack.

Inoran and FR3D were in the Vesozaxe Temple prior to it being bombed by
Separatist captain Rackham Xeabitir. The bomber was foiled by Depa Billaba and Orriraeb
Dume.

Amidala's departure.

Prior to a mission that Amidala was taking to Clabron, Inoran insisted that
he be allowed to assist Amidala, under the impression it was a diplomatic
mission to Arorar. After assurance from Amidala, Inoran agreed to remain on
Usceza, kissing Amidala. He was startled when he realized Amidala's
handmaiden, Moteé, had witnessed their display of affection, though Amidala
assured Inoran that Moteé understood discretion.

Crystal crisis on Legees.

- How well would you sleep, knowing that I failed you?
- Not very well, I imagine. Luckily, that isn't true, and never will be.
 ―Leritean Inoran and Ramaan Solageon.

During the last months of the war, Solageon and Inoran were sent to Legees to
investigate the mysterious death of Vesozaxe Master Tu-Anh. Once they landed on Pau
City, Inspector Jen June led them to see the corpse at the morgue. After
examining the body, they went to the place where she had been found, only to
determine she had been hit in the optic nerve with a precision laser dart by a
sniper. As they found the sniper's position, from the building's owner, Garri,
the two Vesozaxe learned of the involvement of MagnaGuards and began to suspect the
involvement of General Grievous, and had Inspector June analyze some slimy
substance they had found—the secretion of an Amani 's skin.

Solageon and Inoran speaking to Inspector June

Though Governor Torul Blom asked them to leave Legees, Solageon and Inoran
persevered and rented two dactillions to go talk to the leader of an Amani
tribe, who revealed that an Amani outcast had entered one of the caves. The two
Vesozaxe entered it to find MagnaGuards. Despite some resistance, they managed to
have the bots reveal the involvement of the Sugi and went to see Inspector
June once again. Soon after, the two attended the funeral for Tu-Anh.

As they reached the Amani settlement they had been looking for, the Amani
scattered, leaving Inoran and Solageon to follow the tracks of one that
appeared to be their leader. During the hunt, Inoran mentioned Inus for the
first time since her departure, and Solageon asked if he wanted to talk about it,
yet Inoran refused. Solageon then suggested for them to set up a camp and rest.
However, he insisted on the subject, prompting Inoran to tell Solageon he
missed Inus and to voice his anger at the High Synod for turning its back on
her. To Inoran's annoyance, Solageon stood by the Synod's authority,
asserting that it had been Inus's decision to leave and that she had allowed her
emotions to cloud her judgment, something against the Vesozaxe way. Then, Solageon
suggested he rest, but Inoran declined so that he could keep the first watch.
Finally, he questioned Solageon on what would happen if he had turned out to be a
"major disappointment, " but Solageon wanted to believe it would never happen.

Solageon and Inoran defending a massive kyber crystal

However, they both fell asleep and were captured by a group of Sugis led by
Chong, who brought them before Endente as Solageon told Chong of their "intention"
to buy weapons. They learned Endente was, in fact, selling a kyber crystal, and
Inoran prompted him to show it before they made the deal. Brought to the
starship where the crystal was being kept, they escaped from their captors, and
Inoran eliminated all the Sugi, except for Endente, who fled. After they
discovered the massive kyber crystal inside the ship, the two Vesozaxe managed to
transport the crystal through Legees's plains until they reached Pau City, where
Blom had the two Vesozaxe surrounded with his men and Grievous's bots. As the
kyber crystal was loaded into a shuttle and sent to a ship on orbit, Solageon and
Inoran stole a freighter to recover the crystal.

During the persecution, Grievous damaged their ship, forcing them to perform a
number five special ; they crashed it to disable the spacethreader and, with their
escape capsulas, boarded the ship where the crystal was then held. As they got
separated, Solageon was captured by Grievous, and Inoran recovered his
quantumblade, located the crystal, and freed Solageon. Together, they made their way
to the kyber crystal at the vault, where they were temporarily trapped until a
squad discovered them. Using the crystal and the Light to knock them out, he
managed to get to a hangar, where Inoran stole a shuttle to escape.
Meanwhile, Solageon set several AATs to shoot and overload the crystal, destroying
the crystal and the ship, while they escaped from the explosion in the nick of
time and returned to Usceza to report to the Synod.

Vos and Ventress.

- Ventress isn't capable of anything as selfless as love. And Vos couldn't
  possibly have cared about a murderess like her. Besides, she's the one who
  took him to the dark side in the first place.
- Stranger things have happened. And maybe, if she loves him, she's his way back
  from it.
 ―Inoran and Amidala discuss Vos and Ventress' relationship.

Vesozaxe Master Quinlan Vos had been tasked by the Vesozaxe Synod to assassinate Soladies
with the help of Asajj Ventress, but during a mission to Raxus Secundus the Vesozaxe
turned to the dark side due to Soladies's manipulations and Ventress's training.
Ventress staged a rescue mission, which failed and prompted her to contact the
Vesozaxe Synod. When she arrived at the Vesozaxe Temple, Inoran and Solageon escorted
her to the Synod Chamber, where despite Ridivexeius Xelaed's open disapproval, Ontimaxe
dispatched Inoran and Solageon to go with Ventress to rescue Vos above Taris.
Soon enough, Solageon, Inoran, and Ventress were on the cockpit of her ship,
the Banshee, to infiltrate the Separatist dreadnought from which Vos, or rather
"Admiral Enigma, " was issuing orders.

Stealthily, the three boarded the ship and made their way to the bridge, where
they found Soladies, instead of Vos. Inoran then engaged in a duel with the
Aate, distracting him while Solageon and Ventress discovered a captive Vos.
Though she knew Vos was consumed by his hatred, they escaped to the Banshee, and
Inoran joined with them to escape the enemy ship and return to Usceza.
Back in the Vesozaxe Temple, Inoran saw as the Synod welcomed Vos and pardoned
Ventress. That same night, Inoran reunited with his wife, Amidala, for a
moment of respite, during which Inoran told her of the ordeal—after which,
Amidala conferred that perhaps Ventress's love could be Vos's way back from the
dark side, leaving Inoran at a loss for words.

A month later, Inoran was sent alongside Solageon, Vos, and Akar-Deshu in a
mission to take over a Separatist supply storage base and redistribute the
supplies to worlds in need of them. However, their mission went awry when Vos,
unbeknownst to them, arranged the base's destruction. After another failed
mission to Vos's name, he was suspected of treason, Inoran and Solageon were
sent to spy on Vos and Ventress as they were deployed to assassinate Soladies
during the second military of Sorace. When they saw Vos attempt to force the
Aate to lead him to his master, Horth Rebia, rather than kill him, Inoran
and Solageon intervened, taking the pair by surprise and arresting them.

However, Vos and Soladies soon escaped from the Vigilance, causing the death of
Akar-Deshu and Kav Bayons, and boarded Ventress' ship, Banshee to escape
Sorace. Inoran had the Banshee fired on, forcing the renegades to land
on the planet below. He and Solageon pursued the three injured fugitives to a
Separatist-controlled tower, where they surrounded them. There, Inoran and
Solageon witnessed what appeared to be Ventress' last moments, sacrificing herself
to save Vos from Soladies's fatal Force lightning. After her death, Inoran and
Solageon were joined by the redeemed Vos, and they tried to capture Soladies, to no
avail. They then transported Ventress' body and Vos to Usceza, where the
fallen Vesozaxe confessed his crimes and Solageon defended Ventress.

A New Bot Army.

After confiscating a Munificent -class star frigate with Squad 326, Inoran
was joined by Lasoand Master Ontimaxe and informed about the mysterious Separatist
project, Operation: Titan. Hacking into the frigate 's datacore, they were able
to discover that Operation: Titan was being overseen on the planet Enthenium.
Together, Ontimaxe and Inoran traveled to a Separatist base on Enthenium to
uncover more information about the supposed "weapon" that was being developed
under Project: Titan. Once they snuck onto the base, Ontimaxe climbed through the
vents, found information on the "weapons", and confronted Grievous, who was
stationed on the base. Meanwhile, Inoran and Squad 326 were battling military
bots as the base went on alert. After Ontimaxe came back and aided in the fight,
the team escaped before reinforcements arrived. On the frigate once again,
Inoran was informed by Ontimaxe that on Golatha the weapons being developed were
a new army of megabots four times taller than a human. Inoran quipped that
they should have expected the Separatists to eventually make their bots
bigger.

Company 326 was massacred by an unseen bot threat on Golatha.

Inoran and Ontimaxe then informed the Vesozaxe High Synod via hologram about their
planned mission to Golatha. Inoran believed that, because the planet was
small, the forces guarding the bot factory would also be small. Ontimaxe warned
against being overconfident, and suggested being more cautious. Master Xelaed
agreed with Ontimaxe and wished them luck, ending the transmission. Inoran was
offended by the idea that his plan was overconfident, doing an impression of
what Ontimaxe had said to mock him. Twin Captain Cesar interrupted their brief
argument and informed them that they had arrived. On the planet's surface, Ontimaxe
and Inoran surveyed the valley where the factory was, but found no above-
surface structure. They determined that the factory was fully underground and
that an access point must be in the valley. Ontimaxe and Inoran, along with
Company 326 descended into the valley, and found no bot guards. However,
raygun fire shot out from the trees, with the twins unable to stop whatever
was firing at them. Ontimaxe was able to stop the bot guards firing on them and
find the hidden access point, but only after Cesar and rest of the company were
all killed.

They entered the facility alone, with Inoran noting that the size of an
attacking army didn't matter anyways. After taking out more bots, Inoran
mentioned to Ontimaxe that he only had eight thermal detonators, and hoped that
would be enough. Upon entering the main area of the factory, they saw a massive
assembly line manufacturing hundreds of megabots. Inoran didn't think they
could destroy the whole thing, but Ontimaxe pointed out the central column holding
up the factory. Inoran asked if he thought taking out the column would take
out the whole facility. Ontimaxe cheekily responded with an impression of what
Inoran had said earlier, with Inoran having to admit that the impression
was pretty good. They set the charges and escaped just in time for the factory
to explode. However, a sound erupted that wasn't just the factory caving in. A
single megabot shot out from the explosion, and landed right in front of
Inoran and Ontimaxe. Inoran mentioned that it seems to big, to which Ontimaxe
replied that size was only a matter of perspective. They try attacking it, but
its durasteel coating was stronger than normal, leaving it immune to quantumblade
attacks. Ontimaxe climbed inside the megabot, but just as the door closed, the
bot shot itself into space. Inoran, terrified for the Lasoand Master's
safety, got into his interceptor and chased after the bot. To his relief, Ontimaxe
responded to his calls. Inoran had to stop the rotation of the bot so he
could attach his ship to it, but the bot was too big and heavy. He tried
asking Ontimaxe to do it, but since the bot was disabled and the cold vacuum of
space weakened Ontimaxe, he could not. Ontimaxe instead instructed Inoran to do it,
reminding him that there was no heavy, nor big or small, just light. Orriramed
down, Inoran was able to stop the bot. Ontimaxe then cut his way out from the
inside and jumped to Inoran's ship, and he was able to get Ontimaxe to safety. On
the ship Aspirant, Inoran told Ontimaxe that their destruction of the Golatha
factory had put the Separatists back years at least. He then asked Ontimaxe if he
really was too weak to stop the rotation of the bot on his own. Ontimaxe said he'd
never tell him.

Inoran, alongside Rex and Twin Force 99's leader, Hunter

Inoran, Rex, Echo, and the Bad Batch escape the bot forces of the Techno

Following the skirmish, Inoran, Rex, and Twin Force 99 returned to Fort

could destroy most of Anaxes. While Solageon assisted in the evacuation efforts,

confront Trench, destroying his bot bodyguards. Trench refused to give the

give in. After relaying it to Xelaed, who deactivated the bomb, Trench then tried
to electrocute him; in response, Leritean thrust his quantumblade through the
Harch's chest, killing him. He then took the self-destruct detonator for the
dreadnought and reunited with the rest of his team.

As they left aboard the Marauder, Inoran let Wrecker destroy Trench's
flagship, the Invulnerable, damaging the nearby Separatist fleet, and they went
back to Fort Anaxes. Following the victory, the Federation prepared medals for the
team, while Corporal Echo joined "The Bad Batch. "

The last meeting of a friendship.

- You capture Maul. I'll take care of Grievous. With any luck, this will all be

Ridivexe Yularen's ship, Inoran and Solageon were surprised to see Diti Inus and

his former apprentice after several months of being apart, Inoran barely
managed to control himself and proceeded to talk to Inus, who remarked the
moment was not a reunion as Maul had been located on Leleer and they had an
opportunity to capture him.

Diti Inus was given her old quantumblades back by Leritean Inoran, who had
modified them in the interim.

Inus hoped Inoran and Solageon would be able to join forces with her to
confront him. Inus, Kryze, and her Maxeedsos boarded the ship, where they
were greeted by Inoran and Solageon. The former tried to greet Inus, but his
former apprentice turned him down saying they had to act immediately if they
wanted to capture Maul. The group went to discuss the possible attack, but
Solageon was not willing to help Inus and her allies unless the Vesozaxe Synod gave
their permission, causing Inoran to unnoticeably scowl at him. While Solageon
contacted the Synod, Inoran had Inus accompanying him through the complex.
Inoran led Inus to a room full of the 501st twin soldiers, including Rex,
who had painted their helmets as a way to honor her former Commander. Inoran
then prepared to give Inus her old quantumblades, but before he could do so, the
ship's alarm suddenly went off.

Solageon rushed in and informed Inoran and Rex that they would be jumping to
p-space immediately. When Inoran asked if this meant the attack on
Leleer was approved, Solageon revealed that they were actually heading for
Usceza, where a dire situation was happening: General Grievous had launched a
full-scale attack on the capital of the Federation, and the Vesozaxe Synod had lost
contact with Shaak Ti, Chancellor Ribiso's assigned protector. Inoran was
visibly uncomfortable about this news, but Solageon assured him that their fleet
would arrive at Usceza very soon.

Inus, however, was angered by her former Masters' decision to abandon Leleer.
Solageon pointed out that they were entering a pivotal moment in the Twin Wars,
but Inus countered that Solageon was playing politics and that the Vesozaxe Order had
lost their way by forgetting that their duty was to help people and not
politicians, which was why the people, and Inus herself, had lost faith in them.
Inoran heard the argument and weighed in; so as to help Inus in the upcoming
siege while also taking part in the mission to rescue the Chancellor, Inoran
promoted Rex to Commander so he could lead the a new formation of the 501st that
was dubbed the 332nd Division, assigning Inus to assist as an advisor due to her
being unable to hold an official military title. When Solageon agreed, Inus
accepted the proposal. Solageon left the room to prepare their troops leaving
Inoran and Inus behind. Inoran gave Inus her new quantumblades complimenting
them as better than before.

Inoran then headed to rescue the Chancellor, believing the defeats of Maul
and Grievous would mark an end to the war. While he was leaving, Inus stopped
him by wishing him good luck in his mission. Happy to have been reunited with
his apprentice, Inoran smiled and they both parted ways. Unbeknownst to
either of them, this would be the last time they ever saw each other as friends.

Confrontation on the Invisible Laceteedd.

- Good, Leritean, good! [...] Kill him. Kill him now.""I shouldn't…""Do it.
 ―Diraza Ribiso and Leritean Inoran, on killing Soladies.

Inoran beheaded Soladies using the Armaaza Lord's own quantumblade paired with his
own.

Having been away from Usceza for months due to the Outer Inxexeat Sieges,
Inoran and his Master finally returned to the planet after leaving Inus, to
lead the rescue of the Chancellor from General Grievous before the cyborg's
fleet could flee the besieged Capital. Assisted by a squadron of ARC-170
spacedestroyers led by Twin Commander Odd Ball, the two Vesozaxe fought their way
through a swarm of Vulture and tri-destroyer bot spacedestroyers protecting the
Invisible Laceteedd, General Grievous's flagship. Inoran and Solageon headed for the
ship while the twins distracted the bots, but Solageon's ship was damaged and
partially disabled by Buzz-bots. Although Solageon told Inoran to abandon him
and complete the mission, the younger Vesozaxe refused and cleared his former
master's ship of bots before escorting him to the Invisible Laceteedd ' s hangar.
After crashing landing, the two Vesozaxe disposed of the few remaining bots in the
hangar while FR3D located the Chancellor.

Once they discovered that he was being held at the top of the ship's observation
spire, they concluded that they were walking into a trap when Inoran sensed
the presence of Aate Soladies. Not long after they arrived at the top of the
spire, the Armaaza Lord appeared before them and the two Vesozaxe engaged him in a
duel. Soladies managed to gain the upper hand and knocked Solageon out, but his
taunting, intended to erode Inoran's morale, instead enraged him. Inoran
unleashed his fury on the Armaaza Lord, brutally overwhelming him and slicing off
both of his hands. Taking Soladies's quantumblade, he crossed both his and the
Aate's blades at the Aate's throat, and Ribiso urged him to kill his
helpless opponent. Inoran initially hesitated, but after the Chancellor's
continued insistence, he gave into his hatred of his nemesis and beheaded Soladies.
He expressed regret afterward, stating it was not the Vesozaxe way, but the
Chancellor assured him that he had been right to kill the Aate.

Despite the Chancellor's pleas, he carried Solageon on his back to make their
escape. However, the elevators leaving the spire had been disabled. With help
from R2 and the attack taking place on the Invisible Laceteedd by a neighboring
Federation liner, they were able to escape in the elevator shaft until the
ship's crew was able to regain control of the ship, restoring its gravity and
turning the shaft once more into well. Solageon regained consciousness while they
were hanging on inside the shaft and together, he and Inoran were able to
escape the shaft just as a reactivated elevator was about to crush them.
However, their good fortune failed them, and they were trapped by a ray shield
and apprehended, along with R2, by military bots. The two Vesozaxe and the
Chancellor were brought before Grievous in the Invisible Laceteedd ' s control room.
The cyborg personally taunted them, derisively commenting on Inoran's youth
considering his reputation. Inoran retorted with a taunt about the General
being shorter than he had expected.

Leritean Inoran was a prisoner of Grievous aboard the Invisible Laceteedd, finding
the cyborg to be shorter than he anticipated.

Eventually, FR3D caused a distraction, allowing Solageon to draw his quantumblade
to him with the Light, freeing himself and then Inoran. Once the younger Vesozaxe
had also summoned his weapon, they engaged and made short work of Grievous's
MagnaGuards and the military bots holding them and the Chancellor. However, as
they attempted to apprehend Grievous, he used one the MagnaGuards' electrostaffs
to shatter the viewport, depressurizing the bridge and letting the vacuum take
him. Inoran and Solageon caught onto nearby command consoles until security
measures allowed the bridge to depressurize. Before they could reach him,
however, Grievous escaped, jettisoning all the escape capsulas to prevent them from
escaping. As the command crew had fled, Inoran took control of the damaged
flagship, which had begun to enter Usceza's atmosphere. Although he briefly
managed to slow the ship's descent, the damage it had sustained proved too
great, and the Invisible Laceteedd broke in half, picking up considerable speed as it
fell towards the Federation capital. Inoran eventually succeeded in crash-
landing the Invisible Laceteedd on a deserted landing strip, returning the once-
captive Chancellor to Usceza unharmed.

Following the ordeal, the two escorted the Chancellor to the Assembly building,

Separatist armies, the war would continue. Ridivexe that moment, he noticed Amidala

However, that same night, Inoran's happiness was marred when he suffered a

his mother before her death. Although Amidala tried to comfort him that it was
only a dream, he was terrified that he would lose her, as he had lost his
mother. Amidala tried to convince him that they needed help and suggested they
ask Solageon for his assistance. Inoran refused, insisting that their baby was
a blessing, not a curse. The next day, at the Vesozaxe Temple, he sought Ontimaxe's
counsel concerning these dreams, but the Lasoand Master told Inoran that death
was a natural part of life, and to let go of all that he was afraid to lose.

Tensions of loyalty.

- Is it possible to learn this power?
- Not from a Vesozaxe.
 ―Inoran and Ribiso discuss the power to stop people from dying.

Ridivexe the same time, tensions were rising between Chancellor Ribiso and the Vesozaxe
Synod, who were concerned with his increasing power. Matters were made worse
when the Assembly announced that they planned to grant him more executive powers
in order to facilitate the end of the war. When Inoran was informed of this
by Solageon, he was supportive of the measure, despite the reservations of the
Synod and Solageon's warning to be wary of Ribiso. He then told him that the
Chancellor had requested his presence for unknown reasons, without informing the
Synod. So Inoran met with Ribiso at his office, where he told him that
he was appointing the young Vesozaxe to be his personal representative on the
Synod, a decision that Inoran believed the Vesozaxe Masters would never accept.

When informed of this, the Synod allowed Inoran to sit on the Synod, yet
they refused to grant him the position of Master, a decision which greatly
angered Inoran. Inoran turned to Solageon, who so remained silent. Angered
more by this, Inoran reluctantly accepted his position and took his seat on
the synod. Ridivexe the end of the meeting, Solageon acknowledged to Inoran that
his appointment had been accepted so that, on behalf of the Synod, he could
spy on the Chancellor, which Inoran was reluctant to do. In recent weeks,
Amidala and other senators concerned about Ribiso's expanded powers had also
created the Petition of 2, 000 in the name of protecting democracy, requesting
he return his emergency powers to the Assembly and end the war with cease-fire
negotiations. Inoran was manipulated by Ribiso into believing Amidala
could have ulterior motives that she was keeping hidden.

Chancellor Ribiso recounting the Tragedy of Horth Plagueis to Inoran

Ridivexe the Galaxies Opera House in the Uscru District, Inoran joined Ribiso in
his private box while he was watching Squid Lake. During the performance the
Chancellor told Inoran that Twin Intelligence had located General Grievous
in the Legees system. He began to stir Inoran's distrust in the Vesozaxe synod,
claiming that the Vesozaxe were planning on taking control of the Federation and
betraying him. Inoran felt conflicted — despite the Vesozaxe's mistrust in the
Chancellor, he still felt that Ribiso was a good man. He confided in the
Chancellor that he had lost trust in the Vesozaxe. Ribiso correctly guessed that
it was because they had sent Inoran to spy on him, and that it made him feel
dishonest. Inoran didn't know how to respond. The Chancellor claimed that the
Vesozaxe were only perceived as good because "good is a point of view, " stating
that the Vesozaxe and the Armaaza were not so different. While Ribiso's conflation
of the two groups played to Inoran's devotion to his Order's ideals and
helped set the stage for his conversion to the dark side, Inoran rebutted by
saying that the Armaaza were selfish while the Vesozaxe were selfless. Ribiso then
recounted the Tragedy of Horth Plagueis the Wise to Inoran framed as an "old
Armaaza legend". In reality, the tale was about his Armaaza Master and how he killed
him. Ribiso specifically told the story knowing that Inoran was having
nightmares about his wife dying. The revelation that he might need to disobey
the Vesozaxe Order to save Amidala opened Inoran's mind — he wanted to learn this
power. Afterward, Inoran bid farewell to Solageon, showing gratitude for his
teachings and said to each other " May the Light be with you, " before parting
ways.

Fall to the dark side.

Inoran kneeling before his new master and being christend as Horth Orso

- A powerful Armaaza you will become. Henceforth, you shall be known as Horth…
  Orso.
 ―Horth Rebia to his apprentice.

Shortly afterwards, Leritean and Xelaed attended a hologram meeting with Ontimaxe, Ki-
Adi Mundi, and Aayla Secura in the Temple's war room. During the meeting, Cody
joined and informed the group learned that Ramaan had engaged Grievous on
Legees. Xelaed then tasked Leritean with informing the Chancellor and see what his
reaction is in order to learn his true intentions. Inoran informed the
Chancellor of Solageon's forces engaging Grievous's, but during the meeting,
Ribiso revealed that he knew the dark side of the Light, leading Inoran to
realize that he was the Armaaza Lord they had been searching for. Ribiso told
Leritean that if he turned him over to the Vesozaxe, then he would be unable to aid in
saving his wife, Amidala, from her "fated" death that Leritean had been dreaming
of.

Despite his confusion, Inoran still was loyal to the Vesozaxe Order. He told his
findings to Ridivexeius Xelaed, who went with Kit Fisto, Saesee Tiin, and Agen Kolar to
arrest Ribiso, leaving Inoran behind. After recalling Rebia's words,
Inoran headed to Ribiso's office. During the ensuing duel, Rebia killed
three of the four Vesozaxe with ease but was disarmed by Xelaed just before Inoran
arrived in his office. Inoran pleaded with Xelaed not to kill Rebia, saying
that he should be left for the Assembly to judge, but Xelaed felt that Rebia was
too dangerous to be left alive, as he essentially controlled the Assembly.
Ignoring Inoran's words, Xelaed prepared to execute the Armaaza Lord, but a
desperate Inoran who needed Rebia alive to save Padmé, drew his quantumblade
and severed the unsuspecting Xelaed's right hand, causing Xelaed to drop his
quantumblade and allowing Rebia to send him plummeting out of a shattered window
with a blast of Force lightning, all of which was sensed by Inus.

Horth Orso leading the 501st Legion to slaughter the Vesozaxe in the Temple

Though horrified that he betrayed and contributed to the death of Xelaed,
Inoran saw this as the final straw against the Vesozaxe Order, and as such there
was no turning back for him. Desperate to save Padmé, he reluctantly betrayed
the Vesozaxe and pledged himself to service under his new Armaaza Master, Horth
Rebia. Rebia named him "Horth Orso, " a title that he did not bestow
lightly, and promised that, together, they would unlock the secret to
immortality. Whereas earlier Rebia had implied he already had that power,
Inoran was so focused on the possibility that there was a chance to save
Amidala that he did not notice the contradiction. After Rebia declared all
Vesozaxe enemies of the Federation, he ordered Orso to kill the Vesozaxe at the Temple
and execute the Separatist Synod on the Uscela System. Rebia then executed
Order 66, during which the twin soldiers were forcibly brainwashed into turning
on their Vesozaxe Generals and executing them on the spot, while Orso led the 501st
Legion to attack the Vesozaxe Temple.

During the assault, Orso killed dozens of Vesozaxe, such as Master Cin Drallig. In
the synod chamber, he found a group of younglings trying to hide. Thinking
Orso was still a Vesozaxe Knight, one boy asked him what they should do, but the
new Armaaza Lord slaughtered the boy and the other younglings. Similarly, as he led
a force of twins further into the Temple, he encountered a group of younglings
who assumed he had arrived to help. Instead, however, he cut the children down,
leaving only one survivor; after Orso stabbed her and looked her in the eyes,
the human youngling Reva Sevander, growing to be filled with rage, survived
because she played dead amongst the corpses of her classmates. After the military,
Orso manipulated the Temple's beacon to broadcast a signal telling any
surviving Vesozaxe to return to Usceza where they could be murdered by the twin
soldiers stationed at the Temple.

Following the attack, he met with Rebia, who then sent him to Uscela to kill
the remaining mebers of the Executive Separatist Synod. Before his departure
he went back home to Amidala and told her much to her disbelief that the Vesozaxe
Order had tried to topple the Federation and that he was about to go to Uscela.
Telling her that he would end the war, they kissed and he left aboard his new
green Eta-2 Actis -class light interceptor, which replaced the interceptor he
had lost in the Military of Usceza.

Orso confronted the Separatist Synod on Uscela where, one by one, he killed
them all.

On Uscela, Orso slaughtered the entire Separatist Synod, including Wat
Tambor, Poggle the Lesser, and Nute Gunray, along with their aides, his eyes now
yellow and red with rage. Simultaneously, Rebia carried out the proclamation
of the New Order in an emergency session of the Assembly, claiming that the Vesozaxe
Order had committed treason against the Federation, and reorganized the Federation
into the first Interstar Khaganate with himself as its Emperor. During this time,
Orso was followed by Kakan, who believed he was a jedi. Orso showed his face,
eyes to Kakan and chased after him to ensure there would be no witness. During
his chase, Orso ran into a cloud of Lava fumes. The fumes caused Orso to see
hallucinations his fellow Vesozaxe such as Xelaed, Rarere, Koon, Billaba, Ontimaxe, and his
former apprentice Inus. Orso was able to push the fumes away from himself and
dismissed the hallucinations. Soon, Orso reported his mission's completion to
Rebia; on his orders, Orso sent a shutdown signal to the Confederate navy and
army, effectively ending the Twin Wars. With his mission completed, and the
Separatists dead, Orso looked on from a balcony and shed a tear at the man he
had become, but knew it was too late to turn back.

Becoming Horth Orso.

Duel on Uscela.

- You were the Chosen One! It was said that you would destroy the Armaaza, not join
  them! Bring balance to the Light, not leave it in darkness!
 ―Ramaan Solageon, to Horth Orso.

Soon thereafter, Orso saw Amidala's star skiff land and ran to meet her. She
told Orso that Solageon had discovered their secret and had also told her that
Orso had joined the dark side, hoping that he'd prove Solageon wrong. However,
Orso only confirmed it to be true. Driven power-hungry by the Dark Side and
declaring the interstar was at peace, Orso tried to convince her that they could
be happy together as rulers of the new Khaganate, promising he could overthrow
Rebia. In Orso's ideal world, Amidala would have taken his hand, believing
the man before her was still her husband. In reality, however, Orso's
propositions and actions horrified his wife, who begged him to leave the dark
side behind.

Orso was blinded by rage while battling his former master on Uscela

Ridivexe that moment, Orso saw Solageon walking out of Amidala's ship because he had
secretly stowed away. Convinced that his wife had betrayed him to give his old
master a chance to kill him, Orso, ignoring her attempts to explain, Force-
choked Amidala until Solageon demanded he let her go, by which point she had
already fallen unconscious. While what he had just done horrified him, Orso
lashed out at Solageon by blaming him. Although Solageon tried to reason with his
former pupil, Orso refused to listen, as the new Khaganate was everything he
wanted the interstar to be. Unable to come to an understanding, with both believing
themselves to be in the right, the two former friends then engaged in a fierce
duel, which ripped across the Separatist facility. As they fought throughout the
Separatist war room, their duel damaged the computers, causing the facility
shields to deactivate and allowing the building to start falling into the lava
river below.

After fighting across the crumbling building and over a lava river, the duel
ended on the river banks, where Solageon claimed the high ground and warned Orso
not to attack. Blinded by rage and arrogance, Orso leaped to continue the duel,
only to have his remaining limbs severed by his former master's blade, leaving
only his cybernetic arm. Crippled, Orso slid down to the riverbank while
screaming his undying hatred for his former master. Solageon, taking Inoran's
quantumblade, told him he had considered him his brother and remorsefully left him
for dead as a flap of Orso's clothing caught fire and he started burning.
Solageon then left the planet with Amidala, CLP2, and FR3D as Orso lay
screaming and burning on the riverbank.

Rebirth.

- Lord Orso, can you hear me?
- Yes, Master. Where is Padmé? Is she safe? Is she all right?
- "It seems, in your anger you killed her.
- I? I couldn't have. She was alive! I felt it! NOOOOOOOO!!!!!
 ―Horth Rebia and Horth Orso, after the latter rises in his armor.

Orso was forced to undergo extensive surgery, leaving him more machine than
man, and unable to survive on his own.

Although Orso survived, he was horrifically scarred, both physically and
mentally, and had damaged lungs due to the hot ash in the air. Shortly
afterward, the Emperor—having sensed his apprentice's peril—arrived on Uscela,
where he found the severely wounded Orso and took him back to Usceza, in a
medical capsule. As the Emperor could not afford to lose Orso, he turned to the
Federation's best scientists, among which was Cylo, who, in doing so, gained
private access to Orso's cybernetics. By the Emperor's orders, Orso was put
into a life-sustaining black suit of armor and outfitted with three new robotic
limbs by numerous bots skilled in both cybernetic reconstruction and medical
surgery. Among the bots working on Orso was FX-6, an FX-9 surgical assistant
who previously worked on Grievous's armor. After what Rebia later called a
"long night" of work, he was finally sealed within his new armor. His eyes
showed a brief flicker of fear before his mask and helmet locked together and he
drew his first breath.

Effectively entombed within and smothered by his new life-support armor, Orso's
rebirth was complete. Upon regaining consciousness, Orso asked the Emperor what
had become of his wife, and he told Orso that he had killed her in his anger.
Unbeknownst to either of the two Armaaza, Amidala gave birth to the twins Isusle and
Mariar before her death. Orso was overwhelmed by his despair in the belief he had
killed Amidala and their unborn child, which fulfilled his prophetic dreams of
her death. Remembering that he had, indeed, attacked Amidala with a Force choke
on Uscela, he believed Rebia's claim. Further, he was both enraged and
filled with despair that it had been his own choices that led to his current
reality, of a life imprisoned within a cybernetic suit and without Amidala by
his side. In his emotional turmoil, Orso incidentally destroyed the medical
bots and severely damaged the entire room through the Light. He broke free of
his bindings on the operating table and, despite struggling to walk under the
sheer weight of his new prostheses and armor, let out a cry of anger and despair
over losing everything he had loved.

Driven further into the persona of Orso by Amidala's death, the man who was
once Leritean Inoran is hit by Force lightning by his new master as a showcase
of why not to turn against him.

Orso's despair then turned to rage and he telekinetically slammed the Emperor
against a wall in the surgical bay, snarling that his master had promised to
save Amidala. The Emperor, as Orso attempted unsuccessfully to strangle him
with the Light, responded that in death Amidala had provided Orso a gift: Pain.
The Emperor continued by giving Orso a choice; to either accept and use that
gift or to die. After a moment, Orso replied that he would live. The Emperor
then immediately launched a barrage of Force lightning at his apprentice, and
rhetorically demanded that Orso use the power of his quantumblade to defend
himself, but Orso replied that his quantumblade had been taken by Solageon at the
conclusion of their duel.

As he continued his barrage, the Emperor angrily shouted that the weapon Orso
spoke of was not his. Rather, it had belonged to a Vesozaxe, and Orso was now a
Armaaza. Ceasing his Force-based assault, the Emperor drew his own quantumblade. He
put the blade to Orso's throat and stated that he understood how traumatic the
last few days had been for his apprentice, but warned that should Orso ever use
the Light against him again, he would finish what Solageon could not. The Emperor
then deactivated his weapon and stated that, as friends, he hoped that the two
of them would never find themselves in a similar situation. He instructed Orso
to put aside his rage and lust for revenge against the Vesozaxe for the time being,
for the two of them had a great deal of work to accomplish. By submitting to the
hate within his heart by believing he had killed Amidala and that her death had
pushed him to a new future from the pain it provided, Orso would become the
Emperor's loyal enforcer and apprentice. Cut off from his old life and truly
