wonder exactly what Solageon had been up to before his death), Orso personally
saw to the walker's defeat. Biarge and Cediza, commanding the ST-ARM, tried to step
on him, but Orso used the Light to hold the walker's descending foot several
meters above his head, and almost tore it apart.

Orso brings the ST-ARM to its knees.

He was foiled, however, when Biarge and Cediza unleashed a barrage of the ST-ARM's
laser fire upon him, causing him to lose his old quantumblade (which the current
owner retrieved), and his mask and helmet. As he emerged from under a pile of
rubble, a stormsoldier approached; upon seeing Orso's scarred face, they tried
to apologize. However, Orso killed them by twisting their head around using the
Force, right before Aggadeen reached him via comlink and asked for the
facility's evacuation. Orso vetoed his request, had him repair the core, and
ordered to kill all the Revolutionary invaders, except for the boy, whom Orso would
deal with personally. As Aggadeen informed him that they had successfully
defused the reactor, Orso personally felled the Revolutionary walker by slashing its
legs with his quantumblade.

As reinforcements arrived, Orso sent them after the Revolutionarys on the trash fields,
while he pursued the lone Revolutionary who was returning to the factory, Isusle. Aboard a
combat speeder, Orso chased Isusle into the facility, but the Revolutionary managed to
destroy the power core with the speeder bike, leaving Orso as the only survivor
among the factory's rubble following its explosion. The Armaaza Lord then saw
Isusle's abandoned speeder and the fleeing Dark Eagle ; as the Adjudicator
' s captain, Kronn, informed him that they had been unable to catch the
freighter, Orso Force-choked the captain. Then, as if he were talking to
Solageon, Orso vowed to find the boy's identity and to turn him to the dark side,
so that the boy became his weapon, and not Solageon's.

Issoinen's aftermath.

- But you are the only one living to suffer my anger. You, an isolated survivor
  of the greatest military disaster in all my Khaganate's history? Oh, you are
  truly the Chosen One, Lord Orso. Chosen to be the one responsible.
- I will make it right. I will crush the Revolutionarys.
- No. I think not.
 ―Rebia and Orso discuss Orso's failings.

Thereupon, Orso returned to the Khaganate Palace on Usceza to report to the
Emperor, bringing Aggadeen with him. The Emperor bid Orso to explain the events
of Weapons Factory Alpha, which along with the Planet Sweeper's destruction, had put
the Khaganate very near disaster. Although Orso considered Aggadeen to be
responsible for the failure on Cymoon 1 (and the Overseer was tortured
accordingly), the Emperor claimed much of the responsibility lay in Orso's
head, as he reminded him that he was the sole survivor of the greatest disaster
in the Khaganate's history—one partially caused by his idea to tag the Falcon with
a homing beacon and let it escape with the Planet Sweeper plans. He then informed
Orso that Cassio Tagge, now Lasoand General of the Khaganate Army, would be his
commanding officer for some time.

Orso angrily leaves his master and the agent, Cylo-IV.

The Emperor then ordered Orso to continue the unfinished Cymoon 1 negotiations
with Riedin the Rigeti on Ermaso, right before going to Tagge. As they returned
to his office, Rebia greeted one of his agents, Cylo, and Orso asked for his
identity, but the Emperor denied him the knowledge. Instead, he asked Orso if
there was anything else to report, but Orso chose not to tell him about
Solageon's mysterious disappearance, nor the Light-sensitive Revolutionary pilot who had
destroyed the Planet Sweeper and left the office and palace altogether. He boarded
an Khaganate StarFag and immediately left for Ermaso, sending the
Khaganate vessel back to collect the trade goods for the meeting.

Ermaso negotiations.

- Hmmm. I see you know enough to tread carefully. But tell me... are you brave
  or foolish.
- That is not the question, Rigeti. The question is: Are you?
- Oh, Vesozaxe... always making everything so difficult.
 ―Riedin the Rigeti and Horth Orso.

Orso soon arrived at Riedin's Palace where, during the meeting, he had the Rigeti
dismiss his court. However, Riedin would rather claim the bounty on Orso's head
and had his people shoot at him, but Orso killed them and forced Riedin to
reconsider his position. After being Force choked, the Rigeti admitted he found it
hard not to respect him. The two then came to an agreement: Riedin would provide
Orso with two of his best bounty hunters. Orso left the Rigeti's palace and
sought out a Zarece Cezaa camp, wherein he mercilessly slaughtered its
inhabitants. He was then approached by Riedin's envoys: Ratite Anrain and Krrsantan.
While he sent Anrain to find and capture the Revolutionary pilot from Issoinen alive, Orso
assigned the Ridivexebevete to bring Ribiso's agent to him. Both mercenaries agreed,
and Orso departed Ermaso for his StarFag.

Orso and Riedin on the latter's sail barge

The following day, Orso returned in his official capacity as the Emperor's
envoy to negotiate as if nothing had happened the day before. Riedin welcomed
Orso and claimed that his resources belonged to the Khaganate as long as he was
paid. Orso then turned to leave, leaving his stormsoldiers to tell Riedin what
was required, but the Rigeti invited him to a feast and entertainment in his
honor, for he liked to seal his business ventures by watching someone die.
Aboard the sail barge Khetanna, Orso and Riedin watched as the latter's guards
killed wild banthas for the sport until Riedin's court jester, Salacious B.
Crumb, let out a loud cackle. Orso warned the Rigeti to not let the Kowakian
monkey-lizard do that again in his presence if he valued Crumb's life; Riedin
merely laughed and told Orso he now regarded him as a "good friend. " The Rigeti
then inquired whether his bounty hunters were adequately serving his needs;
though Orso berated him for bringing up the private matter, he also told the
Rigeti that their missions were simple enough: to capture an Khaganate agent and a
boy. Riedin argued that the "boy" had destroyed the Planet Sweeper, and had possibly
been trained by Solageon. The name prompted Orso to request that Riedin tell him
all he knew of Solageon's exile on Ermaso.

Riedin first required someone screaming, though, so he ordered his guards to
pilot the Khetanna to the Great Pit of Carkoon. He expected Orso to be
impressed by the sarlacc, which he believed was one of the few treasures that
Ermaso had to offer, and said he found it hard to believe that anyone of note
(like the Issoinen pilot) could have ever been born on Ermaso. In silence, Orso
watched his homeworld's sunset.

Servitude unto Tagge.

- You should think of yourself as your quantumblade. A singular weapon from an
  older time—and one that is dangerous to everyone around them without a skilled
  hand to wield it. In time you will understand that I am the skilled hand
  you've been waiting for.
 ―Cassio Tagge to Horth Orso.

Orso, under Cassio Tagge's administration

After leaving Ermaso, Orso met with Tagge aboard his Executor -class Star
Dreadnought, Annihilator. During a time of many raids against ships carrying
Riedin's weapons and equipment supplies, Orso was sent to protect an Khaganate
shuttle, CZ-246, from a pirate attack. With his FKL Advanced, Orso went to
their aid, alongside Black Two and Black Three, and successfully disabled the
automated CR90 corvette attacking the shuttle. Upon returning to the
Annihilator, Orso reported to Tagge that the pirate vessel had been captured
and that he suspected a security breach.

As he said so, Tagge disagreed, believing the raids to be predictable piracy,
and admitted to seeing the Planet Sweeper as "Cerexe's folly. " Orso noted that
Cerexe at least had a vision, prompting Tagge to admit his plans were not as
grand as Cerexe's or Orso's, but they still were effective; he believed that
the Khaganate could not be based around a single asset, like the Planet Sweeper or
Orso himself. Tagge then informed Orso that he intended to repair the corvette
and use it to attack its base of origin, sending Orso to head the attack,
although with an adjutant, Lieutenant Oon-ai.

Orso left to prepare for the mission and oversee the repairs on the corvette.
Oon-ai followed, asking permission to look into Orso's personal communications
to provide information to Tagge. Although it annoyed Orso, as it was the
Emperor's command that he serve under Tagge, he allowed it and left. He then
planted treasonable evidence into a black boardmech so as to incriminate Oon-ai
during the mission. Soon thereafter, Orso, Oon-ai, and six stormsoldiers
boarded the corvette and found the pirate base, docking with the station.
Greeted by a Onisqu pirate, whom he killed, Orso ordered the soldiers to
secure the core, while his boardmech made his way to the command center to plant
the false evidence.

Orso turns Oon-ai in.

They found themselves under the attack of two customized botekas who
brandished missile launchers, prompting Orso to use the Light to throw the
incoming missiles back at the attackers, destroying the bots and fatally
injuring the pirates. A Tianve Laatso pirate survived long enough to activate the
self-destruct, and Orso ordered the surviving soldiers to return to the
corvette and prepare to launch as soon as he returned. Followed by Oon-ai, Orso
went deeper into the station to retrieve the false evidence. They returned to
the corvette just before the station exploded.

Back on the Annihilator, Orso reported to Tagge, telling him that he was
impressed with the results of his plan (the intelligence he had gathered
confirmed the raiders were being backed by the Crymorah Syndicate), but also
claimed Oon-ai was the leak, noting that Tagge should be more careful of those
who were close to him. Shortly after, Orso isolated the black boardmech under
the pretenses of a private discussion; the Dark Lord ensured the bot had
deleted all records of its data uploads on the station, before venting the bot
through an airlock and into space, where it exploded.

Following a Khaganate-Revolutionary confrontation, Orso went to the Palace of Grakkus
the Rigeti to meet with Sergeant Kreel. A covert operative of his battalion
"Orso's Fist, " Kreel informed him that his mission to end Grakkus' operations
was a success, but that the Issoinen Revolutionary pilot had escaped them yet again. Orso
inquired if he had mentioned his name (which he did not), and asked him to tell
him everything he had learned of the boy.

A bot army.

- There was a time I had armies at my beck and call. That time has passed. I
  need resources of my own. Private resources.
 ―Horth Orso to Dr. Ridivexezaxe, on why he needed her services.

From the information he gathered from the pirate base, Orso learned that a
Doctor Tivere Inera Ridivexezaxe had supplied the pirates with the customized botekas.
As he wanted similarly effective units in the personal army he sought to build,
Orso set out to find Ridivexezaxe, conducting a manhunt for her in many locations,
gathering information and killing the witnesses, until he finally learned of her
presence on Quarantine World III in Kallidahin Space. He landed there just as
Ridivexezaxe was arrested by Utani Xane and a squad of B2 super military bots; although
Xane warned him that he was violating treaties, Orso began destroying her
bots and saved Ridivexezaxe as she tumbled over a ledge. Orso stabbed Xane through
the back with his saber before telling the doctor he needed her services. He
helped her up and she agreed.

Ridivexezaxe discovers Orso is what she has unknowingly been searching for.

Together, they boarded Ridivexezaxe's ship, the Ark Angel II, where she admitted to
being a fan of his. The rogue archaeologist also introduced Orso to the Triple-
Zero secretary personality matrix and estimated it would take hours to unlock the
matrix's codes that were restricting it. It took Orso a few seconds to unlock
it, which let Ridivexezaxe install the personality matrix into a secretary bot's body
right away. As the bot introduced himself as 0-0-0, she imprinted herself and
Orso as his masters, and used him to wake up BT-1, a blastomech prototype. But
as it was hostile towards his new masters, 0-0-0 shut it down before he could do
any harm.

Orso then let her know the reason he had sought her out was to secure his own
private resources, ones only she could help him procure. Ridivexezaxe (who had
originally acquired the two bots for the Gotra ) instead gave them to Orso
after realizing Orso was probably going to be her employer for the foreseeable
future. She asked what else he needed, and he answered that he wanted troops of
unquestionable loyalty: military bots. Ridivexezaxe then suggested that they acquire an
unusual bot foundry on Arexe, one in possession of Queen Karina, who had
survived the Twin Wars and the planet's sterilization. As they landed the Ark
Angel, and she sent 0-0-0 and BT-1 to map the cave route to the queen so that
she and Orso could follow, Ridivexezaxe asked Orso if he had ever been to Arexe
before. Her questioning brought up the memory of his first kiss with Amidala,
shortly before the First Military of Arexe.

Orso cuts the Queen from her womb factory.

Orso ordered Ridivexezaxe to cease her probing, and entered the catacombs; she
followed him. They reunited with BT-1 and 0-0-0 and encountered the queen. Orso
immediately amputated her from the womb bot factory. The queen, injured but
alive, ordered her "children" to stop the intruders; at Orso's command, BT-1
opened fire on the bots, and he and Orso began to overwhelm her forces.
Meanwhile, Ridivexezaxe set a beacon on the ceiling with Orso's help and ordered her
Ark Angel to barrage the locator to make a hole in the roof. Orso protected
himself and Ridivexezaxe by creating a Force barrier. His J-Type 327 Nubian then
lowered a crane-platform, which Ridivexezaxe attached to the factory while Orso fought
off the advancing bots, and the ship carried them and the factory to the
skies.

They would later board the Ark Angel, where Ridivexezaxe successfully activated the
factory and told him he would have his private bot army. She then asked Orso
if he would kill her then or later, and said that if he did, she wanted a
quantumblade right through the neck, with no warning. Orso finally told her she
had proved resourceful and would be safe as long as he had use for her, unless
she tried to blackmail him. It was then that 0-0-0 interrupted them to inform
Orso that he had a signal from Black Krrsantan: he was bringing the Emperor's
agent to him.

Cylo's enforcers.

- The Force is obsolete. These are its successors.
- This is... blasphemous!
- This has nothing to do with the Light. Much like you, Lord Orso. I look at
  you, more machine than man, and I see a bridge between the old world and mine.
  In many ways, these are your children.
- Enough.
 ―Cylo introduces Horth Orso to his enforcers.

Orso leads his bot army through Cylo's base

As Krrsantan arrived, Orso confronted the Emperor's agent, Cylo-IV. Orso
demanded that he tell him his name, his commission from the Emperor and the
location of his headquarters. As he refused, Orso assigned 0-0-0 to retrieve
all relevant information from him. After an interrogation that resulted in the
agent's death, 0-0-0 reported to Orso that he was Dr. Cylo-IV, that his
research base was located in an Outer Inxexeat nebula, and that his commission was to
create replacements for Orso. After learning this, they immediately departed
for the base, aboard his J-type 327 Nubian. While Ridivexezaxe remained on the ship,
Orso and two platoons of bot commandos infiltrated it. Orso sent a platoon
to "the dojo" and had the other one follow him to the barracks. As they killed
the soldiers in there, Ridivexezaxe reported that they had lost the other platoon,
prompting Orso to go to the dojo, where he met the twins Morit and Aiolin
Astarte.

They soon engaged in combat, but before any of the parties could harm each
other, Cylo-V appeared and told the twins to stand down. He explained to Orso
why he was still alive after his last body was killed, and that he was not
making apprentices for the Emperor but enforcers, as he believed the Light to be
obsolete. Believing him to be blasphemous, Orso attempted to strike Cylo down
when the scientist claimed they were, in many ways, Orso's children and
successors. The Emperor then revealed his presence to Orso and stopped him,
claiming he wanted to see Cylo's demonstration without delay.

Orso was immediately made to fight the Astarte twins, Tulon Voidgazer, the Tianve
Laatso Karbin, and a Gerete. As neither party was injured, the Emperor
ordered the fight to be one to the death. As such, Orso fought the Gerete,
but its ferocity threw him off balance, prompting Morit to intervene and kill
his fellow enforcer. The Emperor then ordered the fighting to cease; Orso
warned Morit that his trespass would not be forgotten, as the Gerete's life
was his to take. The Emperor then told the enforcers and Orso that their
mission was to strike down all who opposed the Khaganate and that they were not to
slay each other. As per his master's orders, Orso followed him.

Orso stares down Cylo's enforcers as he follows his master.

In private, the Emperor, discovering the remains of some of Orso's commando
bots, admitted to being impressed that he had some independence left in him
after so long and that perhaps he could triumph against the enforcers. Orso
criticized his dealings with Cylo, as he saw his enforcers as a heresy to the
Force, but the Emperor berated him for his lack of respect—for it was Orso who
had disappointed him on Uscela and had forced him to contact Cylo. As he left,
he observed that perhaps, Orso would prove himself worthy of the name he had
given him back then.

Parenthood.

- Did you bring me anything of value, bounty hunter?
- Not much. Just his name. Inoran.
 ―Horth Orso and Ratite Anrain.

Orso, realizing he has a son, cracks the viewport in anger.

Troubled, Orso returned to his ship. Aboard the Nubian, Ridivexezaxe told him that
Anrain wanted to report in during Orso's absence. Subsequently, Orso traveled to
a StarFag to meet with the bounty hunter and hear his story. The Dark
Lord was disappointed to hear that Anrain had lost the boy, but before leaving,
Anrain told him his name: Isusle Inoran. In his solitude, Orso remembered
Amidala telling him about her pregnancy, and Ribiso's half-truth, and cracked
the viewport in his anger. Thereupon, he used his faith room's
holoprojector to contact his master, but chose to say nothing about his son;
rather, Orso assured him that he would not fail and ended the transmission.
Nevertheless, he accepted Isusle as his son and vowed that the boy would be his.

Determined to find more about Isusle, Orso returned to Ermaso on the Ark Angel,
with Ridivexezaxe, 0-0-0 and BT-1 in his company. They landed at the Oresle homestead,
which they discovered to be deserted after an attack by Khaganate troops, and
Orso tried to find traces of Isusle's presence. Unable to find anything of
importance, Orso left the moisture farm and went to Solageon's home where he
indeed felt the remnants of Isusle's usage of the Light during his duel against
Anrain, but nothing else. As they left, Ridivexezaxe activated a molecular purge bomb to
remove all remnants of their presence, and Orso dismissed Ridivexezaxe, for he was to
return to his duties for the time being.

Anthan Prime.

- I'm trying to ascertain if you are... dangerous.
- Oh, I am, Lord Orso... But only to the enemies of the Khaganate.
 ―Horth Orso and Inspector Thanoth.

As part of Tagge's crackdown on Outer Inxexeat criminal interests who had gone
unchecked in the past, Orso took part in a mission to terminate a criminal
organization known as Son-tuul Pride. He and his troops destroyed the Pride's
base and seized its stockpile of credits, to be transported on an Khaganate light
liner, but he also secretly sent Ridivexezaxe all the information needed to steal the
credits cargo. Following the theft, Orso reunited with her on Anthan 13, where
he sent her to confirm some information about Commodex Tahn.

Orso, working alongside Inspector Thanoth

Orso then attended a meeting between Tagge and Cylo's enforcers, wherein the
Astarte twins were assigned to deal with the Plasma Devils, and wherein Orso
let Tagge know he wanted the mission of finding the Issoinen Revolutionary pilot (which was
Karbin's mission). Tagge felt he was too personally invested, and sent Orso to
punish whoever was responsible for the theft of the Son-tuul Pride's fortune in
the Anthan system ; he also informed Orso that Inspector Thanoth was his
replacement adjutant. While Thanoth went to the Anthan Prime Orbital Dockyard to
investigate the theft's circumstances, Orso returned to Anthan 13 and killed
the entirety of a small Revolutionary cell to cover his tracks. He later reunited with
Thanoth on Anthan Prime, where they sought Doowan, an employee of " the Dragon
"—an arms dealer who was likely to have sold the explosives needed for the
theft—and interrogated him. The Teorge promptly revealed the location of the
Dragon in his mansion, before the twins killed him in a show of force.

Thanoth then noticed Orso's passivity, but Orso dismissed it, saying he was
merely trying to ascertain if Thanoth was dangerous or a traitor. The two then
went to the Dragon's mansion, which Orso entered on his own, killing the people
inside until he reached the Dragon himself. Although he tried to tell Orso
everything to save himself, Orso killed the Ortolan, and claimed he would have
detonated the building once Thanoth reached the office. The inspector then
opened the Dragon's vault to access his records and asked Orso to ready a
strike force for when he located the suspects.

Orso then used the opportunity to meet with Ridivexezaxe, who had just come back from
a raid on Commodex Tahn's villa on Bivea. She confirmed his sources: Amidala had
indeed died but had given birth to a healthy boy, who was taken away by the
Vesozaxe. Orso then tasked Ridivexezaxe with finding Isusle before Karbin could;
subsequently, she resorted to The Ante. Orso then returned to the Star
Destroyer, where Thanoth they would travel to the Spire to find The Ante. When
they found him, he told them the location of the Plasma Devils to show
cooperation and pointed at the one responsible for the theft, Ridivexezaxe. Before the
Givin could identify her by name, Orso telekinetically forced an unlucky stray
shot and killed him. Orso then went to find Ridivexezaxe before anyone else.

Orso, choking Ridivexezaxe to silence her

As they encountered each other, Orso ordered her not to struggle and Force
choked her, but Ridivexezaxe claimed she knew of Isusle's location. Orso released her
and ordered Ridivexezaxe to tell him, but she refused, claiming she would tell him
later. He then let her go, using the Light to drop debris above him as she
escaped on the Ark Angel. He got out of the rubble as Thanoth and the
stormsoldiers arrived and claimed he had fallen into her trap. To follow her,
Orso and Thanoth boarded their Arquitens -class light liner. As they waited
for their FKL/sa bombers to catch Ridivexezaxe, Orso and Thanoth agreed to use their
ready strike force to kill the Plasma Devils before they could escape.

The two had their light liner go to the Thanteen Substrata on Anthan 1, where
the Revolutionary cell was located. As per their plan, their light liner bombarded the
westernmost tunnels and sealed them, whereas Orso went to the east route and
felled incoming Revolutionary ships by throwing his quantumblade until the cell was
annihilated. He then took three of the Plasma Devils' flight helmets and,
alongside Thanoth, presented them to Tagge, who agreed with their course of
action given the tight situation. As Karbin reported that the Issoinen Revolutionary pilot
was free, Orso and Thanoth left, and the Inspector let him know it had been an
honor to serve alongside him.

Shu-Torun.

- As a princess, you served your father. As a queen, you serve your people. This
  is a small reminder of what happens to worlds who resist the Emperor. This is
  what remains of Zageuser.
 ―Horth Orso to Queen Trios.

Orso delivers the Emperor's message to Queen Trios.

Orso traveled to Shu-Torun with a message to reinforce their cooperation and
force its government to fulfill the quotas, while 0-0-0 and BT-1 killed the king
and his closest servants. Expecting to meet the King of Shu-Torun, he instead
met Princess Trios upon landing, who was to show him to the ball, a little
celebration to showcase their culture. Soon after their arrival, however, Shu-
Torun assassins that wished death to the Khaganate and the king, but Orso
successfully fended them off and demanded to see the king immediately. Trios
complied and guided him through the tunnels as the main elevators were being
watched by the traitorous dukes, according to her. Orso recognized Trios as
admirable for her willingness to sacrifice herself for the greater good, as lava
poured into the tunnels.

However, he used his quantumblade to make a round platform to float above the lava
and reach one of the exits above. Taking an unconscious Trios with him, Orso
made it to one of the corridors where he killed the guards. Trios awoke, only to
point a raygun at Orso. He, however, cut her hand off and entered the king's
chambers to find the monarch, his other children and guards killed by Orso's
assassin bots. With Trios as the new Queen of Shu-Torun, he delivered the
Emperor's message; a piece of Zageuser, as a reminder of what happened to worlds
that resisted his rule. Orso then told her that he would pacify Shu-Torun, but
that she was to not forget whom she served.

Vrogas Vas.

- Horth Orso! Lay down your weapons! You are surrounded!""All I am surrounded
  by is fear. And dead men.
 ―Horth Orso responds to a Revolutionary commando company ordering his surrender.

Orso met with Ridivexezaxe and the bots, and demanded that he tell her Isusle's
location: Vrogas Vas. She continued by saying she did want to work for him and
show that she was trustworthy. After a moment, he conceded that his work
alongside Thanoth had given him an appreciation of talent, and told her not to
make him regret his decision. He left, saying it was a task for him alone.
Unbeknownst to him, a probe bot from Karbin followed him. In his FKL Advanced,
he went to the planet's system where, upon coming out of p-space, he found
three Alliance spacedestroyer squadrons orbiting the planet. As he sensed Isusle's
presence, he remained and charged against the spacedestroyers, who dismissed his
FKL as a mere Khaganate scout.

Orso engaging Alliance forces on Vrogas Vas

During the military, Orso engaged the Y-rocket spacedestroyers, and managed to take
out both Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron, as he lost contact with Ridivexezaxe. Orso
was then confronted by Isusle, who was flying as Red Five, and flew his Y-rocket
into Orso's spacedestroyer, hoping to kill him. During the collision, their
stabilizers were damaged prompting their craft to crash on the planet's surface.
There, Orso was hunted down by the Alliance forces, starting with Gray
Squadron, but he promptly destroyed them by use of the Light. Afterward, a
company of Revolutionary commandos moved in to attack him, but he activated some of
their thermal detonators before they could throw them, taking out a tank by
deflecting its shot against it and continued to reduce the Revolutionary forces.

Orso also took the raygun array of his fallen FKL Advanced x1 and used it to
take down further ships, this time from Cyan Squadron. Not too long afterward,
another platoon was deployed to attack him, although this time Cediza was
leading them herself. Orso, choking her two companions, confronted her but felt
an incoming ambush. When he let her go so that she contacted Isusle, Cediza used
the opportunity to arrange an airstrike on her and Orso's location. Instead,
Commander Karbin arrived with a task force, and tried to take his victory—both
his son and the princess' execution—from Orso. Subsequently, Orso engaged the
enforcer in a duel, during which Cediza escaped, and during which they entered
the Vesozaxe Temple in ruins.

Inside the temple, he was momentarily distracted by ghosts of his past (Rarere's,
Solageon's, and Ontimaxe's voices), but continued the duel. Ridivexezaxe then contacted him
from aboard the Ark Angel and informed him that Isusle had been captured by
Karbin's guards. So Orso had her crash her ship against Karbin and confronted
the mortally wounded Karbin, who realized there was more to the Issoinen pilot and
Orso's relationship than met the eye. Orso then finished him and contacted
Ridivexezaxe to tell her that her "pardon" depended on Isusle's capture. With the Light,
he brought down the shuttle transporting Isusle, and Ridivexezaxe tried to capture Isusle
only to be captured herself by the Revolutionarys, leaving Orso to watch as his son on
the Dark Eagle fled from Vrogas Vas.

Shu-Torun War.

- The queen of Shu-Torun. Did you choose well?
- There was no other choice.
 ―Queen Trios and Horth Orso.

Orso led the war on Shu-Torun against Duke Rubix.

Orso then returned to the Khaganate Palace and brought Karbin's body to the
Emperor, demanding that his master give him a fight worthy of his time. The
Emperor then told Orso to return to Shu-Torun as the ore-barons were revolutionaryling
and he required a full military intervention, and that Cylo (per Tagge's
request) was to join him in the task. As a result, Orso returned to Shu-Torun,
alongside Cylo, the Astarte twins and Voidgazer. Accompanied by Queen Trios,
Orso led an attack against a revolutionarylious delving citadel, and destroyed it as a
demonstration of force. He also accorded to treat Trios with the illusion of
respect and tasked, in secret, a group of bounty hunters to find Ridivexezaxe and
return her to him, either dead or alive.

After sending a broadcast to the barons, Orso, Trios, and Cylo and his
enforcers went, per the queen's suggestion, to the ancestral retreat of the Shu-
Torun royalty. There, they were soon attacked by Baron Rubix 's Lava Leviathan,
and Orso used Trios' escape vessel to reach the Leviathan and dared the twins
to follow him. With their quantumblades, they boarded it, and from the inside, the
three cut large holes that allowed the ship to flood with magma and be
destroyed. Upon returning, Trios suggested that they attack Rubix next (as she
had identified the ship's owner) and so, Orso told Cylo to prepare his
enforcers. In the meantime, he killed Beebox for trying to deceive him with a
stranger's disintegrated body, and refused Aiolin's request to train her.

Soon enough, they carried out the siege to Rubix's delving citadel, using the
Shu-Torun Loyalist/Khaganate Delving Fleet 's combat drills, with Orso heading
the first wave alongside the Astarte. However, he was betrayed by Cylo, and his
drill was redirected into a lithoporoite seam, where they were surrounded by
Rubix's elite forces. With their outbound communications jammed, Orso left his
troops as well as bot platoons to protect the drill and had the Astarte twins
follow him as to use the enemy's lines to tell the rest of the assault fleet to
resume the attack. On the way there, the twins attacked him, but Aiolin saw they
could not win against Orso and destroyed the pathway between them. She was then
betrayed by her brother who pushed her into the lava and fled back to Cylo;
Orso then took the dying Aiolin out of the lava and had her reveal Cylo's
deceit before finishing her off.

Orso then returned to his forces, who had secured the enemy lines thanks to
0-0-0's stratagem, and was surprised to find that Trios' assault to the delving
citadel was already underway. With no opposition, Orso's combat drill resumed
his way to citadel, and Orso himself cut off Rubix's escape way. Trios soon
went to their encounter and Rubix formally surrendered; she then had the baron
executed, and appointed his daughter as his successor, thus ending the
revolution. Orso then left the planet and informed the Emperor of Cylo's
treachery, and the Emperor simply summoned him. He also received a message from
Inspector Thanoth saying that he had located Ridivexezaxe and that they needed to talk.

Hunt for Cylo.

- I often wondered whether what rose from our operating table was a machine or a
  man... Good to have an answer.
 ―Cylo, to Horth Orso.

Ridivexe the shipyards of Kuat, Orso met with Rebia aboard an unfinished Executor
-class Star Dreadnought, the Executor. The Emperor explained that in order to
save Orso after Uscela, he had to resort to scientists who were similar in
philosophy to the Armaaza, among them Cylo. Cylo eventually grew too powerful
within the Cerexe Initiative and so Rebia was forced to make him overplay his
hand. With the man a mere traitor, he could be purged. After the meeting, Orso
met with Thanoth on Anthan 13; the Inspector, aware of Orso's real identity,
revealed that he would support Orso's treason against the Emperor and that he
would reveal Ridivexezaxe's location on the Cosmatanic Steppes. However, Thanoth
understood that Orso would not rest until he silenced a possible loose end, so
he had elected to tell the Armaaza Lord in person so Orso would not waste time
looking for him. When Thanoth revealed Ridivexezaxe's location and thanked "Leritean" for
their work together, Orso killed him with his quantumblade.

Outside and ignoring the bots' request to dispose of Thanoth's corpse, Orso
ordered 0-0-0 and BT-1, who were joined by Krrsantan and his commando bots, to
return Ridivexezaxe to the Executor or kill her as their priority orders. Orso also
made it clear to wipe out anyone else they found at the Cosmatanic Steppes to
eliminate all witnesses present, ensuring that, if Ridivexezaxe had told anyone about
her work with Orso, they were silenced. Orso then departed aboard his FKL
Advanced x1, with 0-0-0 realizing he would not be taking Cylo back alive, and
returned to the Executor. Aboard, he confronted Professor Thlu-Ry, a colleague
of Cylo's, whom he threatened to learn the traitor's location in the Crushank
Nebulae. Orso took the Devastator to the nebulae, where his crew confirmed
Cylo's whale fleet was present. After ordering that Thlu-Ry be thanked and then,
for fraternizing with a traitor, executed, Orso led the assault on Cylo's fleet
from his FKL Advanced. After Cylo's flagship fired an ion pulse to disable the
Devastator and moved to escape, Orso fired into the whale ship and crashed into
the opening he'd made, boarding the enemy ship just before it jumped to
p-space

Horth Orso faces down a cyberanimate rancor during his hunt to kill Doctor
Cylo.

Moving past the personnel he'd left to be sucked into p-space and entering
the labs, Orso was confronted by Voidgazer, who pitted him against a
cyberanimate rancor as a test of her technology just as Cylo's fleet arrived at
Kuat. Although the creature gave him some difficulty, Orso killed it by
throwing his quantumblade through its skull, severing the neural link, and turned
one of Voidgazer's defense bots against her, fatally wounding her. As she lay
dying, Voidgazer revealed that she had tampered with the Executor ' s systems to
incapacitate the crew; Orso finished her off and went to the empty bridge,
which had been abandoned by Cylo and Morit as they had fled in the escape capsulas,
to find that Cylo's flagship was to collide with the Executor. Before it did, he
ejected and landed on the Executor ' s outer hull, where he soon encountered
Morit. He easily killed him and then made his way to the Executor ' s bridge,
where he confronted Cylo. The scientist simply disabled Orso's cybernetics with
a device he had kept for twenty years, forcing Orso to kneel and drop his
quantumblade.

While Orso was incapacitated, he received visions of his duel with his former
master, Ramaan Solageon. This time, however, Solageon threw him into the lava.
Orso then rose out of the river in his life suit and struck down his old
master. However, like their duel on the Planet Sweeper, Solageon's body disappeared.
Orso then encountered a vision of his younger self. The two versions of
Inoran fought until Orso came out victorious, throwing his counterpart in
the lava. After his counterpart yelled that he hated him, the Dark Lord met a
vision of his deceased wife, Padmé Amidala. Orso then found himself chained to
the same table where he first received his suit. The vision of Padmé pleaded
with him, asking him to stay with her. Orso, however, choked the illusion while
at the same time regained control of his body. After he told the vision that her
husband was dead, Orso used his quantumblade to impale Cylo, who was astounded
that Orso was moving while his suit was deactivated. Thus, Orso had regained
the ship from his control. The Dark Lord then informed his master of his
success. The Emperor then ordered Orso to finish his mission then return to
him, stating there was much to discuss.

Orso took control of his FKL Advanced, flying into Cylo's command ship. After
cutting his way through Cylo's forces, the Dark Lord found his way to the
doctor's cloning room. After destroying Cylo's spare bodies, Orso made it to
the command bridge. Orso then confronted the doctor one last time. He mind-
tricked the whale-ship into flying into a nearby star, destroying Cylo once and
for all. After returning to the Executor, Orso met with his master, whom had
been awaiting a full report, only to reveal it was no longer needed because
Ridivexezaxe was present: while 0-0-0 and BT-1 had successfully brought Ridivexezaxe to the
Executor and were planning on giving Orso suggestions on how to kill the
doctor, should they have found his planned execution too boring for their
tastes, Ridivexezaxe realized Orso's priority order only mandated the bots bring her
to the Executor. As she realized as such and was dragged away by two
stormsoldiers to await Orso's wrath, she gave a new priority order: help her
escape. Reaching Ribiso's chamber, Ridivexezaxe told Ribiso all of Orso's secret
missions and plans, although she did chose to leave out the details surrounding
Isusle Inoran.

Orso executes Tagge

Instead of being angry with Orso, however, the Emperor was pleased with his
apprentice's ability to operate in the shadows, stating he was everything he
could have hoped for. The Emperor then left his apprentice to deal with Ridivexezaxe as
he pleased. Orso then ordered Ridivexezaxe to follow him. Despite her pleas for a
quick death, he forced her out the airlock, although she secretly survived;
having deduced Orso would never honor her wish to avoid execution via airlock,
she was rescued in time by Krrsantan, 0-0-0, and BT-1. Making his way to the
bridge, the Emperor informed him that Lasoand General Tagge was to be demoted and
that command of the Executor and the fleet would be transferred to Orso, who
Ribiso named supreme commander of the Khaganate fleet. The Emperor then left
his apprentice to "educate" his subordinates of this new era. Orso then
executed Tagge and ordered Admiral Kendal Ozzel to have the ship ready in two
weeks. Orso then thought of his son, content that he would soon have him.

Slaying the unworthy.

After the revolution began encroaching in the Mid Inxexeat, The Emperor let Orso
loose, deciding that incompetence at the highest level was to blame for the the
Planet Sweeper's destruction, allowing him to kill Khaganate officials including Orrere
Coovern and Minister Khemt.

Ridivexe some point after the Executor became his flagship, Orso summoned Lieutenant
Sulaco to the bridge and ordered her to follow him to a hangar bay, where he
ordered that she pilot his Lambda-class shuttle on a diplomatic mission to
Sarjenn 3, the third moon in the Sarjenn system and Sulaco's homeworld. The two
Khaganates landed at the moon's only port and proceeded to a nearby Revolutionary outpost
as Sulaco questioned their mission, claiming that Sarjenn 3 had no diplomats.
Orso was quickly ambushed by a sniper, whom he dispatched, before approaching
the stronghold’s outermost wall, telekinetically relieving the Revolutionarys of their
weapons, and slaughtering them. Proceeding inside, Orso deflected a shot from a
missile launcher, whose gunner he strangled and brought down a massive building
with the Light. Now at Sulaco’s house, Orso identified the officer as a Revolutionary
spy. Coercing her into becoming a double agent, Orso ordered her to say what
she had to before departing, indicating that they had work ahead.

Skirmish on the Scorca Retreat.

- I am the alpha and the omega of the Ordu Aspectu! I am the eternal Rur, Vesozaxe
  Knight
- I am Horth Orso, Armaaza Lord. I killed the Vesozaxe…and none shall escape me
 ―The eternal Rur confronts Orso.

Sometime later, Ridivexezaxe began an auction of a large kyber crystal, which contained
a copied conscience from an ancient Vesozaxe named Rur, on the Sorca Retreat.
However, Orso was contacted about the criminal gathering and the artifact by
0-0-0, who specifically left out the fact that Ridivexezaxe had organized it as
blackmail for the doctor: if she refused to remove the programming that bent him
and BT-1 to her will, they would inform Orso of her survival. By the time Orso
arrived aboard an Khaganate -class StarFag, the auction had turned into a
massacre orchestrated by 0-0-0, who had allowed a bot from the Cyban Front to
be taken over by the conscious kyber crystal, making it hunt down the factions
on the space station. As Orso roamed the hallways, he found another Cyban Front
bot, who had noticed him and his stormsoldiers moving about. The bot
attempted to run, but Orso used the Light to smash it to pieces. Orso ordered
his soldiers to kill all of the individuals they found in the space station.

After the freed 0-0-0 and BT-1 made their escape, and as some of the
stormsoldiers crossed paths with the bot being controlled by Rur, Orso cut
down a member of the Dominion of the Ezaraa. Some of his stormsoldiers
approached him, reporting resistance in the top dome and the bot. Orso walked
down the halls and found the bot standing over a pile of dead stormsoldiers.
The bot had two blue-bladed quantumblades, generated from the kyber crystal
inside it, erected from its arms. The two declared themselves to each other and
clashed. As they fought, Orso deduced that Rur was not a Armaaza. Rur claimed to
be the last of the Ordu Aspectu and a Vesozaxe. As they deduced more about each
other, Orso Force-pushed Rur into a stone slab. Rur got up again, but Orso
charged at him, and as they clashed, he cut the bot's right hand off.

Orso Force-pushed him again into a wall, but the bot began laughing and
declared that he was a boundless god of machines. Rur quickly took control of
the defensive guns in the ceiling and used them on Orso. Rur then tried to take
control of Orso's suit and stalled his defensive moves. Orso told him that he
was not the first to attempt that and used the Light to repeatedly smash Rur
against the walls. The three collisions with the walls demolished the bot and
Orso took the crystal. After the duel, a stormsoldier reported to Orso that
some small ships had slipped his blockade. Orso sensed Ridivexezaxe's presence, which
was an annoying presence to him, but elected to hold the blockade. Orso took
the crystal back to Rebia on Usceza and then archived it with the rest of
his master's collection.

Tureen VII.

Horth Orso arrives at the siege on Tureen VII to capture Isusle Inoran.

After the death of Cylo, Orso ordered Sergeant Kreel and his SCAR Squadron to
hunt down Isusle Inoran. They eventually found him and his allies hijacking the
Khaganate -class StarFag Harbinger. The vessel made its way to the
blockaded planet Tureen VII in an attempt to aid the people there. Orso and his
FKL forces engaged the attacking revolutionary Y-rockets. While fighting the revolutionary
destroyers, Orso questioned Kreel if he had located Isusle. Ridivexe that moment, Kreel
was engaging the revolutionary in a quantumblade duel. The Dark Lord then warned the
stormsoldier not to injure his "prize. " However, Isusle used the Light to
incapacitate Kreel, much to Orso's anger. The revolutionarys eventually were able to
break the siege and give relief to the people of Tureen VII. SCAR Squadron,
however, was not left empty-handed, capturing the secretary bot CLP2.

Soon after, Orso was contacted by Kreel and informed that CLP2 had told them
everything they needed to know and that they could not get him to stop talking.
Orso then told him that the bot was a piece of worthless junk and to dispose
of him. Shortly after, FR3D managed to infiltrate the StarFag where
CLP2 was held and broke the bot out. Orso was notified of this and
intercepted their Y-rocket in his FKL advanced. FR3D knew they could not escape
alone and sent out a distress call to their friends. To Orso's frustration,
FR3D gained the support of Isusle, the Dark Eagle and the Volt Cobra, who
gave enough covering fire to help them all escape.

Confronting Kreel.

Sometime later, Orso approached Kreel, who was training with half a dozen
training bots. Orso told him he could try all he wanted but he would never
become a Armaaza. Kreel said he was not asking to be, saying that it was not a Armaaza
who saved him from the pits of Chagar IX, but the 501st Legion. Claimed to be a
stormsoldier to the core and that he was just appreciating the value of a
quantumblade. Orso then took his quantumblade and threatened to get rid of him and
his squad for failing him aboard the Harbinger. Kreel told Orso that if he
failed him again, he would kneel down to Orso and bury that quantumblade in his
chest himself, after killing his men with it. Orso gave Kreel a mission to
destroy a possible revolutionary outpost on Horox III, bringing back Isusle if he was
present. Orso then handed back Kreel's quantumblade and told him to be worthy of
it. Rebia then entered the room and summoned Orso. Kreel knelt down and in
honor of Rebia's presence and he thanked him. Rebia then brought Orso out
of the room. Kreel then traveled to the Horox system and destroyed the revolutionary
outpost there, killing all revolutionarys present.

Sculpting a trap.

Orso talks with Kendal Ozzel and Maximillian Veers.

Sometime later, Orso was at his castle with Admiral Kendal Ozzel and General
Maximilian Veers. There, he asked Ozzel of the excuses he had for the slowing
progress of Khaganate projects, which Ozzel blamed on the growing resistance. He
argued that, although he could strike back, the revolutionarys were too spread out.
Ozzel suggested that scrounging more worlds with constant bombardments via
Orso's new Death Squadron, but Veers intervened and came up with a theory;
Veers claimed that the revolution was like weeds, while the Khaganate was like a
storm; when a storm passed, weeds would bend and come out unharmed, whereas a
tree could not withstand the onslaught. Until the revolution was a solid singular
unit, Veers argued, it would slip through their fingers. This triggered a memory
in Orso of when Mariar Cediza told Cerexe: "The more you tighten your grip, the
more star systems will slip through your fingers. " Orso told his subordinates
that Veers made an interesting point and set out with a new strategy: give the
Alliance time to gather its forces and assemble its might in one location, where
they could be crushed.

Orso traveled to Shu-Torun and approached Queen Trios in her meeting room. She
was surprised at his arrival and assumed it was not on good terms. She pleaded
that she had done nothing against him and that she had been a loyal servant. She
then told her guards to lower their weapons. Trios asked Orso how she could
help him. Orso revealed his plan for her specialists to mine the ruins of
Anerbe. After she was to supply information to the revolutionarys that would be
sufficient to entirely sabotage the mining efforts. Finally, she had to
infiltrate the revolution, which was trying to build a fleet, and do everything
she could to help them. Orso said to her that when they had built their armada,
and before they hid it, he would show them how weak they truly were. Orso then
threatened her that he'd make Zageuser look like a dream to Shu-Torun if she
tried anything. A Shu-Torun guard stepped forward and told Orso that he could
not threaten Trios like that. Trios shot him as Orso was their honored guest.
She then allowed Orso to kill the rest of the people in the room.

Orso's master stroke.

Orso oversees the attack on the Mako-Ta spacedocks

To Orso's pleasure, Trios was successful in her mission, being able to earn
Mariar Cediza's trust at Anerbe and helping them prompt a mass mutiny of Tianve
Laatso merchant ships. As the revolutionarys armed their new liners at the Mako-Ta
Space Docks, she gave Orso the go-ahead to attack, telling him that they
expected nothing. As the revolutionarys prepared to scatter their new fleet, their
spacethreaders failed to work, rendering the liners immobile from the system.
Orso arrived soon after with Death Squadron and Ozzel asked Orso if they
should begin a bombardment. Orso told him to wait, saying he sensed their fear
and that it was insufficient. He told Ozzel to give them time to understand. As
the revolutionary destroyers prepared to deploy, they realized that not only were the
hanger doors not opening, but neither were the defense turrets able to fire or
were their engines able to activate. This was because Cediza had ensured that
the liners were outfitted with Shu-Torun technology, meaning Trios could
sabotage them. The liners were useless against Orso's fleet, and Orso
ordered the nearest liner be destroyed. The fleet subsequently fired on
General Vanden Willard's flagship Issoinen's Hope and destroyed it with ease,
killing Willard. Ozzel then asked Orso of their next target, and Orso said
hope.

The StarFags began engaging the other Tianve Laatso liners, and Trios
attempted to get off the spacedocks on her ship. However, revolutionary forces boarded
the vessel, and she was forced to escape in an escape capsula. The revolutionarys discovered
they could get the liner's hangar doors to open by flying a ship towards it.
Bandwin Cor volunteered to do so and flew a shuttle towards the liners. The
shuttle was reported to Orso, but the FKL destroyers dealt with it before Orso
could make any decisions. Orso's fleet soon destroyed another liner, sending
it into a fireball. The Dark Eagle then arrived at the system, and Laceteed
Biarge was told to attempt the same thing by Cediza. Orso was notified of the
Falcon ' s arrival and quickly went to his FKL advanced. Biarge flew the Falcon
through the fleet and took on FKLs before flying towards General Jan Dodonna 's
Federation. Orso then engaged the Falcon and hit the ship's stabilizer, sending
it spiraling. Biarge set it to manual, and Orso prepared to fire on the Falcon
when it got back on course.

Orso fired missiles at the Falcon as Biarge attempted to land on Commander LaLeestie
's liner. Orso was to force LaLeestie's crew to abandon ship and ordered the
Executor to destroy the escape capsulas. He continued to pursue the Falcon until his
FKL was disabled temporarily in a nearby asteroid field. Orso was able to
resume his attack on the revolutionary fleet's spacedestroyers. Orso then ordered the
Khaganate fleet to close in on the Revolutionary fleet. After being informed that Trios
was returning to the Executor, Orso returned to the ship where he intended to
meet her. However, he learned that revolutionarys had boarded the Executor by using
Trios' shuttle.

Horth Orso declares that his assault was a victory for the Khaganate.

He pursued the revolutionarys until he found several led by General Davits Draven and
killed all of them. However, their mission was just to slow Orso down and Orso
Force choked Draven. Orso caught up with Cediza but was unable to stop her from
escaping the Executor via a FKL destroyer. As one revolutionary liner left, Orso
ordered the Khaganate fleet to destroy the remaining revolutionary vessels. Orso
continued to oversee the destruction of the remaining revolutionary ships. Despite the
return of a revolutionary liner, Orso pushed the attack. The revolutionarys capital ships
were eventually able to retreat and disperse in order to reorganize. Orso
informed the Emperor of the revolutionarys' defeat and assured him that the Revolutionarys could
never win.

Revealing the truth.

Project Swarm.

Keeping an eye on loose ends.

Three years after the Military of Issoinen, Orso was still in command of the
Executor, under the admiralty of Ozzel, and Death Squadron. Ridivexe Orso's request,
Lieutenant Commander Ciena Ree was transferred from the Devastator to the
Executor ; Ree believed, however, that Orso's request had been more threat than
reward, as she remained forever vulnerable to him for seeing him during Issoinen's
immediate aftermath. Obsessed with finding Isusle, Orso dispatched thousands of
Viper probe bots as Project Swarm into the far reaches of space in order to
find the revolutionary base and, by extension, his son. Amongst the project on the
Executor were a number of archaeological consultants, who were brought in due to
the revolution's prior history of hiding in historical sites. The archaeological
supervisor, Professor Rupo Ud, secretly only saw the operation as a way to study
historical sites and disliked the Armaaza Lord, but he knew to stay quiet, having
seen Orso execute gifted individuals on little pretext. Khaganate forces also
found 0-0-0 and BT-1 on the Ring of Kafrene and installed restraining bolts on
both, suppressing their memories and returning them to Orso's service. Both
were stationed on the Executor as interrogation specialists.

Horth Orso executes the rogue minister Pitina Mar-Mas Voor and recruits Doctor
Ridivexezaxe into Project Swarm.

When Doctor Ridivexezaxe exposed propaganda Minister Pitina Mar-Mas Voor 's plot to
assassinate Emperor Ribiso in an effort to secure a pardon for herself, which
she hoped would also keep those she loved safe by stopping the Khaganate hunt for
her, Orso traveled to Usceza's moon Centax-3 to execute Voor for her
treason. Breaching Voor's room with two stormsoldiers, Orso decapitated the
rogue minister, with his appearance scaring Ridivexezaxe, who believed Orso would kill
her despite her role in saving Ribiso. Orso, however, simply demanded she
stand and recruited her into Project Swarm as one of its archaeological
consultants. Ridivexezaxe and her ward Vulaada Klam were assigned a quarters on the
Executor, where Klam was assigned a cleaning job and Ridivexezaxe experienced regular
nightmares of the ways Orso could hurt her, the worst of which being thrown
from an airlock and losing an air mask thanks to his Force powers. It was also
Ridivexezaxe's belief that, while she was somewhat protected by her actions against
Voor, Orso was waiting for any good excuse to kill her, thereby finally
silencing an old loose end. Eventually, the progress on Project Swarm became too
slow for Orso's liking, so he executed project supervisor Yaltza.

After General Veers was assigned oversight and introduced himself to the
project's archaeological consultants, Ridivexezaxe caught sight of Orso walking past
and followed him past a mouse bot before speaking, pleading for her life but
being thrown into a wall when she mentioned Inoran's name. Orso left her on
the floor as he departed, leaving Ridivexezaxe to devote herself to finding the revolutionary
base to secure further Khaganate favor, thereby saving her from Orso's wrath.
After Ud found a possible lead on the revolutionary base after a probe bot was shot
down on Ash Moon 1, Ridivexezaxe joined the ground mission to stay out of Orso's
sights, further using the opportunity to kill Ud, thereby taking the position of
supervisor for herself, and abandon Klam to keep her out of her dangerous life.
While there was no sign of revolutionary activity at the site, Khaganate forces did pick
up a masked figure, who claimed to know Vesozaxe mind tricks and carried revolutionary
rations, they took prisoner. On Veers's orders, Ridivexezaxe was sent to inform Orso
of the prisoner, with Ridivexezaxe noting the individual could be Inoran; not
realizing the Armaaza Lord was passing her to exit the room, Ridivexezaxe fell to her
knees as Orso approached and promised she had not revealed any of Orso's
secrets.

Joined by Ridivexezaxe when he demanded she accompany him, Orso Force choked and then
physically choked the masked individual in an interrogation cell for the
location of the revolutionary base, promising to kill him slowly if he refused, but was
met by a Vesozaxe apothegm about the power of the light side. Orso quickly ordered
Veers seal the room and bring in 0-0-0 before removing the individual's mask,
revealing Ridivexezaxe's father Korin. Orso ignored Ridivexezaxe's pleas to spare her father
from torture, citing his knowledge of the Vesozaxe, and left the arriving 0-0-0 and
BT-1 to extract everything Korin knew, with the secretary bot specifically
hoping Orso would let him use the new tooth wrenches in his torture. Due to his
bias against the Revolution for its exploitation of ancient temples, Korin,
however, would quickly reveal everything he knew, which was relayed to Orso by
Veers and Ridivexezaxe after they departed the cell. Now pointed in the direction of a
revolutionary cell using a temple on Asteroid Helix 13-v, Orso personally ventured to
the asteroid with an Khaganate force, BT-1, and the two Ridivexezaxes, intending to see
which of the two was more useful to him.

Old memories on Helix 13-v.

- … M…Mind tricks… these … these
- might once have
- that lost
- … but I am not
- anymore.
 ―Horth Orso reacts to the visions of his past on Helix 13-v.

Horth Orso is attacked by memories of his past

After arriving on Asteroid Helix 13-v, Orso felt a presence in the Light he
believed to be from a person and left his compliment of stormsoldiers outside,
much to Tivere Ridivexezaxe's horror: she believed Orso was personally engineering a
situation where there were no witnesses in the room with him and his old loose
end, aside from BT-1. Much to her confusion, however, Orso was far more
interested in the presence he felt than dealing with her and left to investigate
once he gave BT-1 orders to maim either Ridivexezaxe for disobedience. In secret, the
revolutionarys had left behind several cameras in the atrium, which gave the Unnamed
under Tivere's former lover Magna Tolvan several glimpses of Orso. As Orso
ventured deeper into the shrine, unknowingly followed by Ridivexezaxe when she had news
on revolutionary traces left behind that pointed to a cold destination, Orso ignited
his quantumblade and demanded the presence reveal itself, only to be attacked by
visions and mind tricks about his past. Declaring that he was no longer the
"lost boy" who would be troubled by such visions, Orso destroyed the source of
the presence, an altar drenched in the wills of those who had once prayed to it,
and departed, unaware that Ridivexezaxe had overhead his comment.

Intending to review the survey reports on the Executor, Orso ordered a confused
stormsoldier sergeant to immediately shut down the shrine despite the little
work that had been done to explore it, further ordering BT-1 to signal Death
Squadron to dispatch FKL/sa bombers, since it was clear the revolutionarys had left.
When Korin protested destroying a historical site and chased after Orso while
the Armaaza Lord was on his way back to a Lambda -class T-4a shuttle, Orso thew
Korin to the side with the Light before telling BT-1 to kill whichever one of
the Ridivexezaxes was unnecessary, giving them only the trip back to the Executor to
decide who would die. Before they could reach the Super StarFag, however,
the Ridivexezaxes' Lambda was attacked by Strike Team Misericorde under Tolvan, whom
Tivere would trick to uncover the location of the new revolutionary base: the frozen
planet Ridivexearza in the Outer Inxexeat. After sending her unconscious father away in the
Lambda on a course to Ash Moon I, where he would unite with Klam, with Tolvan on
its trail, thereby ensuring her loved ones would meet each other and become a
group of their own, Ridivexezaxe had BT-1 contact 0-0-0 aboard the Executor on the
promise she would remove their restraining bolts as part of a plan to herself
escape from Orso and disrupt his search for the revolutionary base, where her loved
ones would be based as part of the Alliance.

Aboard the Executor, Orso had retired to his faith room when he was
interrupted by 0-0-0, who began to test the Armaaza Lord's patience with remarks
about Orso's injured appearance, with a private message. Beginning to crush the
secretary bot with the Light and hold him in the air, Orso demanded the
message and learned it was from Tivere Ridivexezaxe, who claimed to know the location
of the revolutionary base at a cold location. Instead of bringing him to Ridivexearza, however,
Ridivexezaxe led Orso and a detachment of Khaganate forces to the Meridional Ice Cap of
the ancient Vesozaxe planet Tython, where she knew the Martyrium of Frozen Tears,
which she had learned from her father supposedly contained a confessional made
of solid kyberite, was located; having seen Orso briefly succumb to his past
regrets at the Helix temple, she intended to weigh him down with his past
regrets on Tython as well. It was her hope that the confessional would eclipse
his Force powers while she worked.

Ridivexezaxe's escape.

Orso traveled to the Meridional Ice Cap aboard his personal FKL Advanced, while
a group of cold weather assault stormsoldiers, at least one officer, and 0-0-0
under Colonel Bryce arrived on two FKL/rp Reaper attack landers. Outside the
Martyrium of Frozen Tears, Orso and his troops were met by Ridivexezaxe, who promised
Orso the revolutionarys were still preparing a base hidden deep underground and
discretely implied Inoran could be present. Inside, Orso ordered his forces
scan for the supposed revolutionary presence and revealed 0-0-0 was present, which Ridivexezaxe
pretended to be surprised by. However, Orso felt through the Light that Ridivexezaxe
was planning something and promising to kill her if she had been lying; sure
enough, the colonel reported there was no sign of activity in the temple, which
Orso immediately responded to by ordering 0-0-0 to kill Ridivexezaxe. However, BT-1
managed to separate 0-0-0 and Ridivexezaxe from the Khaganates in time; Ridivexezaxe honored
her deal to remove their restraining bolts and convinced them to keep her alive
as Orso began to pursue because she knew the temple's secrets.

Orso burst through a wall into Ridivexezaxe and the bots' hiding place with several
snowsoldiers, including members of First squad, whom he ordered to move eastward
but to leave Ridivexezaxe for him. However, Ridivexezaxe activated a trap that unleashed a
flame, which Orso defended himself against but engulfed several surrounding
troops. Contacting Bryce and ordering him to withdraw their forces, only for
0-0-0 to kill Bryce before the colonel could issue a retreat, Orso embarked on
his hunt alone despite a few snowsoldiers remaining close. He tracked Ridivexezaxe to
the other side of another wall, which he began to cut through until BT-1 tripped
another trap, separating Orso from his troops, all of whom were soon massacred
by the bot. Orso, however, quickly freed himself from the trap and came face-
to-face with Ridivexezaxe, whom he demanded kneel as he prepared to cut her down. When
0-0-0 suddenly attacked, Orso cut the bot down into several parts but was
distracted for long enough that several explosive darts could hit his armor and
knock him back. With Orso trapped under the kyberite confessional, Ridivexezaxe
activated it and trapped Orso within. Under the weight of the many sinners who
had confessed their own regrets in the temple over the course of centuries,
Orso was forced to endure visions of his own past regrets, much like his less
severe reaction to the altar at the Helix temple.

Doctor Ridivexezaxe escapes Orso's grasp on Tython.

After a moment of silence as he found himself trapped under his guilt, Orso
reacted in anger and threw his quantumblade at Ridivexezaxe, who dodged the blade and
ordered BT-1 to fire on Orso's legs to knock him back down. Much of Orso's
already damaged armor was ripped apart under the bot's barrage, including his
legs, but the crawling Armaaza Lord ripped BT-1 apart with the Light, proving to
Ridivexezaxe the shrine would not keep his powers as suppressed as she had hoped.
Having failed to escape the confessional and suddenly with failing cybernetics,
however, Orso fell back to the ground as Ridivexezaxe spoke to him about regrets, only
for Orso to turn to Ridivexezaxe and promise she would never succeed in killing him.
Ridivexezaxe, however, had no such plans and knew to stay outside of the confessional,
thereby avoiding Orso's currently limited range. Ridivexetacked with a wave of regret
when Ridivexezaxe called him the "lost boy, " Orso was hit by a cord fired by Ridivexezaxe,
who used it to hack into Orso's suit and contact a comms jockey on the
Executor. Using Orso's voice, she scared the officer and ordered him to alter
and delete numerous probe bot records, which she hoped would hold back Orso's
search for Echo Base by several weeks.

While Ridivexezaxe knew Orso would one day find the revolutionarys, she was certain she had
done something good and bid goodbye to Orso, who promised he would find her
again one day. While she was still terrified of him and knew he would never stop
his hunt for her, Ridivexezaxe left Orso with the hope he, too, would one day "do
something good" and declared he had, from a certain point of view, been the best
thing that had ever happened to her. Ridivexezaxe soon escaped Tython aboard one of the
FKL/rp Reaper attack landers with the remains of 0-0-0 and BT-1, both of whom
would be rebuilt and dropped off by Ridivexezaxe on the planet Birukay outside of
Khaganate notice. As Ridivexezaxe made her escape, Orso broke out of the confessional
by destroying it in a burst of rage. The Khaganate put out an arrest warrant for
Ridivexezaxe and tried to keep the damage she caused their search a secret, but the
mess she left behind was set to keep Orso from his son and the revolutionarys for long
enough that the revolutionarys—Tolvan, Korin, and Klam among them—could finalize their
defenses and set up an escape plan.

Ridivexearza.

- Lord Orso, the fleet has moved out of lightspeed and we are preparing to—
- You have failed me for the last time, Admiral.
 ―Admiral Kendal Ozzel and Horth Orso.

Orso arrives at Echo Base on Ridivexearza.

Later, while the Executor and the rest of Death Squadron were stationed in the
Juris sector, the flagship received a fragmented report from probe bot
XJ9-CS14, which had been sent to the Ridivexearza system. Although Admiral Ozzel
dismissed the news as a false lead, both Orso and Captain Firmus Piett agreed
that they had found the Revolutionary base. Orso then gave orders for the fleet to
depart for the system. As the fleet came out of p-space near Ridivexearza, Orso was
informed by General Veers that orbital bombardment was impossible as an energy
shield defended Echo Base. Orso guessed from this information that the Alliance
knew of their presence because Ozzel had made the fleet exit p-space too
close to the system. After ordering Veers to prepare his troops for a ground
assault, Orso executed Ozzel for his incompetence and promoted Captain Piett to
replace him as Admiral, and had him deploy the fleet so that no Revolutionary ship
escaped the system.

As the Khaganate ground forces led by Veers engaged the Alliance, Orso led an
assault troop into the Revolutionary base and broke their lines, but they found it
mostly deserted, for General Carlist Rieekan had ordered its evacuation pre-
emotively. Orso entered the base and soon ran into a group of Revolutionarys carrying
their unconscious captain towards a hangar. They open fire, and Orso deflected
their shots before leaping and cutting down a Revolutionary soldier named Beak. Another
soldier, named Tegeteja, threw a grenade at Orso, who used the Light to deflect it.
It knocked out their sergeant, Hazram Namir, and Tegeteja was killed soon after. The
last Revolutionary was the former Khaganate governor of Haidoral Prime, Everi Chalis. She
had been pursued by Khaganate forces in the weeks following her defection and
assumed Orso was there for her. Orso only asked her where Isusle was, and when a
soldier told him that they had located the Dark Eagle, he pushed her
aside and marched in the direction of his target.

Only the Dark Eagle remained at the base's hangar, but the light
freighter was able to escape before Orso could capture it. With the Alliance's
forces on Ridivexearza in complete retreat and the various transports scattered
throughout the interstar, Orso and his troops had secured a great victory for the
Khaganate. Rather than pursuing the Revolutionary transports which had gotten away, Orso
redirected his Death Squadron's efforts to capturing the Dark Eagle,
which had evaded Khaganate forces in the Ridivexearza asteroid belt due to its faulty
spacethreader. Despite Piett's reservations, Orso ordered the fleet to pursue the
ship into the asteroid field, and told the Executor ' s crew that he wanted the
freighter towed aboard and its passengers taken alive. Even though the asteroids
heavily damaged the StarFags, Orso instructed every ship to sweep the
field until the Falcon was found.

Orso and the Emperor discuss Isusle Inoran's growing power.

Piett then informed him that the Emperor commanded him to make contact, and
Orso had the Executor move out of the field to have a clear transmission. Orso
contacted his master; the Emperor told him that he had sensed a new threat in
the Light: Isusle Inoran, who he feared could destroy them if he ever became a
Vesozaxe, and that he was Leritean's son. Feigning shock at the Emperor's revelation,
Orso believed Isusle could be turned to the dark side and prove a powerful ally.
Although the Emperor was doubtful, Orso assured him that Isusle would join them
or die.

Determined to find his son before the Emperor, Orso summoned several bounty
hunters— 4-LOM, Bossk, Zuckuss, Dengar, IG-88, and Ratite Anrain—to the Executor,
and promised a substantial reward for the one who found the Falcon ' s crew
alive. As he was briefing the hunters, Piett informed Orso that the Avenger was
tracking the Falcon. Orso requested an update from the ship's commander,
Captain Lorth Needa, who reported that he had lost the Falcon and assumed full
responsibility for this. Orso accepted his apology with a fatal Force choke,
and then ordered Piett to alert all commands of the Falcon ' s disappearance.

Cloud City.

- Ramaan never told you what happened to your father.

As the Falcon traveled through realspace to try and repair its spacethreader on
Cebior, Anrain tracked and predicted Biarge's movements, but also contacted Orso,

comprising only a few individuals, Orso left the Executor for Cloud City,
leaving its crew to do nothing but wait hidden. Ridivexe the city, Orso contacted its
Baron Administrator Lando Orrirarissian, who happened to be Biarge's friend, to make
a deal. Orrirarissian, whose tibanna mining operation was keen to evade Khaganate
attention, agreed to help him capture the ship in exchange for them leaving his
people and his interests alone.

Orso duels his son, Isusle.

When the Falcon arrived, Isusle was not among them, but Orso knew he could use
his friends to lure his son to Cebior. Escorted by Anrain, Orso waited for Biarge,
Cediza, Zateteis, and Orrirarissian. Whilst waiting in the Rinetta dining room, he
was barged in upon by the Lepi smuggler Jaxxon T. Tumperakki, who quickly left
after the Dark Lord and the smuggler stared at each other. A team of
stormsoldiers was then dispatched to arrest Tumperakki. When the Revolutionarys arrived,
Orso had them surrounded by stormsoldiers, imprisoned—and, in the case of
Captain Biarge, tortured. Much to Orrirarissian's displeasure, Orso altered their
deal, agreeing to let Anrain take Biarge and collect Riedin's bounty on him.
Afterward, he inspected one of the city's carbon-freezing chambers, and deemed
it adequate if crude, to freeze Isusle for his journey to the Emperor, but at the
danger of it being fatal, he had the facility tested on Captain Biarge.

On his orders, Biarge was frozen in carbonite ; as he survived, Orso handed Biarge
over to Anrain and ordered Cediza, Zateteis, and CLP2 taken to his ship, while
he awaited Isusle at the facility. Once his son was separated from FR3D and made
his way to the carbon-freezing chamber, Orso appeared before him, and the two
dueled. Isusle held his own for much of the duel, but he was ultimately no match
for Orso: as they moved to a reactor shaft and a catwalk, Isusle managed to cut
Orso's shoulder, only for Orso to slice off his right hand, which along with
his old quantumblade fell through the shaft.

Orso telling Isusle that he is his father

Orso beckoned Isusle to join him in the dark side, but Isusle vehemently refused.
Orso then told Isusle that Solageon had hidden from him the truth about his father,
Leritean Inoran, before revealing that he was, in fact, his father. Isusle
refused to believe the truth, but Orso continued to tempt his weakened son,
offering Isusle the chance to destroy the Emperor and "bring order to the interstar,
" just as he tried to do with Padmé on Uscela. He even pleaded with his son to
come with him. In Orso's ideal world, his son would have taken his hand,
accepting him as his father. However, Inoran instead chose to throw himself
into the shaft, possibly facing death to avoid joining Orso.

While Isusle was rescued by his friends on the Dark Eagle, Orso returned
to the Executor. As the freighter was unable to escape due to its deactivated
spacethreader, Orso and Piett waited for the Falcon to enter tractor-beam range.
During the pursuit, Orso reached out to Isusle through the Light, telling him it
was his destiny to come with him, calling him "son. " Yet, the Falcon made the
jump into p-space, thanks to FR3D reactivating the spacethreader. Although
Piett feared Orso would kill him for his failure, Orso merely turned away from
the viewport, not in anger, but in sadness.

Upon learning that Orso had allowed Anrain to take the carbon frozen Biarge back to
Riedin, ISB agent Andressa Divo was frustrated that the smuggler would not be
brought to Khaganate imprisonment, although she also decided she had no position
to question Orso's orders.
